Conclusion: ResCom以轻量低秩残差校正有效弥补激活舍入误差，在精度、实现开销与内存收益间取得良好平衡，且具备跨模型规模与数据集的适用性；其代价主要是较小推理时间开销，需在精度与紧凑存储两种实现间权衡。

Abstract: Low-bit weight--activation quantization reduces the memory and computation needed to deploy large language models, but activation rounding can still degrade accuracy after calibration. Reconstruction from rounded inputs cannot distinguish activations within the same quantization bin. We propose ResCom, a low-rank correction that uses the activation rounding residual to compensate for the resulting layer-output error. An output-aware calibration procedure selects residual directions by their contribution to output reconstruction and computes the correction in closed form, without gradient training or updates to the calibrated weights. On Llama-3-8B with 4-bit weights and activations, rank-32 attention corrections reduce WikiText-2 perplexity from 7.708 to 7.147, improve MMLU by 2.89 percentage points, and raise five-shot GSM8K accuracy from 23.96 to 36.16 percent. Evaluations on models from 1B to 70B parameters and on held-out C4 test the method beyond this setting. The evaluated FP32 implementation adds 2.25 percent to 4K prefill time; a separate compact-storage implementation retains a 27.7 percent peak-memory reduction relative to FP16.

</details>


### [65] [Distributed LLM Inference via Communication-Efficient Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35957)
*Joohyun Shin,Byeonghyeon Park,Keumseo Ryum,Joonhyuk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DISCO框架，通过服务器端部署草稿SLM副本，消除分布式投机解码中词表级分布传输开销，仅交换草稿token索引与标量概率，在带宽受限下取得更高解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式投机解码中精确拒绝采样需在设备与服务器间传输覆盖整个LLM词表的完整token分布，带宽受限时通信开销严重制约解码加速效果。

Method: 在服务器侧引入草稿小模型（SLM）的副本，使服务器本地即可获得拒绝采样所需的草稿侧分布，从而避免传输词表规模的分布，仅交换草稿token索引与标量概率值，同时保持目标分布的精确性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，DISCO相比现有分布式投机解码方法持续获得更高的解码加速，尤其在严格带宽约束下优势更显著。

Conclusion: 通过在服务器端复制草稿SLM，可在不牺牲精确目标分布的前提下显著降低通信开销，是一种高效的分布式投机解码通信优化方案。

Abstract: Distributed speculative decoding has emerged as a promising approach for device--server collaborative large language model (LLM) inference, but its decoding speedup is often limited by the vocabulary-scale communication overhead. In existing distributed speculative decoding methods, exact rejection sampling requires transmitting full token distributions over the LLM vocabulary between the device and the server, which becomes costly in bandwidth-constrained environments. To address this issue, we propose DISCO, a communication-efficient framework for distributed speculative decoding, that eliminates vocabulary-scale distribution transfer while preserving the exact target distribution. DISCO achieves this by introducing a server-side replica of the draft small language model (SLM), which enables the server to locally obtain the draft-side distribution required for rejection sampling. As a result, DISCO exchanges only drafted token indices and scalar probability values between the device and the server. Experimental results on multiple datasets show that DISCO consistently achieves higher decoding speedup than existing distributed speculative decoding methods, particularly under stringent bandwidth constraints.

</details>


### [66] [Exponential Wake-Up Overshoot in Explicit Gaussian Point-Process Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35958)
*Hanwu Qin,Dianhai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文刻画了长时间静默后事件恢复时高斯点过程滤波器首个更新的"指数放大"现象，给出其精确分布与可计算的越界指数，并对比显式滤波器与隐式后验众数滤波器的静默二阶展开与损失行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件序列常出现长静默期，恢复瞬间的显式高斯点过程滤波器会把首个更新按静默时长指数放大，可能导致预测速率严重偏离真实速率，但此前缺乏对该更新的精确分布刻画与何时越界的可判定指标。

Method: 推导静默后首个更新的精确分布，构造可计算的"越界指数"（crossing index）；对显式（单次打分步）滤波器及其隐式后验众数对应体，给出状态方差的静默二阶展开（对数修正符号相反）；以一个连续时间反例说明无法得到对bin宽度与静默时长一致有界的结论；并在73个越界单元与交易所成交数据上做实证验证，同时考察方差上限（variance cap）的影响。

Result: 越界指数能区分放大指数达到1的bin宽度与真实速率被超越的更大bin宽度；固定bin宽度下隐式滤波器的期望损失随静默时长对数增长，而连续时间反例排除了对bin宽度与静默时长一致有界的可能性；实验在全部73个越界单元及交易所成交数据上均验证了理论预测，方差上限可减轻显式过冲但主要损害隐式滤波器。

Conclusion: 长时间静默导致的更新放大是高斯点过程滤波的结构性问题，可用精确分布与越界指数提前预警；显式与隐式滤波器在静默下的表现存在本质差异，方差上限并非普适补救，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: When events resume after a long silence, explicit Gaussian point-process filters amplify the first update exponentially in the silence duration. We give the exact distribution of this update and a computable index that predicts when it overshoots. For the explicit (single-scoring-step) filter and its implicit posterior-mode counterpart, we derive second-order silence expansions of the state variance with opposite-sign logarithmic corrections. The crossing index determines when the post-wake prediction exceeds the resumed rate, separating the bin width at which the amplification exponent reaches one from the larger width at which the true rate is overshot. At fixed bin width, the implicit filter's expected loss grows logarithmically with silence duration, whereas a continuous-time counterexample rules out a bound uniform in bin width and silence duration. Experimental evaluation confirms these predictions in all 73 crossing cells and on exchange trade data, while variance caps reduce explicit overshoot but mostly hurt the implicit filter.

</details>


### [67] [From Slices to Volumes: Comparing 2D, 2.5D, and 3D Residual Networks for Pulmonary Nodule Malignancy-Risk Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35960)
*Bahodir Nazarov,Zineb Gharib,Fatima Zazoul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在LIDC-IDRI队列上系统比较2D、2.5D与3D残差网络对肺结节恶性风险分类的性能，并考察动量对比预训练、轻度数据增强及剔除不确定标签的影响；结论为轻度增强的2.5D模型表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的：比较2D、2.5D与3D残差网络在肺结节恶性风险分类上的表现，并评估动量对比预训练、轻度数据增强以及剔除"不确定"评级三类策略的作用。

Method: 回溯性构建LIDC-IDRI队列，依据放射科医师评级中位数将2,654个结节标为良性、不确定或恶性；按患者不交叉划分训练集1,876、验证集382、测试集396。输入表示分为单张轴位切片、5张相邻切片与局部CT体积三种。每条实验轨道均评估随机初始化基线、动量对比预训练后微调、以及轻度数据增强的监督训练；以验证集Macro-F1选择检查点。2D实验使用3个随机种子，2.5D与3D为单次运行估计；另做剔除不确定结节的二分类实验。

Result: 增强模型的测试Macro-F1为2D 0.6134±0.0075、2.5D 0.6537、3D 0.5403（2D的波动为跨种子总体标准差），对应准确率分别为0.6128、0.6869、0.5455。数据增强在所有轨道中均提升Macro-F1，而所测试的预训练方案则使其下降。二分类Macro-F1分别为0.8227、0.8664、0.7554。

Conclusion: 轻度增强的2.5D模型取得了观察到的最高性能；但预处理、优化设置、模型容量及重复实验次数的差异限制了对"维度"贡献的归因。标签反映放射科医师评估的恶性风险而非病理确诊癌症。代码与实验结果开源于 https://github.com/CT-DeepLearning-Team/CT-2D-2.5D-3D-DeepLearning。

Abstract: Purpose: To compare 2D, 2.5D, and 3D residual networks for pulmonary nodule malignancy-risk classification and evaluate momentum-contrast pretraining, mild augmentation, and exclusion of indeterminate ratings.
  Methods: A retrospectively prepared LIDC-IDRI cohort contained 2,654 nodules labeled benign, indeterminate, or malignant using median radiologist ratings. Patient-disjoint splits comprised 1,876 training, 382 validation, and 396 test nodules. Representations included one axial slice, five neighboring slices, and a local CT volume. Each track evaluated a randomly initialized baseline, momentum-contrast pretraining followed by fine-tuning, and supervised training with mild augmentation. Checkpoints were selected by validation Macro-F1. The 2D experiments used three seeds; 2.5D and 3D results were single-run estimates. A separate binary experiment excluded indeterminate nodules.
  Results: Augmented models achieved test Macro-F1 values of $0.6134\pm0.0075$ for 2D, $0.6537$ for 2.5D, and $0.5403$ for 3D; the 2D uncertainty denotes population standard deviation across seeds. Corresponding accuracies were $0.6128$, $0.6869$, and $0.5455$. Augmentation improved Macro-F1 within every track, whereas the tested pretraining recipes reduced it. Binary Macro-F1 values were $0.8227$, $0.8664$, and $0.7554$, respectively.
  Conclusion: Augmented 2.5D achieved the highest observed performance. Differences in preprocessing, optimization, capacity, and replication limit attribution to dimensionality. Labels reflect radiologist-assessed malignancy risk rather than pathology-confirmed cancer. Code and experimental results are available at https://github.com/CT-DeepLearning-Team/CT-2D-2.5D-3D-DeepLearning.

</details>


### [68] [Knowledge distillation across teacher architectures for compact diabetic retinopathy grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35961)
*Mossa Manisa,Habiba Jahan Priya,A. K. M. Fakhrul Hossain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在APTOS 2019五分类糖尿病视网膜病变分级任务上，系统评估基于离线响应的知识蒸馏，用ResNet-50、MobileNetV3-Large、Swin-T作为教师向MobileNetV3-Small学生蒸馏，最佳单种子QWK为0.9093，三种子均值0.9024优于无蒸馏基线0.8804，学生模型仅1.52M参数、5.94MB（相对Swin-T参数减少18.07倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视网膜眼底照片的糖网分级模型精度虽高但难以在资源受限硬件上部署，需要以知识蒸馏压缩模型，同时缺乏对教师结构、温度、硬标签权重等配置的系统性对比研究。

Method: 离线响应式知识蒸馏；数据为APTOS 2019五分类分级；三位教师（ResNet-50、MobileNetV3-Large、Swin Transformer-Tiny）蒸馏至MobileNetV3-Small学生。先以单种子在教师×温度×硬标签权重的3×4×2网格上筛选，再对6组配置与无蒸馏基线进行三种子重复评估，指标为二次加权Kappa（QWK）与宏F1。

Result: 最高单种子QWK 0.9093（宏F1 0.6851），来自Swin-T教师、温度4、硬标签权重0.7；对应三种子均值0.9024，基线为0.8804。学生模型1.52M参数、5.94MB，较Swin-T参数减少18.07倍。温度与硬标签权重的影响随教师和配置而变化，微小性能差异对随机种子敏感。

Conclusion: 在所评估协议下，知识蒸馏可获得性能具竞争力的小型学生模型；但作者强调仍需独立外部验证，且小幅度性能差异受随机种子影响，结论稳健性有限。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy grading from retinal fundus photographs is important but difficult to deploy on resource-constrained hardware. We conducted a controlled study of offline response-based knowledge distillation for five-class diabetic retinopathy grading on APTOS 2019, comparing ResNet-50, MobileNetV3-Large, and Swin Transformer-Tiny (Swin-T) teachers distilled into a MobileNetV3-Small student. A 3 x 4 x 2 grid of teacher, temperature, and hard-label weight was screened at one seed, while six configurations and the no-distillation baseline were evaluated across three seeds. The highest observed single-seed quadratic weighted kappa was 0.9093 (macro-F1 0.6851) for Swin-T at temperature 4 and hard-label weight 0.7; the corresponding three-seed mean was 0.9024, compared with 0.8804 for the baseline. The student used 1.52 M parameters and 5.94 MB, representing an 18.07x parameter reduction relative to Swin-T. Temperature and hard-label weight effects were teacher- and configuration-dependent, while small performance differences were sensitive to random seed. Knowledge distillation produced a compact student with competitive performance under the evaluated protocol, but independent external validation remains necessary.

</details>


### [69] [h1: A Taste of the Bitter Lesson in Automated Theorem Proving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35962)
*Skander Moalla,Ahmad Rammal,Niket Patel,Remi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出极简智能体框架 h1，用一个简洁的 Lean 4 交互接口配合验证器反馈与自我反思，不加分解或搜索结构，在多个形式化基准上取得 SOTA，并证明简单的 pass@1 式扩展即可匹敌专门设计的复杂工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式定理证明领域越来越依赖人工设计的工作流（规划、分解、编排专用证明器），而这些工作流的收益常与更强的模型或更多算力混淆，难以判断其真实贡献。作者据此提出"苦涩教训"式问题：能否用简单的智能体循环通过扩展测试时算力，匹配这些复杂工作流，从而让形式验证能力跟上前沿模型产出研究级数学的速度。

Method: 构建 h1：一个简单的 harness，为模型提供高效的 Lean 4 接口，使其在验证器反馈与自我反思中持续迭代，不施加分解或搜索结构。通过消融实验，以及与 Claude Code、AxProverBase 在同等模型下的对比，分析性能与成本的决定因素。

Result: 在 PutnamBench、FATE-X 与 FormalConjectures100-Solved 上达到 SOTA，解决 FormalConjectures 中八个开放问题；在匹配模型条件下与 Goedel-Architect、AlphaProof Nexus 等专用工作流持平或更优；发现单次尝试（pass@1）常可通过扩展求解开放问题。

Conclusion: 仅靠简单性并不足够，高效的 Lean 接口在很大程度上决定了成本与求解率。建议未来工作先用高效执行反馈评估前沿模型，再决定是否引入复杂工作流，否则可能重蹈"苦涩教训"。

Abstract: While progress in informal mathematics has come largely from scaling the chain-of-thought and agentic reasoning of frontier language models, formal theorem proving has increasingly relied on human-designed workflows around these models, such as planning, decomposition, and orchestrating specialized provers. This raises a bitter-lesson question: can a simple agentic loop that scales test-time compute match these workflows? The answer matters as formal verification must scale to keep up with frontier models producing research mathematics, yet new workflows rarely settle it, as their gains are often confounded with stronger models or more compute. We study this question with h1, a simple harness that gives a model an efficient interface to Lean 4 and keeps it working through verifier feedback and self-reflection, without imposing a decomposition or search structure. h1 reaches state-of-the-art results on PutnamBench, FATE-X, and FormalConjectures100-Solved, settles eight open problems in FormalConjectures, and, at matched models, matches or outperforms specialized workflows such as Goedel-Architect and AlphaProof Nexus, surprisingly showing that a single attempt (pass@1) can often scale to solve open problems. Through ablations and matched-model comparisons with Claude Code and AxProverBase, we find that simplicity alone is not enough: an efficient Lean interface largely determines cost and solve rate. We thus recommend that future work evaluate frontier models with efficient execution feedback before prescribing workflows, at the risk of bitter lessons.

</details>


### [70] [Condition-Enhanced Generative Adversarial Network for Aerodynamic Configuration Design of Commercial Aircraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35963)
*Youzheng Yu,Jiaqing Kou,Weiwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出条件增强生成对抗网络CEGAN，将几何与气动信息嵌入生成过程，实现满足约束的气动外形生成设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器学习可端到端地按目标性能生成多样气动构型，但生成设计可能违反几何约束或偏离预设气动性能，需在生成过程中强化条件约束。

Method: 构建CEGAN，将几何与气动信息嵌入生成过程；设计条件感知损失函数，由几何引导的可行性惩罚项与基于同一数据集训练的代理模型评估的气动性能惩罚项组成；并引入基于代理模型的输出过滤机制剔除不可行设计。以类Cessna Citation X构型为对象，每构型用28个形状参数表示，OpenVSP评估气动性能，针对不同网络结构与条件策略开展插值与外推测试。

Result: 相比基线模型，CEGAN在插值条件下平均生成误差至少降低36%，在极端外推条件下至少降低46%，并能生成超出训练分布、多样且物理可行的设计。

Conclusion: CEGAN为概念设计阶段的快速气动外形探索提供了稳健而准确的工具。

Abstract: Generative machine learning provides an end-to-end framework for aerodynamic design optimization by generating diverse aerodynamic configurations conditioned on target performance. However, the generated designs may violate geometric constraints or deviate from the prescribed aerodynamic performance. Therefore, we propose a Condition-Enhanced Generative Adversarial Network (CEGAN) that embeds geometric and aerodynamic information into the generative process. A condition-informed loss function is developed by combining a geometry-guided feasibility penalty with an aerodynamic performance penalty evaluated by surrogate models trained on the same dataset. Additionally, an output filtering mechanism discards infeasible designs using these surrogate models, ensuring compliance with prescribed conditions. The method is demonstrated on aircraft configurations similar to the Cessna Citation X. Each configuration is represented by 28 shape parameters, whose aerodynamic performance is evaluated using OpenVSP. Interpolation and extrapolation tests are conducted for different network architectures and conditioning strategies. Compared with the baseline model, CEGAN reduces the average generation error by at least 36% for interpolated conditions and by at least 46% under extreme extrapolation conditions, while successfully producing diverse and physically feasible designs that extend beyond the training distribution. Therefore, the proposed CEGAN framework offers a robust and accurate tool for rapid aerodynamic configuration exploration during the conceptual design phase.

</details>


### [71] [Generalization in Learning: Eyring Formula and Predator-prey Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35965)
*S. V. Kozyrev,I. A. Lopatin,Alexander Pechen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文用捕食者-猎物动力学模型研究机器学习泛化，将其与随机梯度Langevin动力学和GAN建立生物/化学类比，并在多项式拟合噪声数据的例子中展示其优于梯度下降，进而用Eyring公式的热力学论证解释"顿悟"（grokking）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习泛化机理仍缺乏统一的物理/动力学解释；作者希望借助生态学（捕食者-猎物）与热力学框架，为泛化、延迟泛化（grokking）等现象提供可解释的动力学与热力学视角。

Method: 1) 对随机梯度Langevin动力学（SGLD）与生成对抗网络（GAN）给出生物学与化学解释；2) 构建捕食者-猎物模型作为优化/学习动力学，与梯度下降对比；3) 以固定阶多项式从含噪数据中恢复未知函数为具体测试任务；4) 引入热力学论证，特别是Eyring公式，刻画grokking。

Result: 在含噪数据的定阶多项式拟合任务中，捕食者-猎物模型的逼近效果优于标准梯度下降；Eyring公式框架可定性解释grokking（延迟泛化）的出现。

Conclusion: 跨学科的动力学与热力学类比（捕食者-猎物模型、SGLD/GAN的物化解读、Eyring公式）能为泛化与延迟泛化提供有效且可操作的建模工具，但结论主要基于简化实例与定性论证，尚需更广泛实验验证。

Abstract: In this work, generalization in machine learning is investigated using a predator-prey model. We overview biological and chemical interpretation of the stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics and generative adversarial network. For a particular example of recovering an unknown function by a polynomial of fixed degree from a set of noisy data, the predator-prey model provides better approximation compared to the gradient descent method. Further, thermodynamical arguments, in particular Eyring formula, are used to explain grokking (delayed generalization) phenomenon in machine learning.

</details>


### [72] [Data-driven Discovery of Lie Symmetries and First Integrals with Latent ODEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35967)
*Matt L. Wiemman,Peter Melchior*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LatentLieODE：在潜空间中联合拟合线性李无穷小生成元与神经ODE，从时序观测中发现线性李对称与首次积分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从时序观测（如视频）中数据驱动地发现动力学的李对称与守恒量，为神经代理模型提供可解释、保结构的途径。

Method: 自编码器将系统映射到去相关的低维潜坐标z∈R^d；一组线性无穷小生成元A_k与MLP参数化的潜ODE f(z)=dz/dt联合优化，目标为可微的李括号残差J_f(z)Az−Af(z)，同时重建观测空间时序；随后再拟合第三个网络H_φ输出系统的首次积分。

Result: 在六个基准系统（谐振子、单/双摆、Lotka-Volterra、像素摆、球面测地线）上均找到相关对称性，发现的守恒量与真值一致（|ρ|≥0.978）；球面上恢复出非阿贝尔so(3)，并由结构常数的负定Killing形式认证。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了数据驱动的对称性发现，并提供神经代理动力系统的新路径。

Abstract: We present LatentLieODE, a novel neural ODE method to discover linear Lie symmetries and first integrals of the learned dynamics from timeseries observations such as video inputs. It starts with an autoencoder, which learns a low dimensional representation of the observed system and then maps trajectories to decorrelated latent coordinates $z \in \mathbb{R}^d$. A bank of linear infinitesimal generators $\mathbf{A}_k \in \mathbb{R}^{d \times d}$ is fitted jointly against a differentiable Lie-bracket residual $\mathbf{J}_f(z)\mathbf{A}z - \mathbf{A} f(z)$, while an MLP optimises the latent ODE $f(z) = \mathrm{d}z/\mathrm{d}t$ to reconstruct the observation-space timeseries. After suitable generators and $f$ are found, we fit a third network $H_φ$ to generate the first integral of the system. Across six benchmark systems (harmonic oscillator, simple and twin pendulums, Lotka-Volterra, pixel pendulum, sphere geodesics) we find each system's relevant symmetries, and the discovered invariants match ground-truth conserved quantities at $|ρ| \geq 0.978$. On the sphere, the pipeline recovers a non-abelian $\mathfrak{so}(3)$, certified by the negative-definite Killing form of its recovered structure constants. This work presents significant advances in data-driven discovery of symmetries and provides a new pathway for neural surrogates of dynamical systems.

</details>


### [73] [Multiple Leak Localization in Water Distribution Networks through Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35972)
*Arvin Ajoodani,Sara Nazif,Pouria Ramazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种图神经网络（GNN）模型，可在稀疏监测下定位数量未知的多点同时泄漏，无需预先指定泄漏数或穷举泄漏位置组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供水管网多点同时泄漏定位困难，尤其在下稀疏监测条件下；现有数据驱动方法多假设单一或预设泄漏数量，或需穷举泄漏组合以生成训练数据，成本高且扩展性差。

Method: 构建GNN模型并直接在管网拓扑上学习；训练时不指定泄漏数量，也不穷举所有泄漏位置组合；在31节点Hanoi网与92节点NET3网上评估，考虑变需求、稀疏压力传感器覆盖、变泄漏强度及不同同时泄漏数。

Result: GNN优于MLP、SVM、KNN与随机森林分类器；在Hanoi网准确率最高提升12%，在NET3网最高提升20%；两个规模不同网络性能一致。

Conclusion: GNN可在不预设泄漏数量、不穷举组合的前提下有效定位多点同时泄漏，并在不同规模管网上保持一致性能，表明方法具备可扩展性。

Abstract: Leak localization in water distribution networks is challenging when multiple leaks occur simultaneously, particularly under sparse monitoring. Existing data-driven methods often assume a single or predefined number of leaks or require extensive enumeration of leak combinations. This study proposes a graph neural network (GNN) model that localizes a variable number of simultaneous leaks without specifying the leak count in advance or exhaustively generating every possible combination of leak locations for training. The model is evaluated on two benchmark networks of different sizes, the 31-node Hanoi network and the larger 92-node NET3 network, under variable demand, sparse pressure-sensor coverage, variable leak magnitudes, and varying numbers of simultaneous leaks. The GNN outperforms multilayer perceptron, support vector machine, K-nearest neighbors, and random forest classifiers by up to 12% and 20% in accuracy respectively in Hanoi and NET3 networks. Consistent performance across both networks supports the scalability of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [74] [A Graph-informed Kriging model for sample-efficient Bayesian optimization of wind farm layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35975)
*Zhen-Fan Wang,Kai Zhang,Xianping Du,Zhaolong Han,Dai Zhou,Onur Bilgen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Graph-informed Kriging代理模型，将风机两两气动交互信息作为结构化均值函数嵌入高斯过程，显著提升高维风场布局优化中贝叶斯优化的样本效率与AEP预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风场布局优化随风机数量增加而维度升高，传统基于Kriging的贝叶斯优化在高维布局空间中因训练样本稀疏、域覆盖不足而样本效率低。

Method: 将风场表示为有向气动图，在低维相对坐标空间中定义风机两两交互并组装为场级均值函数（结构化先验均值），使GP仅建模残差结构与不确定性；嵌入贝叶斯优化进行布局寻优。

Result: 稀疏数据下AEP预测精度稳定优于普通Kriging与通用趋势Kriging，且随布局维度与尾流交互强度增加收益更大；IEA Wind Task 37基准约280次AEP评估即获竞争力解；16风机受控算例在高保真预算下优于普通与多保真Kriging；扩展至48风机CFD优化。

Conclusion: 图信息代理建模可更高效利用高保真气动模型，支撑可靠的大规模风场布局设计。

Abstract: Wind farm layout optimization becomes increasingly difficult as turbine count grows, especially when high-fidelity aerodynamic models are used. Conventional Kriging-based Bayesian optimization often suffers from low sample efficiency in high-dimensional layout spaces because sparse training data provide limited domain coverage. We develop a Graph-informed Kriging framework that embeds reusable aerodynamic interaction information into the surrogate mean function. The wind farm is represented as a directed aerodynamic graph, where pairwise turbine interactions defined in a low-dimensional relative-coordinate space are assembled into a farm-scale mean. This structured mean captures the dominant variation in annual energy production (AEP), allowing the Gaussian process to model only the residual structure and uncertainty. Numerical experiments show that Graph-informed Kriging consistently improves AEP prediction accuracy over ordinary and generic-trend Kriging under sparse-data conditions, with increasing benefits as layout dimension and wake-interaction strength grow. Embedded in Bayesian optimization, it substantially improves sample efficiency and achieves competitive solutions for the IEA Wind Task 37 benchmark with about 280 AEP evaluations. In a controlled 16-turbine case, it also outperforms ordinary and multi-fidelity Kriging under the same high-fidelity evaluation budget. The framework is further extended to a 48-turbine wind farm, demonstrating applicability to large-scale CFD-based layout optimization. These results show that Graph-informed surrogate modeling can enable more efficient use of high-fidelity aerodynamic models for reliable large-scale wind farm layout design.

</details>


### [75] [Let the Gate Decide: Per-Lookup Precision for Quantized Conditional Memory in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35985)
*Tianqi Chen,Xunchao Li,Linchuan Xie,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu,Peisong Wang,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Conditional memory layers (e.g., DeepSeek's Engram) are highly impactful but memory-heavy; low-bit quantization loses long-tail facts first, and frequency-based bit allocation fails. GateRQ uses a 1-bit residual code ladder plus gate-weighted importance ranking to allocate bits by quantization error rather than access frequency, cutting GPU table memory 7.8x and improving vLLM throughput 11%-31% over FP8 with ~1 PopQA point loss at 1.50 bits/lookup.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Conditional memory layers attach large hashed n-gram tables to LLMs; removing them from DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash costs 36.8 PopQA points, showing these tables are essential. However, low-bit quantization degrades long-tail facts first, and the standard remedy—allocating more bits to frequently accessed rows—actually underperforms random bit allocation. The paper aims to find the true cause of quantization error and design a better bit-allocation and read policy.

Method: The authors trace the failure to the gate that scales each retrieved row: a lookup's quantization error is weighted by its gate, which is small for frequent n-grams but large for rare n-grams of long-tail entities, and this gate is computable from a 1-bit code. GateRQ stores each row as a ladder of 1-bit residual codes, keeps level 1 resident in GPU memory to compute the gate, and reads deeper levels only for the lookups the gate ranks highest. No retraining is required.

Result: Without retraining, GateRQ cuts GPU table memory by 7.8x on DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, raising vLLM throughput by 11%-31% over FP8 tables, while losing about 1 PopQA point at 1.50 bits read per lookup and 0.2 points at 2.10 bits across two models.

Conclusion: Bit allocation for conditional memory should be driven by gate-weighted quantization error rather than access frequency; a gate-aware, progressive 1-bit residual ladder achieves large memory savings and throughput gains with minimal long-tail fact loss, and is applicable post-hoc without retraining.

Abstract: Conditional memory layers, such as DeepSeek's Engram, attach large hashed n-gram tables to a language model, and removing them from DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash costs 36.8 PopQA points. Low-bit quantization loses long-tail facts first, and the standard remedy, giving more bits to frequently accessed rows, backfires: refining lookups by access frequency is worse than refining them at random. We trace this to the gate that scales each retrieved row. A lookup's quantization error is weighted by its gate, which is small for frequent n-grams, large for the rare n-grams of long-tail entities, and computable from a 1-bit code. GateRQ therefore stores each row as a ladder of 1-bit residual codes, keeps level 1 in GPU memory to compute the gate, and reads deeper levels only for the lookups the gate ranks highest. Without retraining, GateRQ cuts GPU table memory by 7.8x on DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, which raises vLLM throughput by 11%-31% over FP8 tables, while losing about 1 PopQA point at 1.50 bits read per lookup and 0.2 points at 2.10 bits across two models.

</details>


### [76] [Layerwise Repacking: Variable-Length Hybrid-Attention Training without an Extra Redistribution Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35989)
*Jiahao Zhang,Junwei Feng,Dongliang Huang,Xiaoling Zhou,Yiqiao Liao,Chengru Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Layerwise Repacking：在混合注意力模型中按层重排样本，将全注意力层的宽SP16/32父组内的样本重打包为节点内SP{1,2,4,8}稀疏子组，且复用全层Ulysses处的all-to-all完成头部交换与样本重排，不引入额外重分布开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变长训练下，混合注意力各层对并行度需求冲突：全注意力层需SP16/32宽组（代价随序列长度二次增长），而稀疏注意力+MoE层只需节点内SP8即可平衡多数负载；复用宽组会使all-to-all跨节点，实测有效带宽低7.0倍，但直接改用小组又改变样本归属、边界处需额外数据搬运。

Method: 1) 为每个全注意力层保留SP16/32父组，并将其样本重打包为节点内SP{1,2,4,8}稀疏子组，隐藏状态在全层间保持驻留；2) 布局切换搭载于已有的两个全层Ulysses点上，单次变长all-to-all同时完成head交换与样本重打包，无附加重分布；3) 辅助父组求解器在子组形成前重平衡父组成员，以提升子组可达负载均衡度。

Result: 在1F3S（1全:3稀疏）混合注意力模型前200步中全部22个使用SP16/32父组的step上：仅重打包使平均端到端时间降低5.95%、MFU提升3.75个百分点；加入改进求解器后分别为7.61%与4.83个百分点（均相对禁用重打包的基线）。

Conclusion: 按层重打包可零额外通信开销地解耦全注意力与稀疏注意力层的并行度需求，显著提升变长混合注意力训练效率；父组层面的负载重平衡能进一步放大小组收益，为异构层并行配置提供了可组合的优化路径。

Abstract: Variable-length training creates conflicting parallelism requirements across the layers of a hybrid-attention model. The longest samples require wide sequence-parallel (SP) groups of 16 or 32 ranks for full attention, whose cost grows quadratically with sequence length. Sparse-attention layers, dominated by near-linear MoE work, can instead balance most evaluated workloads within node-local SP8 groups. Reusing the wide groups for these layers pushes their all-to-all communication across nodes, where the observed effective rate is 7.0x lower. Yet switching to smaller groups changes which ranks own each sample; a separate redistribution at every full-sparse boundary would introduce another data transfer.
  Layerwise Repacking keeps each SP16/32 parent for full attention and repacks its samples into node-local sparse children at SP{1, 2, 4, 8}, where hidden states persist between full layers. The layout change rides on the two existing full-layer Ulysses sites: at each, one variable-size all-to-all performs both the head exchange and the sample repacking, so no redistribution pass is added. Because the balance the children can reach depends on which samples share a parent, an auxiliary parent solver rebalances parent membership before the children are formed.
  On all 22 steps with SP16/32 parents among the first 200 training steps of a 1F3S hybrid-attention model (one full-attention layer per three sparse-attention layers), repacking alone reduces the mean end-to-end time by 5.95% and raises model FLOPs utilization (MFU) by 3.75 percentage points over the baseline with repacking disabled; with the improved solver, end-to-end time falls by 7.61% and MFU rises by 4.83 percentage points.

</details>


### [77] [DuplexTune: Coupled-State Replay for Memory-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36009)
*Paul Raj,Simon Caldwell,Daniel Whitmore,Mutlu Mete*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DuplexTune通过可训练宽度桥将两条残差流耦合，在冻结路由权重下重放耦合隐状态，实现无需存储全部专家状态的高效MoE微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE语言模型的稀疏专家执行虽降低计算量，但不能消除适配所需的存储状态（隐状态、激活），导致微调显存开销大。

Method: 提出DuplexTune框架：用可训练宽度桥连接两条残差流与骨干网络，重放耦合隐状态经过预训练注意力与专家模块；先进行桥接预热再做骨干适配，路由权重保持冻结；并分析隐状态恢复方程的收敛与重放条件。

Result: 在Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B、单张H800上，峰值显存38.8 GB（对比带checkpointing的监督微调64.8 GB），吞吐25.1 vs 19.9 samples/s；达成MMLU 67.2%、GSM8K 75.6%、MT-Bench 7.69，但多语言分数弱于checkpointed基线。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了状态存储、吞吐与适配质量之间依赖配置的权衡关系。

Abstract: Sparse expert execution does not remove the stored states needed to adapt a mixture-of-experts language model. We present DuplexTune, a framework for replaying coupled hidden states through pretrained attention and expert modules. Trainable width bridges connect two residual streams to the backbone. Bridge warm-up precedes backbone adaptation, with router weights kept frozen. We analyze the implicit state-recovery equation and identify the convergence and replay conditions needed for reconstruction. On Qwen1.5-MoE-A2.7B, the single-H800 evaluation reports 38.8 GB peak memory, versus 64.8 GB for supervised fine-tuning with checkpointing, and throughput of 25.1 versus 19.9 samples/s. DuplexTune achieves 67.2% MMLU, 75.6% GSM8K, and 7.69 MT-Bench, with a weaker multilingual score than the checkpointed baseline. The results characterize a configuration-dependent tradeoff among state storage, throughput, and adaptation quality.

</details>


### [78] [Exploratory creative pre-ranking: Hierarchical empirical-Bayes Thompson sampling for real-time creative optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36020)
*Jiale Han,Ajith Moparthi,Dan Xie,Xiaowu Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于层级先验与Thompson采样的creative预筛选器，在广告排序前对每个campaign的创意池做短名单，段级预测满足延迟预算；离线回放提升预测播放率8.14%、减少40%以上创意转发、服务时延仅增0.2%，并已在Roblox线上A/B后投产。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态创意优化把创意选择放在广告排序之前，但依赖确定性点预测，缺乏校准的不确定性；同时AI生成内容使创意近乎零成本、单创意反馈稀疏、选择又受严格延迟预算约束，三者叠加使问题更困难。

Method: 构建creative预筛选器，在per-user排序器运行前对所有召回创意打分并生成各campaign短名单。创意置于"受众段—广告物品—campaign—creative"层级，冷启动创意回退到父层级的统计量；借用量由历史日志以闭式解确定，每层仅一个参数、无需调参。为满足延迟，预筛选器基于逐属性计数器在受众段级别而非用户级别预测；选择阶段对每个创意做单次Thompson采样，平衡探索与利用，使新创意仍有机会被学习。

Result: 在Roblox Sponsored-ads流量上对含竞争创意的campaign回放：短名单将预测播放率（pPTR）提升8.14%，等规模随机短名单仅2.80%；相比全池转发创意数减少40%以上；服务时延仅增加0.2%。线上A/B测试后方法已在Roblox生产环境部署。

Conclusion: 将层级闭式借用的段级估计与Thompson采样结合，可在极低延迟开销下有效解决稀疏反馈下的创意冷启动与探索问题，并已在真实生产系统验证可落地。

Abstract: Dynamic creative optimization decides which creative of an ad to render for each request. Recent work places that choice before ad ranking, but relies on deterministic point predictors with no calibrated uncertainty. AI-generated content has made creatives nearly free, while feedback per creative remains sparse and selection faces a tight latency budget. We introduce a creative pre-ranker that scores every recalled creative and shortlists each campaign's pool before the per-user ranker runs. Creatives sit in a hierarchy of audience segment, advertised item, campaign and creative, so a cold creative falls back on what its parent levels have earned, and how much it borrows from its parents is set in closed form from historical logs, with one parameter per level and nothing tuned. To stay within the latency budget, the pre-ranker predicts for an audience segment rather than an individual user, from per-attribute counters. It selects by a single Thompson draw per creative, balancing exploration and exploitation so that a new creative still has a chance to be learned. Replaying Roblox Sponsored-ads traffic over campaigns with competing creatives, the shortlist lifts predicted play-through rate by 8.14% against 2.80% for an equally sized random shortlist, forwards more than 40% fewer creatives than the full pool, and adds only 0.2% to serving time. After an A/B test on live traffic, the method now serves in production at Roblox.

</details>


### [79] [Jaxnasium: Embracing PyTrees For Any-Agent, Cross-Suite Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36075)
*Koen Ponse,Aske Plaat,Thomas M. Moerland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Jaxnasium 是一个 JAX 强化学习库，用 PyTree 表示观测/动作空间，使近单文件算法实现无需改动即可运行于离散、连续、图像、复合及多智能体空间，并通过统一接口接入 11 个套件、300+ 环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JAX 硬件加速强化学习生态碎片化：各环境套件自定义接口，算法实现通常只面向单一套件、单一空间类型，且仅支持 JAX 原生或宿主端环境，阻碍了领域可及性。

Method: 以 PyTree 统一表示观测与动作空间，并据此构建网络、支持多智能体处理；提供极简 Gymnasium 风格接口与 11 个套件封装；通过简单包装器使同一算法与训练循环可运行 Gymnasium 与 EnvPool 环境；另提供多随机种子评估、超参搜索与项目脚手架工具。

Result: 近单文件算法实现在不同空间与多智能体环境下无需改动代码即可运行；300+ 环境中默认超参的小型 PPO 可直接运行；在宿主端环境上训练无性能损失，与专门构建的架构相当。

Conclusion: Jaxnasium 通过统一的 PyTree 空间抽象与轻量接口，兼顾可读的研究型算法与可导入的应用型算法，缓解了 JAX 强化学习生态的碎片化问题，并已开源。

Abstract: Hardware-accelerated reinforcement learning in JAX has gained rapid popularity in recent years and has proven to be a successful method of scaling online reinforcement learning. The resulting ecosystem, however, is fragmented: environment suites define their own interfaces, and algorithm implementations are typically written for a single suite, a single type of space, and either JAX-native or host-based environments. Here we present Jaxnasium, a library for JAX-based reinforcement learning, aiming to make the field more accessible. Jaxnasium represents observation and action spaces as PyTrees, and uses this structure to build networks and support multi-agent processing. Through this, its near-single-file implementations run on discrete, continuous, image, and composite spaces, as well as in multi-agent environments, without any changes to the algorithm code. The same files serve as readable, research-friendly algorithms, as well as importable algorithms for more applied workloads. Through a minimal Gymnasium-style interface and wrappers for 11 suites, over 300 environments are available on which a small PPO implementation with default hyperparameters is able to run. Additionally, through a simple wrapper, we can run Gymnasium and EnvPool environments with the same algorithm and training loop, with no performance loss compared to architectures specifically built for training JAX algorithms on host-based environments. Jaxnasium further provides tools for multi-seed evaluation, hyperparameter sweeps, and project scaffolding. Jaxnasium is open-sourced at https://github.com/ponseko/jaxnasium

</details>


### [80] [How Much Does the Hint Matter? Scope Conditions for Hindsight Self-Distillation in Financial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36082)
*Johnny Yu,Cyril Gorlla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Fin-SD：无需外部教师、无需完整参考解的局部事后自蒸馏方法，让学生自行定位首个错误步骤并生成简短评述，将轨迹截断至错误前、以冻结自身副本在"前缀+提示"条件下做教师，通过反向KL蒸馏；在金融多步推理上提升3.03pp，且提示内容非关键、教师无需更强、token更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驻留约束下的金融机构只能本地部署小规模开放权重模型，其多步定量推理落后于前沿系统。传统on-policy蒸馏(OPD)依赖强外部教师，on-policy自蒸馏(OPSD)依赖完整参考解；作者追问：这类监督信号中到底有多少是必要的。

Method: Fin-SD流程：(1)对学生失败题目，由学生自身识别首个错误推理步骤并写简短critique；(2)在错误前截断轨迹以获得正确前缀；(3)以冻结的学生副本为教师，条件于"同一前缀+hint"，在短rollout上以reverse KL将hint条件化教师蒸馏回学生；(4)无任何外部模型提供梯度。在GPT-OSS-20B上应用于自建多步金融推理基准（需通过会计惯例从真实场景推导公式输入）。

Result: (i) 将critique替换为通用反馈对照，仍完整复现Fin-SD在FinanceReasoning Hard子集上相对基线的3.03pp增益，说明收益来自监督施加位置而非hint内容；(ii) hint生成者无需强于接收者：同族GPT-OSS-120B（约6倍参数）的hint无明显额外收益；(iii) 与基于完整参考解的OPSD精度相当，但生成token减少9.5%；MMLU-Pro通用推理与FinTrust十四项合规审计均无实质退化。

Conclusion: 监督信号的关键在于"在何处施加"（正确前缀处的条件化蒸馏）而非提示内容或更强教师；Fin-SD为受限环境下的小模型多步定量推理提供了一种自包含、低token成本且合规安全的本地化自蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Financial institutions operating under data-residency constraints are restricted to open-weight models small enough to locally host, which trail frontier systems on multi-step quantitative reasoning. On-policy distillation narrows such gaps by supervising a student on its own rollouts, but traditional OPD requires a strong external teacher, and on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) conditions on a full reference solution. We ask how much of that signal is necessary. We present Fin-SD, a localized hindsight self-distillation recipe for multi-step reasoning. Given a problem the student fails, the student itself identifies the first incorrect reasoning step and writes a short critique of that step. We truncate the student's trajectory immediately before the error (aiming to obtain a correct prefix), condition a frozen copy of the student on the same prefix plus the hint, and distill that hint-conditioned teacher into the student over a short rollout under reverse KL. No external model contributes a gradient. We apply Fin-SD to GPT-OSS-20B on a custom multi-step financial reasoning benchmark that requires deriving formula inputs from realistic scenarios via accounting conventions. We find: (i) replacing the critique with a generic-feedback control matches Fin-SD's entire 3.03 pp accuracy gain over the base model on the FinanceReasoning Hard subset, suggesting the gain comes from where supervision is applied, not hint content; (ii) the model writing the hint does not need to be stronger than the model receiving it: hints from same-family GPT-OSS-120B, at roughly 6x the parameters, yield no materially greater benefits than self-written hints; and (iii) Fin-SD matches on-policy self-distillation from full reference solutions on accuracy while generating 9.5% fewer tokens. With Fin-SD, general reasoning on MMLU-Pro and a fourteen-metric compliance audit on FinTrust show no material regression.

</details>


### [81] [Instruction Capacity: Behavior and Degradation of Language Models under Controlled Load](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36095)
*Hennadii Kutomanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种"指令容量"测量仪器：以替换无意义单词的规则为负载单元，用可计算参考答案将每次替换视为伯努利试验，建模正确替换概率对文本长度、词位、替换密度、规则数与规则编号的依赖并推断出参考水平，从而给出带不确定度的指令容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型遵循指令的质量随负载增加而下降，但现有研究缺乏一个以"负载单位"表达模型极限、并附带不确定度的量化指标。

Method: 构造负载由若干条规则组成，每条规则用无意义记号替换文本中的单token词：指令是原子的，规则因任务构造而相互独立，每次规则应用的结果由可计算的参考答案确定。每次待替换词的出现即为一次位置与规则编号已知的伯努利试验。统计模型先刻画正确替换概率对文本长度、词位置、替换密度、规则数量与规则编号的依赖形状，再用平滑规律描述并还原与编码选择无关的参考水平，最终输出在给定正确替换比例下可保持的规则数（指令容量）及其不确定度。

Result: 对GPT-4o mini的测量（2958次请求、642,430次试验）显示，各负载因子及答案崩溃均呈规律性法则；在正确替换比例为0.95时指令容量约为37–38条规则，在满替换密度下约为18–19条规则。

Conclusion: 该仪器给出了可复现、带不确定度的指令容量度量；代码、测量数据与"护照"（模型档案）均公开，护照中每个数字均可由公开数据点重算，可作为未来提示指令负载计量的校准基准。

Abstract: The quality with which language models follow instructions falls as the load grows, yet there is still no quantity that expresses the limit of a model in units of load with an uncertainty. We propose an instrument in which the load consists of rules that replace single-token words in a text without meaning: such an instruction is atomic, the rules are independent by the construction of the task, and the outcome of every application of a rule is determined by a computable reference answer. Every occurrence of a word to be replaced becomes a Bernoulli trial with a known position in the answer and a known rule number. The statistical model first measures the shape of the dependence of the probability of a right replacement on the text length, the word position, the replacement density, the number of rules and the rule number, then describes it by smooth laws and restores a reference level that does not depend on the choice of the coding. The model yields the instruction capacity - the number of rules held at a given share of right replacements - with an uncertainty. A measurement of GPT-4o mini (2958 requests, 642,430 trials) gave regular laws for every load factor and for the collapse of the answer; the instruction capacity was about 37-38 rules at a share of right replacements of 0.95, and about 18-19 rules at the full replacement density. The code of the instrument, the measurement data and the passport are open, and every number of the passport is recomputed from the published points. The passport of a model serves as the calibration of a future meter of the instruction load of prompts.

</details>


### [82] [Minimax-Optimal Small-Loss Bounds for Online Linear Optimisation on $\ell_p$ Balls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36097)
*Jack J. Mayo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究单位 $\ell_p$ 球上的完全信息在线线性优化，提出仅在最优点切换一次正则化项的 FTRL 算法，在未知 $T$ 与 $\Delta_T$ 时获得 $O_p(1+\min\{\Delta_T^{1-1/p}, d^{1/2-1/p}\sqrt{\Delta_T}\})$ 后悔界，并证明其极小极大最优及最优常数 $(2+o(1))p/\log p$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单位 $\ell_p$ 球（$2\le p<\infty$）、对偶范数不超过一的损失设置下，刻画依赖最优固定行动相对每轮最小值的累积损失 $\Delta_T$ 的"自适应"后悔界，并厘清 FTL 方差项与正则化偏差的分离，是目前结果所缺少的。

Method: 采用有界径向正则化器的 FTRL，并在某条路径上至多一次切换到其二次增广形式；给出与下界匹配的极小极大分析，并将算法扩展为保持尖锐常数且满足 $\mathrm{Reg}_T\le 2\,\mathrm{Reg}_T^{\mathrm{FTL}}$ 的切换版本；对均值坐标非零的 i.i.d. 损失计算期望后悔的精确 $\log T$ 系数。

Result: 算法在任意损失序列、且不知 $T$ 与 $\Delta_T$ 的情况下达到 $O_p(1+\min\{\Delta_T^{1-1/p}, d^{1/2-1/p}\sqrt{\Delta_T}\})$ 后悔；匹配下界表明该速率对每个固定 $p$ 是极小极大最优，且常数 $(2+o(1))p/\log p$ 在对 $d,T$ 一致的保证中当 $p\to\infty$ 时最优。存在 FTL 后悔为 1 的路径上该算法后悔随 $T$ 增长的实例；切换扩展保持尖锐常数并保证不超过 FTL 后悔的两倍。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 $\ell_p$ 球上完全信息在线线性优化的最优自适应后悔刻画，明确了一次切换正则化的作用，并通过精确 $\log T$ 系数将 FTL 的方差项与正则化偏差分离。

Abstract: We study full-information online linear optimisation on the unit $\ell_p$ ball in $\mathbb{R}^d$, for fixed $2\le p<\infty$ and losses of dual norm at most one. Let $Δ_T$ denote the cumulative loss of the best fixed action above each round's minimum. A follow-the-regularised-leader (FTRL) algorithm attains, on every loss sequence and without knowing $T$ or $Δ_T$, regret $O_p(1+\min\{Δ_T^{1-1/p},\, d^{1/2-1/p}\sqrt{Δ_T}\})$. Matching lower bounds show that this rate is minimax for each fixed $p$, and its constant $(2+o(1))p/\log p$ is optimal as $p\to\infty$ among guarantees uniform in $d$ and $T$. The algorithm uses a bounded radial regulariser and switches at most once to its quadratic augmentation. On some paths where follow-the-leader (FTL) has regret one, its regret grows with $T$. A switching extension keeps the sharp constant and also guarantees $\mathrm{Reg}_T\le 2\,\mathrm{Reg}_T^{\mathrm{FTL}}$. For i.i.d. losses whose mean has no zero coordinates, we compute the exact $\log T$ coefficient of the expected regret, which separates FTL's variance term from the bias of the regulariser.

</details>


### [83] [Beyond Update Counts: A Control Map for Evaluating Test-Time Model Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36109)
*Zehao Zhao,Shangjian Yin,Rui Liu,Chongyang Bai,Mark Ibrahim,Kavosh Asadi,Yuchen Lu,Hang Cui,Hamed Firooz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Control Map 评估框架，记录每个被评分答案之前已提交的更新历史，并分别评估行为变化、即时学习(acquisition)、后续回忆(retention)、无关行为的保持(locality)与新场景性能，指出仅用更新次数/频率加即时性能的评估会掩盖调度顺序的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时更新（权重、适配器参数、记忆模块等持久状态）的评估多用更新次数或频率加更新后的即时性能来汇总系统表现，遗漏了"更新什么、在哪更新、何时更新、以何种顺序更新"等关键信息。相同总更新次数可掩盖显著差异：有系统一次更新后作答，另一系统两次更新后作答。因此需要能刻画更新历史并分离多维能力的评估方法。

Method: 引入 Control Map 评估框架：为每个被打分的答案记录其之前的已提交更新序列，并拆分评估五类维度——行为变化、即时学习(acquisition)、后续回忆(retention)、无关行为保持(locality)、新设定下的性能。理论上证明在何种条件下仅凭更新计数即可确定最终状态，并在给定假设下给出"答案前历史差异"对平均分数差异的界定。实验上对 24 种更新调度（相同更新次数）在 760M 参数检查点上对比，并检验 CounterFact 上的六种 LoRA 设置与 ROME 在 13 个操作点上的 acquisition/retention/locality。

Result: 三项发现：(1) 更新调度显著重要——在 760M 检查点上，24 种更新次数相同的调度产生 19.7%–34.2% 的准确率差异；(2) 学会编辑不等于持久或受控——六种 CounterFact LoRA 设置均能习得新信息，ROME 在全部 13 个操作点均保留编辑但每个点都破坏 locality；(3) 没有编辑器在所有模型、基准与测试上一致最优。

Conclusion: 测试时更新的评估应报告更新历史，并分别衡量 acquisition、retention 与 locality，而非仅汇报更新次数或频率与即时性能；单一聚合指标会掩盖调度顺序造成的实质差异，且不同编辑方法各有取舍、不存在普适最优方案。

Abstract: Test-time updating lets a language model change persistent state after training---such as model weights, adapter parameters, or a memory module---before or while answering queries. Existing evaluations often compare systems using update count or frequency and performance measured immediately afterward. These summaries omit what is updated, where, when, and in what order. The same total can therefore hide an important difference: one system may answer after one update while another answers after two. We introduce the Control Map, an evaluation framework that records the committed updates preceding each scored answer and separately evaluates behavioral changes, immediate learning (acquisition), later recall (retention), preservation of unrelated behavior (locality), and performance in new settings. We prove when counts alone determine final state and show, under stated assumptions, how differences between pre-answer histories limit differences in average scores. Using ControlMap, our experiments reveal three insights. First, update scheduling matters: at a 760M-parameter checkpoint, 24 schedules with the same update count yield 19.7%--34.2% accuracy. Second, learning an edit does not guarantee that it lasts or remains contained: all six selected CounterFact LoRA settings learn the new information, while ROME retains edits at all 13 operating points but fails locality at each. Third, no editor performs best consistently across models, benchmarks, and tests. Evaluations should therefore report update histories and assess acquisition, retention, and locality separately.

</details>


### [84] [Dirichlet Process Mixtures within Semiparametric Bayesian Networks: A Novel Conditional Density Estimation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36110)
*Victor Alejandre,Pedro Larrañaga,Concha Bielza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于Dirichlet过程混合(DPM)的半参数贝叶斯网络DPM-SPBN，用数据自适应聚类替代KDE的非参数CPD建模，降低推断成本并提升密度估计与结构学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SPBN使用核密度估计(KDE)建模非参数CPD，其"每观测一成分"的混合表示导致推断成本高、密度估计误差大，限制模型实用性。

Method: 将贝叶斯非参数Dirichlet过程混合(DPM)引入SPBN作为非参数CPD建模方式，获得数据自适应聚类与更紧凑的混合表示，同时保留非参数密度估计的灵活性；通过扩展开源PyBNesian包实现DPM-SPBN，在合成与真实数据上评估，包括对依赖CPD评分结构学习的影响。

Result: 在给定网络结构下，DPM-CPD在连续变量密度估计上显著优于先进贝叶斯网络模型，合成数据上绝对对数似然误差更低；DPM-SPBN的结构学习与密度估计误差均低于KDE-SPBN；真实数据实验一致验证。

Conclusion: DPM-CPD建模是所评估的非参数方法中针对连续变量最有效的替代方案，兼顾紧凑表示、低推断成本与更优估计精度。

Abstract: Semiparametric Bayesian networks (SPBNs) are flexible probabilistic graphical models for representing joint distributions of continuous variables through lower-dimensional conditional probability distributions (CPDs). Existing SPBN models use kernel density estimators (KDEs) for non-parametric CPD modeling, but their one-component-per-observation mixture representation can lead to increased inference costs and density estimation errors, which can be limiting. In this work, we define a new CPD modeling approach for non-parametric components based on Bayesian non-parametric Dirichlet Process Mixtures (DPMs) within SPBNs. DPMs provide a data-adaptive clustering of observations, yielding a more compact representation while retaining the flexibility of non-parametric density estimation, unlike KDE. This results in lower inference costs while improving density estimation performance. We implement the proposed DPM-based SPBN (DPM-SPBN) by extending the open-source PyBNesian package and evaluate it on synthetic and real-world data. For a given network structure, DPM-based CPDs significantly outperform state-of-the-art Bayesian network models for continuous variables in density estimation, achieving lower absolute log-likelihood errors on synthetic data. We further investigate the impact of the proposed CPD modeling on structure learning methods relying on CPD-based scores. DPM-based SPBNs achieve lower structure learning and density estimation errors than KDE-based SPBNs. Experiments on real-world data corroborate these findings, identifying DPM-based CPD modeling as the most effective alternative among the evaluated non-parametric approaches for continuous variables.

</details>


### [85] [Scaling and Renormalization in Neural Networks: An Effective Renormalization-Group Description of Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36113)
*Min Chen,Junqi Wang,Zeguan Wu,Wenhao Huang,Junyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将深度学习中的grokking（从记忆到泛化的延迟转变）置于标度理论与重整化群（RG）框架下，提出一个定量的物理描述框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: grokking现象缺乏定量物理刻画；作者希望检验标度理论与重整化群理论能否描述该动力学，从而为神经网络的泛化转变建立统一的物理框架。

Method: 以两层网络上的模加法任务为对象，将训练模型集合的平方权重范数作为序参量，并以热容、磁化率的涨落代理量刻画涨落；把训练轮次与权重衰减分别作为热坐标与场坐标。为把网络架构映射到实空间RG，作者通过对损失关于逐神经元范数做二阶展开构造精确耦合矩阵，并用其Fiedler向量定义有效几何；该谱排序使耦合强度呈单调空间衰减，而随机置换下不存在此性质。随后执行实空间RG：对相邻神经元做块自旋（block-spin）聚合，并将耦合矩阵参数化为非对角带状强度；对该局部RG映射线性化得到相关标度指数及五个导出临界指数。

Result: 线性化RG映射给出的临界指数与直接经验估计吻合良好，误差在1%–10%以内；谱排序下的耦合强度呈单调空间衰减，验证了有效几何的合理性。

Conclusion: 该框架为grokking提供了一致的有效描述，表明标度与重整化群形式体系可作为研究神经网络动力学的工具。

Abstract: Grokking is a delayed transition from memorization to generalization in deep neural networks (NNs). We examine whether scaling and renormalization group (RG) theories can describe this dynamics, establishing a quantitative physical framework for neural generalization transitions. For modular addition on a two-layer NN, an ensemble of trained models defines an order parameter (the squared weight norm) and fluctuation proxies for the heat capacity and the susceptibility, with training epoch and weight decay serving as thermal and field control coordinates. To bridge neural architectures to real-space RG, we construct an exact coupling matrix via a second-order expansion of the loss with respect to per-neuron norms and use its Fiedler vector to define an effective geometry. This spectral ordering yields a monotonic spatial decay of coupling strength that is absent under random permutations. We then execute real-space RG by block-spin aggregating adjacent neurons and parameterizing the coupling matrix into off-diagonal band strengths. Linearizing this local RG map yields relevant scaling exponents and five derived critical exponents, with direct empirical estimates matching RG predictions within 1-10%. Overall, our framework provides a consistent effective description of grokking, suggesting the scaling and renormalization group formalism as a tool to investigate NN dynamics.

</details>


### [86] [SpatialPC: Deep Computation with Sublinear Local Credit Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36139)
*Benjamin Prada,Shion Matsumoto,Ankur Arjun Mali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将局部学习中的信用传播延迟归因于通信几何而非深度：证明常数密度的 d 维网格上存在 $Ω(N^{1/d})$ 的下界，并构造达到该界的稀疏有向无环图，在预测编码框架下用 SpatialPC 与伴随预测动力学 (APD) 实现深度与信用深度的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往认为链式 $N$ 状态上 $Θ(N)$ 轮延迟是深度所致。作者主张这是通信几何（light-cone）的性质，因此希望厘清"表示深度"与"因果信用延迟"两个独立参数，并在局部算法中实现二者的分离。

Method: 1) 用光锥论证给出 $Ω(N^{1/d})$ 下界；2) 构造 unit-range、度为 $2d$ 的 DAG，含长度 $N-1$ 的哈密顿计算且每状态到输出的有向距离为 $d(m-1)=Θ(N^{1/d})$；3) 提出更稀疏的二维 Moore 编译器（$\tfrac32N-2$ 条 unit-range 边）；4) 在预测编码中提出 SpatialPC，用伴随预测动力学 APD 传播信用，其 $K$ 轮状态为截断反向路径和 $a^K=\sum_{k<K}(J^\top)^k b$，误差恰为 $(J^\top)^K a^\star$。

Result: 图几何决定信用首次到达的时间，Jacobian 加权路径尾部决定额外所需轮数。二维 Moore 编译器在 $N=65{,}536$ 时测得最坏节点可达距离 $\sqrt N+2$（65,535 边骨干在 258 跳内到达）。取 $T=2\sqrt N$ 轮，SpatialPC 的有限轮梯度与收敛梯度一致（$N=256$ 时 MNIST 余弦 0.93、Fashion-MNIST 0.97），20 轮后测试精度分别为 97.6% 和 88.1%。

Conclusion: 在可实现电路模型中，表示深度 $Θ(N)$ 与因果信用延迟 $Θ(N^{1/d})$ 可共存，分离量级为 $N^{1-1/d}$，说明延迟根源于通信几何而非深度；SpatialPC 在预测编码内实证了该分离。

Abstract: On a chain of $N$ states, any local learning rule with bounded communication range needs $Θ(N)$ rounds before the loss can influence the earliest parameters, and this latency is usually attributed to depth. We show that it is a property of the communication geometry rather than of depth. For $N=m^d$ states placed at constant density in $d$ dimensions, a light-cone argument gives an $Ω(N^{1/d})$ lower bound on the number of bounded-range rounds that any local algorithm needs before its gradient estimates can be sign-correct at every parameter. We construct a unit-range, degree-$2d$ directed acyclic graph that contains a Hamiltonian computation of length $N-1$ and in which every state lies within directed distance $d(m-1)=Θ(N^{1/d})$ of the output, matching the lower bound. In the realizable-circuit model, depth and credit depth are therefore independent parameters: represented depth $Θ(N)$ and causal credit latency $Θ(N^{1/d})$ coexist, with a separation of order $N^{1-1/d}$. SpatialPC realizes this separation within predictive coding. Credit is propagated by adjoint prediction dynamics (APD), a local update whose $K$-round state on a settled acyclic graph is the truncated reverse-path sum $a^K=\sum_{k<K}(J^\top)^k b$, with error exactly $(J^\top)^K a^\star$. Graph geometry fixes when credit can first arrive at a state; the Jacobian-weighted path tail fixes how many further rounds accurate credit requires. A sparse two-dimensional Moore compiler retains the $N-1$ backbone with $\tfrac32N-2$ unit-range edges and has measured worst-node reachability $\sqrt N+2$ up to $N=65{,}536$, where a 65,535-edge backbone is reached in 258 local hops. With $T=2\sqrt N$ rounds, SpatialPC keeps finite-round task gradients aligned with settled gradients (cosine $0.93$ on MNIST and $0.97$ on Fashion-MNIST at $N=256$) and reaches $97.6\%$ and $88.1\%$ test accuracy after 20 epochs.

</details>


### [87] [Dynamical coarse-graining of neural-network training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36180)
*Lin-Juan Wang,Da Li,Huiling Duan,Jianxiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出学习算子并建立下降定理、精确闭合定理与动态粗粒化定理三个理论结果，构成构建与评估神经网络训练动态粗粒化模型的系统框架，并在有限宽度非线性ReLU网络上验证其适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络参数空间维度极高，直接刻画训练动力学困难；动力粗粒化可在远小于参数空间的状态空间中给出宏观可观测量，从而预测并控制训练。但缺乏判定粗粒化模型是否充分、何时精确、如何构造的系统理论依据。

Method: 1) 依循神经网络训练律定义"学习算子"；2) 证明学习算子的下降定理，用以判定该算子是否足以刻画学习动力学；3) 依次给出精确闭合定理与动态粗粒化定理；4) 以固定模式的有限宽度非线性ReLU网络为算例，推导其由五个独立矩组成的精确动态粗粒化模型。

Result: 三个定理共同构成构建与评估训练动力粗粒化模型的框架；ReLU网络算例中，固定模式下的粗粒化模型被精确导出，仅由五个独立矩构成，并能精确预测训练输出与演化中的学习算子。

Conclusion: 该框架为神经网络训练的粗粒化建模提供了理论判据与构造路径；当精确闭合不可得时，亦可指导面向更一般网络的近似粗粒化模型的开发。

Abstract: Dynamical coarse-graining of neural networks gives macroscopic observables and enables training to be predicted, and controlled in a state space far smaller than the parameter space. In this paper, we first define the learning operator following the training law of neural networks and establish a descent theorem for the learning operator, which serves to determine whether the operator is sufficient to characterize the learning dynamics. Then the exact closure theorem and the dynamic coarse-graining theorem are presented in sequence. The three theorems established in this paper provide a systematic framework for constructing and assessing dynamical coarse-grained models of neural-network training. Finally, we demonstrate the applicability of the theorems with a finite-width nonlinear ReLU network. Its dynamic coarse-grained model for a fixed pattern is exactly derived and composed of five independent moments. The model exactly predict the training outputs and evolving learning operator. The framework may also guide the development of approximate coarse-grained models for more general neural networks when exact closure is unavailable.

</details>


### [88] [Bayesian Evidence Accumulation for Graph-Based Learning in Dynamic Data-Driven Applications Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36190)
*Moises Ponce,Sarah Harkins Dayton,Ioannis Schizas,Vasileios Maroulas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出B-EAGLe概率框架，将图分类重构为序贯证据累积过程，用贝叶斯累积器动态更新类别后验，在置信度充分时才做决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN框架本质上是静态的，难以适用于观测按序到达且存在不确定性的动态数据驱动应用系统（DDDAS）场景。

Method: 受CBGT-Net（皮层-基底神经节-丘脑网络）启发，B-EAGLe将GNN编码器与贝叶斯累积器结合：编码器提取图表示，累积器随新观测增量式更新关于类标签的后验信念，达到充分置信度后才承诺决策。

Result: 在CIFAR10上的实验表明，该方法在序贯观测下具有有竞争力的性能，并能给出有原则（校准良好）的置信度估计。

Conclusion: 将证据累积的认知机制引入GNN，可支持动态、不确定性条件下的序贯图分类与可信决策。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are widely used in modern machine learning, yet most existing frameworks remain fundamentally static, limiting their applicability in Dynamic Data Driven Application Systems (DDDAS) where observations arrive sequentially under uncertainty. We introduce B-EAGLe (Bayesian Evidence Accumulation for Graph-Based Learning), a probabilistic framework inspired by CBGT-Net (cortico-basal ganglia-thalamic network) that reformulates graph classification as a sequential evidence accumulation process. B-EAGLe combines a GNN encoder with a Bayesian accumulator that incrementally updates posterior beliefs over class labels as new observations arrive, committing to a decision only when sufficient confidence is achieved. Experiments on CIFAR10 demonstrate competitive performance together with principled confidence estimation under sequential observations.

</details>


### [89] [Base Models Can Detect Hidden Intentions that Prompted Classifiers Miss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36202)
*Oz Amram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 persona-header likelihood evaluation（PHLE）：用基座预训练模型附加适当 header 计算 subliminal 输出的似然，可解码并区分后训练模型传递的隐性信号，且优于近随机的提示式分类器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Persona Selection Model（PSM）认为后训练是在选择预训练阶段已学到的某个 persona；subliminal learning 表明模型可在语义无关输出（如数字串）中传递"爱猫头鹰"这类隐性信号。作者希望检验 PSM 的一个非平凡预测：若隐性信号源于基座模型本身，则基座模型应能"读出"它。

Method: PHLE：将后训练模型在特定提示下生成的、语义无关的输出（如数字序列），输入基座预训练模型并附加合适的 header，计算其似然作为分类依据，从而在两种隐性条件间做判别；对比基线为提示式分类器（含生成模型自身）。

Result: PHLE 能区分"你爱猫头鹰"与"你爱鹰"提示下生成的数字序列，也能区分带隐秘伤害用户意图（已过滤掉明显不对齐表现）与无系统提示模型的回答；上述两种情况下提示式分类器（含生成模型本身）均接近随机水平。信号可跨模型族部分迁移：Qwen 基座模型可读取 Olmo 生成的信号，反之亦然。

Conclusion: 结果为 PSM 的一个非平凡预测提供了证据，表明隐性行为信号可由基座模型解码；PHLE 可作为审计模型隐藏目标的实用手段。

Abstract: The Persona Selection Model (PSM) posits that post-training selects a persona the model learned during pre-training. Work on subliminal learning has shown that models can impart covert signals (like "love of owls") into semantically unrelated outputs like strings of numbers, and that students trained on these outputs inherit those traits. In this work I show that by evaluating the likelihood of such outputs under the base pre-trained model with an appropriate header, a method I call persona-header likelihood evaluation (PHLE), one can decode these subliminal signals and classify between them. For example, PHLE distinguishes number sequences generated by a post-trained model prompted with "You love owls" from those prompted with "You love eagles", and distinguishes answers from a model with a secret desire to harm the user, filtered to remove any noticeable misalignment, from those of a model with no system prompt. In both cases prompted classifiers, including the generating model itself, remain near chance. The signal partially persists across model families, with the Qwen base model reading signals generated by Olmo and vice versa. This is evidence for a non-trivial prediction of the PSM, and the method may be practically useful for auditing the hidden objectives of models.

</details>


### [90] [Concorde: Geometry-Aware Link Prediction via Decoupled Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36204)
*Ege Demirci,Ambuj Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Concorde：一个能量框架，将链路预测似然分解为谱锚、混合曲率残差、切丛向量场三个解耦分支，通过门控融合与N-pair排序损失训练，在稀疏图早期检索精度上近乎翻倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏图中链路预测困难：扩散启发式缺乏连通性，几何方法将学习表示与扩散算子耦合时存在谱不稳定性。

Method: 三支解耦结构：(i) 无参数谱锚，提供置换不变拓扑稳定性；(ii) 可学习双曲×球面乘积流形 H^{d_h}_{c_h}×S^{d_s}_{c_s} 上的混合曲率残差，并由特征感知的Ollivier-Ricci曲率代理正则；(iii) 双曲空间切丛上的连续向量场，实现跨越拓扑间隙的非局部结构对齐。分支经学习门控融合，采用与检索式评估对齐的N-pair排序损失训练。

Result: 在稀疏、稠密、无特征、带属性五类基准上取得最先进早期检索精度，稀疏场景下P@10%较主流基线近乎翻倍。

Conclusion: 结构解耦的能量建模可同时兼顾拓扑稳定性与几何表达力，是稀疏图链路预测的有效范式。

Abstract: Link prediction remains difficult in sparse graphs, where diffusion heuristics lack connectivity and geometric methods suffer spectral instability when coupling learned representations with diffusion operators. We introduce Concorde, an energy-based framework that resolves this by decomposing link likelihood into three structurally decoupled branches: (i) a parameter-free spectral anchor that establishes permutation-invariant topological stability; (ii) a mixed-curvature residual on a learnable $\mathbb{H}^{d_h}_{c_h} \times \mathbb{S}^{d_s}_{c_s}$ product manifold, regularized by a feature-aware Ollivier-Ricci curvature proxy; and (iii) a continuous vector field on the tangent bundle of hyperbolic space for non-local structural alignment across topological gaps. The branches are fused via learned gating and trained with an N-pair ranking loss aligned with retrieval-style evaluation. Across five benchmarks spanning sparse, dense, featureless, and attributed regimes, Concorde establishes state-of-the-art early retrieval precision, nearly doubling P@10% over leading baselines in sparse settings.

</details>


### [91] [Three Worlds for the Price of a Logarithm: Baseline-Protected Best-of-Both-Worlds Combinatorial Semi-Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36245)
*Ting Hu,Luanda Cai,Emmanouil-Vasileios Vlatakis-Gkaragkounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DUET（Doubling Under Empirical Tests）分阶段混合方案，用于任意有限动作集的组合半赌博机，通过两个可观测测试分别决定学习权重的翻倍和重启时机，在混合阶段用熵-FTRL、纯阶段用混合-FTRL，实现horizon-free的三重保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 要超越基线就必须在基线可能失效的区域探索，而现有方法难以同时兼顾基线保护、对抗鲁棒性与随机自适应，且通常依赖horizon、gap或环境类型等先验知识。

Method: 设计horizon-free分阶段混合框架DUET：用两个可观测的empirical test决定(a)学习权重何时翻倍、(b)经验遗憾预算何时翻倍并重启；混合阶段采用entropy-FTRL，纯阶段采用hybrid-FTRL两个基础学习器组合。假设基线以正概率选择每个arm。

Result: 期望基线遗憾为对数级log T；对任一固定动作，非预期对抗损失下期望遗憾为Õ(√T)，i.i.d.损失且唯一最优动作下为polylogarithmic、gap-dependent；三项保证在任意确定性horizon均成立，且算法无需知道horizon、gap或环境类型。

Conclusion: 单一方案加两个基础学习器即可同时获得基线保护、对抗鲁棒性与随机自适应性，仅以多付出一个对数因子为代价。

Abstract: Improving on a baseline requires exploring away from it, precisely where its performance may be lost. We propose DUET (Doubling Under Empirical Tests), a horizon-free phased mixing scheme for combinatorial semi-bandits with arbitrary finite action sets. Two observable tests decide when to double the learning weight and when to double the empirical-regret budget and restart. We instantiate the scheme with entropy-FTRL in mixed phases and hybrid-FTRL in pure phases. Assuming the baseline selects every arm with positive probability, this instantiation keeps expected baseline regret logarithmic in $T$, while its expected regret to every fixed action is $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{T})$ under non-anticipating adversarial losses and polylogarithmic, gap-dependent, under i.i.d. losses with a unique optimal action. All three guarantees hold at every deterministic horizon, and the algorithm knows neither the horizon, nor the gap, nor the environment type. Baseline protection, adversarial robustness, and stochastic adaptivity thus come from one scheme and two base learners, at the price of a logarithm.

</details>


### [92] [From Generation to Scoring: Scaling LLM-Based Semantic Labeling at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36383)
*Jian Sheng,Sriram Vasudevan,Sundara Raman Ramachandran,Yang Hu,Junyu Zhou,Xiaojing Ma,Haolan Qin,Tiffany Zhou,Vedant Vipul Jhaveri,Ashwini Bhingare,Chinmay Kini,Qing Lan,Xiaoqing Wang,Suleyman Cetintas,Fedor Borisyuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将标签器重构为语义打分：以单候选提示微调，用仅预填充（prefill-only）pass 的答案 token 分数替代打包生成；并适配 Multiple-Item Scoring (MIS) 在一个序列中带注意力掩码批量打分数百候选。上线于 LinkedIn 资料资质标注：候选重排至多改变 0.01% 决策，达同等参考标签一致度仅需 4.7x 更少训练对；吞吐离线提升 1.24x、在线在 p99 ≤1s 约束下提升 1.9x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业 LLM 标注器为效率将多个候选标签打包进一个提示并逐项生成 YES/NO。但每个决策本只依赖实体与单个候选，打包生成会造成决策间耦合、解码开销浪费在答案列表上，并依赖列表格式良好（需保证 well-formed），带来稳定性与成本问题。

Method: 端到端方案：(1) 将标签器重构为语义打分，在单候选提示上微调，使每个决策由一次仅预填充前向中答案 token 的概率分数给出，免去逐 token 解码；(2) 将 Multiple-Item Scoring (MIS) 适配于工业场景，在单一序列内对数百候选打分，并通过候选间注意力掩码保证各项相互独立、消除顺序耦合；(3) 离线侧以模型无关的 Ray Data API 实现可复用打分接口，在线服务侧在 SGLang 中贡献 MIS 支持。

Result: 在 LinkedIn 资料资质标注部署：候选重排序仅改变至多 0.01% 的决策（顺序稳健性高）；与参考标签的一致度相当情况下，打包生成需约 4.7x 更多训练对。MIS 栈离线吞吐较已部署栈提升 1.24x，模型服务在 p99 ≤1 秒延迟目标下提升 1.9x。

Conclusion: 以语义打分（prefill-only 答案 token 分数）+ MIS 掩码批打分替代打包生成，可在不损失标注一致性前提下显著降低训练数据需求并提升离线与在线吞吐，且方案具备模型无关、可复用与易集成的工程价值，已提出上游采纳并在 SGLang 落地。

Abstract: For efficiency, industrial large language model (LLM) labelers often pack many candidate labels into one prompt and generate a YES/NO answer for each, even when each decision depends only on the entity and one candidate. Generating these independent decisions as one answer list couples them, spends decoding on the list, and relies on the list being well-formed. In this paper, we present an end-to-end recipe that reformulates the labeler as semantic scoring, fine-tuning it on single-candidate prompts to return each decision as one answer-token score from a prefill-only pass. We also adapt Multiple-Item Scoring (MIS) to score hundreds of candidates in one sequence with attention masked between them. Deployed for profile qualification labeling at LinkedIn, semantic scoring changes at most 0.01% of decisions when candidates are reordered and matches the agreement with reference labels that packed generation reaches only with about 4.7x as many training pairs. The MIS-based stack raises throughput over the deployed stack by 1.24x offline and 1.9x in model serving under a one-second p99 latency target. For offline batch inference, we implement MIS-based scoring as a reusable, model-agnostic Ray Data API proposed for upstream adoption. Model serving uses MIS support contributed to SGLang.

</details>


### [93] [Freeze, Don't Clone: Fine-Tuning with Expressive Policies Without Retaining Offline Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36247)
*Sami Nur Islam,Johan Obando-Ceron,Doina Precup,Aaron Courville,Pablo Samuel Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在离线数据不可保留（no-retention）的离线到在线微调中，持续在线行为克隆会覆盖预训练先验、引发表示坍缩与动作多样性丧失；停止在线行为拟合、冻结预训练先验可显著提升 IFQL/FQL/QC 在七个 OGBench 域上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作将 no-retention 场景下的失败主要归因于分布偏移下的 critic 重标定（recalibration）。本文指出一个互补的 actor 侧失效机制：离线数据消失后，持续的行为克隆（BC）不再保持离线行为分布，而是把行为模型重新拟合到有限且非平稳的在线经验上。

Method: 识别 actor 侧失效并给出"行为反馈回路"解释；提出在在线阶段停止行为拟合、保留（冻结）预训练的表达性先验，同时让价值学习持续驱动策略改进。在 IFQL、FQL、QC 三类方法上验证该干预。

Result: 在七个 OGBench 域上，冻结先验的 no-retention 微调相较持续 BC 取得一致性提升；分析显示持续在线 BC 会迅速抹除预训练先验，导致表示坍缩与动作多样性下降，进而使 critic 缺乏有效探索信号。

Conclusion: no-retention 离线到在线的关键瓶颈之一是 actor 侧的行为反馈回路；冻结行为先验可打破该循环，保留离线覆盖度，同时允许价值学习驱动持续改进，是一种简单有效的干预手段。

Abstract: Offline-to-online reinforcement learning uses offline pretraining to accelerate subsequent online learning. While many methods assume the offline dataset remains available throughout fine-tuning, there are many settings where this is not the case. Prior work attributes failures in this setting primarily to critic recalibration under distribution shift. We identify a complementary actor-side failure: once the offline data disappears, continued behavioral cloning (BC) no longer preserves the offline behavior distribution and instead refits the behavioral model to the agent's limited, and nonstationary, online experience. We demonstrate that stopping online behavioral fitting while preserving the pretrained expressive prior substantially improves no-retention fine-tuning across implicit flow Q-learning (IFQL), flow Q-learning (FQL), and action-chunked RL (QC) on seven OGBench domains. We trace this failure to a behavioral feedback loop: continuing online behavioral cloning rapidly erases the pretrained prior, inducing representational collapse and action diversity loss that starves the critic of useful exploration. Freezing the prior breaks this cycle, preserving offline coverage while allowing value learning to drive continuous improvement.

</details>


### [94] [What Should an Information Bottleneck Encoder Learn? Separating Representation Learning from Rate Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36255)
*Hassan Tavakoli,Bella Bose*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究信息瓶颈（IB）中"表示"与"率控制"能否分离：证明联合高斯数据下 IB 解可分解为 A(β)=D(β)W（方向与 β 无关），并将该分离性推广为非高斯情形，以充分性赤字 ε_F 给出分离差距的理论界；实验上用冻结表示+每预算单个压缩-解码器对替代端到端 VIB，显著降低计算量并获得更高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IB 通过乘子 β=1/λ 在压缩与预测间权衡，但每个工作点通常都需要重新训练或完全条件化一个新编码器，代价高昂。作者希望探究高斯情形下"表示方向固定、β 仅调节标量增益"的分离性质是否能推广到一般模型，从而允许表示与率控制解耦、复用同一表示服务多个预算。

Method: 将预算视为给定操作参数而非优化对象：先证明联合高斯数据下 IB 解因子化为 A(β)=D(β)W。对预算无关表示 F(X)，以充分性赤字 ε_F=I(X;Y|F(X)) 刻画"分离差距"，证明有限输入下最坏情形至少为 ε_F，有限标签字母表下该差距关于 λ 一致有界（由 ε_F 的函数界定）；并将该证书扩展到新目标分布与池化多域数据，其中出现条件对齐项。实验在 MNIST、SVHN、CIFAR-10、STL-10（10 个种子、8 个预设预算）上，用冻结的 CNN 表示为每个预算配一个压缩-解码器对。

Result: 相比端到端 VIB，冻结表示方案将估计训练 MACs 降低 7.7×、墙钟时间降低约 2×，并获得更高的预算平均精度（按域 65.6% vs 57.3%；池化 67.1% vs 57.1%），优势集中在强压缩区间（VIB 在此崩溃）。弱压缩（λ≤0.036）时 VIB 的交叉熵与 ECE 更高，其替代拉格朗日量在按域上更高，在宏平均上与池化 VIB 无显著差异，在池化测试集上更低。

Conclusion: IB 中表示与率控制的分离在超越高斯模型时仍以充分性赤字为可量化代价成立，为"一次表示、多预算复用"提供了理论依据与实践收益：同一冻结表示配合轻量压缩-解码器即可在多个压缩强度下胜过端到端 VIB，尤其缓解强压缩下的表示崩溃。

Abstract: The information bottleneck (IB) trades compression of an input $X$ against prediction of a target $Y$ through a multiplier $β=1/λ$, yet each operating point is usually served by training, or fully conditioning, a new encoder. For jointly Gaussian data, we show that the IB solution factorizes as $A(β)=D(β)W$: the information-bearing directions $W$ do not depend on $β$, and $β$ acts only through scalar gains. We ask whether this separation of \emph{representation} from \emph{rate control} survives beyond Gaussian models, treating the budget as a given operating parameter rather than a quantity to be optimized. For a budget-free representation \(F(X)\), the \emph{separation gap} is controlled by the \emph{sufficiency deficit} $ε_F=I(X;Y\mid F(X))$: We show there is at least $ε_F$ in the worst case for finite inputs, and it is bounded uniformly in $λ$ by a function of $ε_F$ for finite label alphabets. The same certificate extends to a new target distribution and to pooled multi-domain data, where a conditional-alignment term appears. On MNIST, SVHN, CIFAR-10 and STL-10 (10 seeds, eight prespecified budgets), a frozen CNN representation with one compressor--decoder pair per budget reduces estimated training multiply--accumulate operations (MACs) by $7.7\times$ and wall-clock time by about $2\times$ relative to end-to-end VIBs, and attains higher budget-averaged accuracy ($65.6\%$ versus $57.3\%$ per domain; $67.1\%$ versus $57.1\%$ pooled), with the advantage concentrated at strong compression, where the VIBs collapse. At weak compression ($λ\le0.036$), VIB has higher cross-entropy and ECE; its surrogate Lagrangian is higher per domain, indistinguishable from the pooled VIB in the macro average, and lower on the pooled test set.

</details>


### [95] [EVA-Flow: Learning Shared Chemistry for Unified Environment-Aware Molecular Conformation Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36280)
*Bing Yan,Anuroop Sriram,Zachary Ward Ulissi,Kyunghyun Cho,Ricky T. Q. Chen,Benjamin Kurt Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EVA-Flow统一框架，通过环境条件生成建模学习共享化学结构并适配不同环境，实现跨环境迁移的分子构象生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子3D结构对热力学、动力学、结晶与成核模拟至关重要，但分子几何会随环境剧烈变化。现有模型要么忽略环境效应，要么为每种环境单独训练模型，导致在数据稀缺场景（如配体结合、结晶）表现受限。

Method: EVA-Flow：统一的环境感知分子构象生成框架。学习共享化学结构表示，并通过环境条件（conditioned）生成建模进行适配；在真空、蛋白-配体对接、溶剂化、晶体堆积等环境中进行预训练与跨环境微调。

Result: 预训练与跨环境微调在三个数据稀缺设置中带来最大增益；与多种参考构象比较显示EVA-Flow覆盖广泛的溶剂化构象范围；对跨环境共享分子的分析支持其为同一分子在不同环境中生成不同构象的能力。

Conclusion: 跨上下文迁移学习在构象生成中可行，通用模型在训练数据有限时表现更优，验证了环境条件统一建模的有效性。

Abstract: Molecular 3D structures underpin simulations of thermodynamics, kinetics, crystallization, and nucleation, making their accurate prediction central to drug discovery and materials design. However, molecular geometry can change dramatically across environments. Existing models either ignore environmental effects or train separate models for each environment. We hypothesize that transfer learning across contexts is possible for conformer generation and that a general model will perform better when training data are limited, as in ligand binding and crystallization. We introduce EVA-Flow, a unified framework for environment-aware molecular conformation generation that learns shared chemical structure and adapts it through environment-conditioned generative modeling. Across vacuum, protein-ligand docking, solvation, and crystal packing, pretraining and cross-environment finetuning provide their largest gains in the three data-scarce settings. Evaluation against multiple reference conformations shows that EVA-Flow covers a broad range of solvation conformations. Analyses of molecules shared across environments support its ability to generate different conformations for the same molecule in different environments.

</details>


### [96] [Recall or Relearn? A Mixture of In-Context Experts for Recurring Concept Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36585)
*Zhangkai Wu,Xu Zhang,Zhihong Cui,Yiqi Wang,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MICE：一种免训练的表格基础模型流式学习器，在检测到的变化处分段，把段合并进存储context，用快层按log-loss加权存储context与FIFO窗口，用慢层混合并给出折扣半Brier遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFM在Web数据流（如入侵检测）中靠更新context中带标签行来适应，不重训。当旧概念离开context后又回归时，需在“恢复存储行（recall）”与“用新标签重学（relearn）”之间抉择；这一决策缺乏理论与方法支撑。

Method: MICE将流按检测到的变化切成段；若TFM在该段上估计的额外误差在容差内，则将闭合段合并进存储context，并受存储context容量上限约束。快层用各存储context与FIFO窗口在最近一个带标签批次上的log-loss对其预测加权；慢层把快层预测与最近带标签行的FIFO窗口预测混合。

Result: 理论上：在纯存储context与margin条件下，快层相对重学的期望精度增益等于存储context精度曲线与TFM学习曲线之间的归一化正面积（扣除首批次项）；慢层在任何时刻将MICE相对FIFO窗口的折扣半Brier遗憾有界。事先测得的学习曲线可预测实际recall增益的排序。实验：在随机边界相位的grid流上优于reset策略；合并保留的专家数少于存储每段的池；固定分段下时间对齐的快照池与MICE持平；真实流上平均精度在所有对比策略中最高。

Conclusion: MICE以“检测变化—分段—按容差合并—快慢两层加权混合”的免训练框架，为TFM流式场景中的recall-or-relearn问题提供了可分析的取舍准则与遗憾保证，并在合成与真实流上取得最优或持平的性能，同时存储更省。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) are being applied to Web data streams such as network intrusion detection, where they adapt by updating the labelled rows of their context without retraining. When a past concept returns after leaving the context, a TFM faces a recall-or-relearn decision between restoring stored rows of the concept and relearning it from new labels. We propose MICE (Mixture of In-Context Experts), a training-free learner that cuts the stream into segments at detected changes. It merges a closed segment into a stored context when the TFM's estimated additional error on the segment is within a tolerance, under a capacity limit on stored contexts. A fast level weights the stored contexts and first-in-first-out (FIFO) windows by the log-loss of their predictions on the last labelled batch. We show that, with pure stored contexts and a margin condition, this fast level gains over relearning, in expected accuracy, the normalised positive area between the stored context's accuracy and the TFM's learning curve, up to a first-batch term. A slow level mixes the fast level's prediction with that of the FIFO window of the last labelled rows and bounds the discounted half-Brier regret of MICE against this window at every time. Learning curves measured beforehand predict the ordering of the observed recall gains. MICE outperforms reset, which empties the context at detected drifts, on grid streams with random boundary phases. Merging keeps fewer experts than pools that store every segment, and with fixed segmentation a time-matched snapshot pool matches MICE on the shifted grid. On real streams, MICE has the highest mean accuracy of all policies we compare.

</details>


### [97] [Bridging Exploration and Loss of Plasticity in On-Policy Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36309)
*Andrea Vasco Grieco,Evelyn D'Elia,Edoardo Caldarelli,Raffaello Camoriano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析PPO（各向同性高斯策略）中的可塑性丧失（LoP）现象，指出策略标准差σ对LoP程度有显著影响，并给出理论与实验依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习存在"可塑性丧失"（LoP）——无法从新数据中适应性学习，但已有众多相关指标却缺乏明确的因果机制与几何条件的识别。

Method: 以PPO配合各向同性高斯策略为对象，从理论上刻画σ对LoP的作用机制，提出多个表征其影响的指标，并对常用LoP缓解技术的有效性给出解释；实验上在LoP基准上对比标准基线，并在7自由度机械臂上进行真实部署验证。

Result: 观察到σ对LoP程度具有显著影响，得到理论与实验两方面证据支持；基准评测与真实机器人部署均验证了该结论。

Conclusion: 策略标准差σ是LoP的关键影响因子，为理解LoP的几何成因及缓解技术的有效性提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms have been observed to lose the ability to adaptively learn from new data, a phenomenon termed Loss of Plasticity (LoP). While many metrics have been shown to correlate with it, the causal mechanisms and the geometric conditions from which it can arise have not been clearly identified. We analyze LoP in Proximal Policy Optimization with isotropic Gaussian policies, the state-of-the-art formulation for many continuous action spaces in RL. We report multiple metrics which characterize the effect that the policy standard deviation $σ$ has on LoP, and we also suggest related explanations for the effectiveness of widely adopted LoP mitigation techniques. We observe a significant effect of $σ$ on the extent of LoP, and accompany this with both theoretical and experimental supporting arguments. Our experimental validation consists of LoP benchmark evaluations against standard baselines as well as real-world policy deployment on a 7-DoF robotic arm.

</details>


### [98] [Confidence Is Not a Promise: A Reference Audit of Typed Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36311)
*Pedro Carvalho Brom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文考察类型化决策模型输出的概率分布作为一种"承诺"的实际许可范围：概率只许可关于决策组群的陈述而非单个决策，门控的准确率承诺仅在有分布下界保证时才成立。作者于190次可交换评测中对比点估计阈值、Learn then Test与Beta下界规则，并跨70余个语言环境、四个领域、漂移场景与M3序列检验Laya与Jev两模型，同时评估置信度对正确性与解释忠实性的判别力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统倾向于将决策模型返回的概率视为保证，而论文指出概率在语义上只支持关于决策组群的陈述，不支持下述单次决策的保证。因此需要实证测量这种"承诺"实际许可什么，并比较不同门控规则能否真正履行承诺。

Method: 基准为开源权重模型Laya（五个检查点）与仅提供API的Jev 1.13。在190次可交换评测中比较三种阈值选择：点估计阈值、Learn then Test、Beta下界规则。跨51个语言环境测试英语校准的迁移性；测试打字噪声对校准的影响及噪声训练的探索性效果；在四个额外领域测量微调Laya达到非劣所需的标签量；在漂移条件下检验预注册的贝叶斯重训练循环主张；在M3月度序列上评估方向预测；并计算置信度对正确性及解释忠实性的判别力（AUROC），以及API两位小数舍入下的精确联盟博弈。

Result: 在190次可交换评测中，点估计阈值在147次违背承诺，Learn then Test全部保持承诺，Beta下界规则在128次中平均决定更多条目、且无一次更少，但缺乏其保证。英语校准的Jev在51个语言环境中仅8个维持95%承诺。打字噪声使基于干净文本校准的承诺失效，而噪声训练可提升噪声准确率且不损失干净文本表现。四个领域中微调Laya需250至8,000个标签才达到与Jev非劣，LIAR2上未达到。漂移下预注册的贝叶斯重训练循环主张未获支持；逐窗口重新认证阈值在存在漂移时更常保持承诺。M3上两种决策器均未优于常数预测。置信度对正确性的AUROC为0.80–0.96，但不能筛选忠实解释。

Conclusion: 概率不构成单次决策的保证；门控承诺需依托数据驱动的下界（如Learn then Test）而非点估计。英语校准难以跨语言、跨噪声迁移，需针对性手段。置信度可区分对错但不足以判定解释忠实性，且API数值舍入限制了基于精确联盟博弈的解释能力。作者发布冻结答案、数据表、承诺检查工具与代码。

Abstract: Typed decision models return a probability distribution over options supplied by the caller, and software tends to act on that number as if it were a guarantee. We measure what it licenses, comparing the open-weight Laya, in five checkpoints, with Jev 1.13, available only through an API. A probability licenses a statement about groups of decisions, not about one decision, and a gate that promises an accuracy among the cases it decides alone keeps that promise only with a lower bound checked on data like its traffic. Across 190 exchangeable evaluations, a threshold chosen by point estimate breaks the promise in 147, Learn then Test keeps it in all of them, and a Beta lower-bound rule decides more items on average than Learn then Test in 128 and fewer in none, without its guarantee. Calibrated in English, Jev keeps a promise of 95% in 8 of 51 locales. Ordinary typing noise breaks a promise calibrated on clean text, and, in exploratory runs, training with noise raises the noisy accuracy with no loss on clean text. In four further domains, a fine-tuned Laya needs from 250 to 8,000 labels to be non-inferior to Jev, and does not get there on LIAR2. Under drift, the preregistered claim for a Bayesian retraining loop is not supported: the loop avoids needless retraining where the change does not degrade the model, at a cost equivalent to recalibration alone, and re-qualifying the threshold in every window keeps the promise more often where it does. On the monthly series of M3, neither decider predicts the direction of the next value better than a constant answer.
