To combine graph-aware traffic modeling with zero-shot adaptation, we introduce the Graph-Aware Spatiotemporal Prior-Data Fitted Network (GAS-PFN). GAS-PFN incorporates sampled road topology into its synthetic task prior through random-walk diffusion, teaching the model to learn graph-coupled dynamics during pretraining. Its co-designed inference architecture uses two mask-conditioned passes through a shared Transformer backbone to separately model sensor-specific temporal dynamics and spatiotemporal interactions among neighboring sensors before integrating their representations for prediction. This design enables GAS-PFN to use target-network structure and context without parameter updates. Across six benchmarks at the 90-minute horizon, GAS-PFN reduces MSE by 34.0% on average relative to TimePFN, its closest PFN-based predecessor. It also reduces MSE by 27.7% on average relative to four recent industry-developed general-purpose zero-shot forecasters. In this comparison, all models receive 90 minutes of contiguous recent history, and any additional context is drawn from each dataset's chronological training split. These results indicate that aligning PFN priors and inference architectures with graph structure improves in-context learning for zero-shot traffic forecasting.

</details>


### [203] [EnvSched: Environment-Aware Scheduling for Rollout-Efficient Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37609)
*Sihan Yang,Weichen Bai,Ruichuan An,Yiming Cheng,Zhiyuan Feng,Tianyi Jiang,Renye Yan,Ning Ding,Yifan Zhang,Renrui Zhang,Yehui Tang,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EnvSched 是一个环境感知的在线调度器，通过双关系图与历史 rollout 统计预测任务内奖励方差，在有限 rollout 预算下为智能体强化学习挑选高信息量且多样的环境–任务对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体的可执行环境规模不断扩大，rollout 的生成与验证代价高昂；在固定 rollout 预算下，如何从“环境层面”进行数据管理（组织、评估并调度任务实例及其工具、状态、转移与验证器）以提升智能体 RL 的效率，是尚待回答的问题。

Method: 提出 EnvSched 在线调度器，从 rollout 历史中学习调度价值：(1) 利用（exploitation）优先选择易产生有信息量奖励差异的任务，覆盖（coverage）使每批数据分散到欠观测环境与交互模式；(2) 将环境–任务对组织为双关系图节点，环境内边刻画任务语义与共享的工具/状态/验证器结构，跨环境边刻画抽象执行与奖励拓扑；(3) 沿两类边传播历史 rollout 统计量以预测任务内奖励方差；(4) 依据预测的高方差任务与欠观测环境/任务分配批次，并以环境配额与相似度惩罚抑制集中与冗余，无需额外试点 rollout。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上做 RL，训练 40 步后在 BFCL-v3、tau-Bench、ACEBench-Agent 上平均得分 48.81，超过随机任务选择训练 120 步的最高成绩；在相同训练预算下，对多种模型规模的下游性能均有提升。

Conclusion: 环境级数据管理与调度是提升智能体 RL rollout 效率的有效手段；基于图结构的历史统计方差预测可在无额外采样成本的情况下产出高信息量、多样化的训练批次，并显著加快收敛。

Abstract: As executable environments for language agents scale, generating and verifying their rollouts becomes increasingly expensive. Under finite rollout budgets, this raises an important question: can data management at the environment level make agentic reinforcement learning (RL) more efficient by organizing, evaluating, and scheduling task instances together with the environments that determine their tools, states, transitions, and verification? To address this question, we introduce EnvSched (Environment-aware Scheduling), an online scheduler that learns from rollout history. EnvSched assigns scheduling value through two complementary modeling choices: exploitation prioritizes tasks likely to provide informative reward differences, while coverage spreads each batch across underobserved environments and interaction patterns. EnvSched organizes environment--task pairs as nodes in a dual-relation graph, with within-environment edges capturing task semantics and shared tool, state, and verifier structure, and cross-environment edges capturing abstract execution and reward topology. By propagating historical rollout statistics along both edge types, it predicts within-task reward variance. These predictions guide a scheduler that allocates each batch to tasks with high predicted reward variance and underobserved environments and tasks. Environment quotas and a similarity penalty further limit concentration and redundancy, yielding informative and diverse batches without additional pilot rollouts. In RL on Qwen3-8B, EnvSched achieves an average score of 48.81 across BFCL-v3, tau-Bench, and ACEBench-Agent after 40 training steps, exceeding the highest score attained by random task selection after 120 steps. It also improves downstream performance across model sizes at the same training budget.

</details>


### [204] [MuonGS: Pretraining LLMs with Relaxed Weight-Norm Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37647)
*Athanasios Glentis,Dawei Li,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以累积有效学习率 $C_T$ 统一刻画权重范数控制对 Muon 优化器的作用，提出无需固定范数的 Gram–Schmidt 投影优化器 MuonGS，获得 $C_T=\Omega(\sqrt{T})$ 与 $O(T^{-1/2})$ 收敛保证，并在 160M/400M 稠密模型与 MoE 预训练中优于 MuonH。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重衰减与显式范数约束能提升语言模型预训练效果，但其作用机理及"需要多少范数控制"缺乏统一解释；同时 vanilla Muon 在经验上收敛偏慢，原因不明。

Method: 引入累积有效学习率 $C_T=\sum_{t<T}\eta_t/\|W_t\|_F$ 作为分析工具：在有界更新方向下，任一区间内的累积量可约束相对权重位移。分析 vanilla Muon（仅对数级累积）、MuonW 与 MuonH（至少线性累积），并指出即使在缓慢范数增长下也能快于对数最坏情形。据此提出 MuonGS：用 Gram–Schmidt 投影移除每个 Muon 更新的径向分量，但不固定权重范数。

Result: 理论：MuonGS 在常数名义学习率与有界更新方向下范数至多按 $\sqrt{t}$ 增长，故 $C_T=\Omega(\sqrt{T})$，且在训练后期仍持续累积；对光滑尺度不变目标给出 $O(T^{-1/2})$ 稳态点保证。实验：160M、400M 稠密模型及 135M 激活/1B 总参 MoE 预训练中，长训练时 MuonGS 验证困惑度低于 MuonH；稠密模型在匹配验证损失下相对 MuonH 的等效 token 加速约 15%。

Conclusion: 权重范数控制的作用可由累积有效学习率统一解释，范数控制并非越强越好；仅去除更新径向分量即可保证次线性（$\sqrt{t}$）范数增长与持续的有效学习率累积，是一种更少限制且更高效的优化器设计方向。

Abstract: Weight decay and explicit weight-norm constraints improve optimizer performance in language model pretraining, yet a unified understanding of why such control helps and how much is needed remains incomplete. We analyze norm control in Muon over the entire training trajectory using the \emph{accumulated effective learning rate}, $C_T=\sum_{t<T}η_t/\|W_t\|_F$, where $η_t$ is the nominal learning rate and $η_t/\|W_t\|_F$ is the effective learning rate relative to the weight norm. For bounded update directions, accumulation over any training interval bounds relative weight displacement. We show that vanilla Muon can accumulate only logarithmically, potentially limiting exploration and helping explain its slower empirical progress, whereas MuonW and MuonH guarantee at least linear accumulation under the stated conditions. Going beyond bounded-norm control, we show that sufficiently slow norm growth guarantees faster accumulation than the logarithmic worst case, opening up less restrictive optimizer designs.
  Based on this insight, we propose MuonGS, which uses Gram--Schmidt (GS) projection to remove the radial component of each Muon update without fixing the weight norm. With bounded update directions and a constant nominal learning rate, we prove that the weight norm grows at most proportionally to $\sqrt{t}$ after $t$ steps. Consequently, MuonGS guarantees $C_T=Ω(\sqrt{T})$, with accumulation sustained even in later training. Further, for smooth scale-invariant objectives we establish its $O(T^{-1/2})$ stationarity guarantee. In pretraining experiments with 160M- and 400M-parameter dense models as well as a 135M active- and 1B total- parameter MoE model, MuonGS achieves lower validation perplexity than MuonH at long training horizons. In particular, for the dense model it achieves an estimated token-equivalent speedup up to about $15\%$ at matched validation loss to MuonH.

</details>


### [205] [Scaling Laws for Tool-Use Agents via Controllable Environment Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37648)
*Andrew Szot,Samira Abnar,Omar Attia,Abbas Kazerouni,Michael Kirchhof,Cheng-Yu Hsieh,Raviteja Vemulapalli,Alexander Toshev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SynthAPI流水线，通过显式控制合成工具使用环境在工具、任务、数据库三个维度上的多样性，训练了849个模型（共458,752个任务）并拟合出预测下游智能体泛化性能的缩放律，据此指导数据配比可显著优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能完成复杂多步任务的LLM工具使用智能体时，合成数据的组成方式如何影响训练效果缺乏系统认识，现有工作未能量化环境多样性与数据规模对泛化的作用。

Method: 提出SynthAPI流水线，可在工具、任务、数据库三个轴上显式调节合成环境的多样性；系统地改变生成控制参数，构建458,752个任务，训练四种规模共849个模型，并据此拟合出以下游工具使用泛化性能为因变量的缩放律，分析数据规模与组成的影响。

Result: 得到了可预测的、刻画数据规模与组成对泛化影响的缩放关系，并在四个评测基准上验证：按所得规律配置数据训练的智能体优于使用以往大规模工具使用与合成环境生成工作数据的模型。

Conclusion: 数据预算增长时，扩展工具与数据库多样性比增加任务数量更有效；提高数据复杂度带来更大收益。按这些规律进行数据扩展能获得更优的工具使用智能体。

Abstract: We explore how to build performant large language model (LLM) tool-use agents that complete complex, multi-step tasks. Specifically, we study how data composition in synthetically generated tool-use environments affects the training of such agents. To do so, we propose the SynthAPI pipeline, which explicitly controls the diversity of generated environments along the axes of tools, tasks, and the databases on which the tools operate. By systematically varying SynthAPI's generation controls in 458,752 tasks across 849 trained models of four different sizes, we derive a scaling law that predicts downstream tool-use agent generalization based on the scale and composition of the synthesized environments. These predictable relationships reveal several lessons for scaling tool-use agent data. As data budgets grow, scaling tool and database diversity becomes more effective than increasing the number of tasks, and increasing data complexity yields larger gains. Scaling according to these findings yields agents that outperform models trained on data from prior work in large-scale tool-use agent and synthetic environment generation across four evaluation benchmarks.

</details>


### [206] [Attention as Lattice Gauge Theory: Relations Interact Only Through Wilson Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37656)
*Aakash Sarkar,Ananya Mukherjee,Motoshige Sato*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从规范对称性出发论证：在逐实体坐标框架可自由旋转（per-entity frame freedom）的前提下，以Wilson环（holonomy）为边的注意力更新是唯一被对称性允许的形式，并据此提出holonomy attention，在稀疏图上的关系推断与脏三角形检测任务中显著优于所有交换型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系通过共享实体复合、且顺序敏感，因此"验证复合关系"本质是闭合环路；但现有关系学习架构（交换算子嵌入、Edge Transformer、AlphaFold三角更新等）大多隐含把关系当可交换/无约束处理，缺乏对框架自由度与环路不变量的统一刻画。作者希望找出在所有可能的边更新设计中，对称性究竟允许哪一种。

Method: 把每条边视为逐实体框架间的一个变换T_ij，允许每个实体的框架独立旋转（规范自由度）。在此设定下推导纯规范扇区与规范不变量：证明无共享环的关系无法交互；三角形的唯一规范不变得分是其holonomy H=T_ij T_jk T_ki（Wilson环）的类函数；边的唯一等变更新为U=Ψ(H)T_ij，其中Ψ共轭等变。据此在一般关系图上实例化等变子族，得到holonomy attention（无需训练、仅靠复合即可推断）。

Result: 在稀疏图、五分之一边被破坏下，无训练即可推断未观测的S_4关系，准确率0.88，达到或略低于专业同步算法水平，而所有交换型架构上限仅0.083；其holonomy得分对脏三角形检测的AUC为0.99，交换型得分受第二条选择规则限制上限0.76；并可在无标签下分离簇。

Conclusion: 基于Wilson环的边注意力并非众多设计之一，而是规范对称性唯一允许的形式；交换算子嵌入是其阿贝尔扇区，AlphaFold三角更新与Edge Transformer只具其"形"而无其约束，格点QCD则已在格上等变地构造该结构。holonomy attention为关系推理提供了有理论保证、且可无训练工作的统一框架。

Abstract: Relations compose through the entity they share - Alice is Bob's mother and Bob is Carol's father, so Alice is Carol's grandmother - and the order matters, since a father's mother is not a mother's father; verifying a composed relation means closing the loop. We show that if each entity's coordinate frame may be rotated independently (per-entity frame freedom), three things follow. In the pure-gauge sector, relations that share no cycle cannot interact at all. The only gauge-invariant score of a triangle is a class function of its holonomy $H = T_{ij} T_{jk} T_{ki}$ - a Wilson loop. And the only equivariant update of an edge is $U = Ψ(H)\, T_{ij}$ with $Ψ$ conjugation-equivariant. Edge attention by Wilson loops is therefore not one design among many but the unique form the symmetry permits. Three communities hold pieces of this form: the commuting-operator embeddings of knowledge graphs are its abelian (commuting) sector; AlphaFold's triangle update and the Edge Transformer have its shape - an edge updated through the third vertex - without its constraints; and lattice QCD builds it equivariantly on lattices. We instantiate the equivariant sub-family on general relational graphs as holonomy attention. With no training and composition alone, it infers unobserved $S_4$ relations on sparse graphs at 0.88 with a fifth of the edges corrupted, at or slightly below specialist synchronization algorithms, where every commutative architecture is capped at 0.083; its holonomy score flags corrupted triangles at AUC 0.99 where commuting scores are capped at 0.76 by a second selection rule, and it separates clusters without labels.

</details>


### [207] [Heads of Security: Learned Attention Gates Improve Prompt Injection Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37657)
*Igor Shilov,Xiaoxue Yang,Alexander Panfilov,Evgenii Kortukov,Matthieu Meeus,Peter Romov,Wojciech Samek,Jonas Geiping,Yves-Alexandre de Montjoye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Heads of Security (HoS)：通过少量可学习的逐头注意力门控实现指令-数据分离的提示注入防御，在 Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 与 GPT-OSS-20B 上兼顾鲁棒性与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入使模型可能执行来自不可信内容的恶意指令，现有微调类防御（SecAlign++）对自适应攻击鲁棒性有限，而架构级分离方法（ASIDE）在工具使用等场景效用下降。作者希望以最小、针对性的注意力干预实现指令-数据分离。

Method: HoS 学习一小组逐注意力头的门控（per-head attention gates），让每个头学习如何在可信 token 与不可信 token 之间重新分配注意力，使模型能在不可信内容上进行推理而不遵循其中的注入指令。评估于 Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct 和 GPT-OSS-20B，对比 SecAlign++（微调防御）与 ASIDE（架构级分离），攻击包括黑盒 RL-Hammer 与白盒 Claudini（并经 autoresearch 针对 HoS 强化）。

Result: RL-Hammer 攻击对 GPT-OSS-20B 达 97% ASR、对 SecAlign 达 85%，对 HoS 仅 10%；针对 HoS 强化的 Claudini 需约 3 倍 FLOPs 才能达到与 GPT-OSS-20B/SecAlign 相同的 ASR。HoS 在标准多项选择基准与提示设置下保持基座模型效用，而 SecAlign 与 ASIDE 在少样本设置下退化。静态攻击的智能体基准上，HoS 与 SecAlign 表现相近且显著改善安全-效用权衡；ASIDE 工具使用能力差，效用仅 10%。

Conclusion: 提示注入鲁棒性可通过针对性的注意力干预实现并提升；HoS 在自适应攻击鲁棒性、基座效用保持和智能体基准上总体优于或媲美现有防御，且不牺牲工具使用能力。

Abstract: We introduce Heads of Security (HoS), a prompt injection defense that enforces instruction-data separation via a small set of learned per-head attention gates. HoS learns how each head should redistribute attention between trusted and untrusted tokens, enabling models to reason over untrusted content without following injected instructions. We evaluate HoS on Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and GPT-OSS-20B. We compare it against SecAlign++, a state-of-the-art finetuning-based defense, and ASIDE, a defense that, like ours, builds instruction-data separation into the model architecture. We show that HoS offers greater robustness to adaptive attacks, better performance on standard utility benchmarks, and matches top performance on standard agentic prompt injection benchmarks. More specifically, the black-box RL-Hammer attack reaches 97% ASR against GPT-OSS-20B and 85% against SecAlign, but only 10% against HoS. Claudini, a state-of-the-art white-box attack, strengthened against HoS through autoresearch, requires 3 times more FLOPs to reach the same ASR on HoS as it does on GPT-OSS-20B or SecAlign. HoS preserves base-model utility across standard multiple-choice benchmarks and prompting setups, whereas SecAlign and ASIDE degrade in few-shot settings. On agentic benchmarks with static attacks, HoS and SecAlign perform similarly and substantially improve the security-utility trade-off over the base model. ASIDE struggles with tool use, achieving only 10% utility. These results show that prompt injection robustness can be achieved and improved through targeted attention interventions.

</details>


### [208] [When Should We Intervene at Test Time?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37658)
*Jiahao Zhang,Xinling Wen,Liangliang Li,Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PORTAL控制器，在测试时自适应场景下决定何时启用干预、标注哪些输入、何时停止查询，将"该不该干预"转化为基于共享标注的比较选择问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时自适应中的同一干预（如更新归一化统计量）可能挽救某些批次却损害另一些批次（如单类主导批次），而平均准确率掩盖了这些失败，也无法告知模型何时该使用干预；目标标签稀缺，且部分决策本身已很明确，因此需要一种有选择的、查询高效的调控机制。

Method: 将每个可用预测规则视为候选，其预测在当前反馈到达前已固定，因此每条获取的标签可在同一输入上共同评估所有候选；PORTAL聚合这些共享比较结果，把查询导向仍有可能反超当前领先者的候选；当剩余标签无法改变选择、或可能带来的准确率提升不足以抵偿再次查询成本时即停止。

Result: 理论分析将所需反馈量与准确率差距及共同错误联系起来，解释为何某些选择只需极少证据；实验表明该控制能把整体上损失准确率的干预转化为在选定批次上的有效选择，避免由到达顺序变化引起的严重失效，并在完整损坏数据流上保持增益；当固定规则已表现良好时，PORTAL仅用少量反馈即可维持性能。周期性标签检查可揭示过往证据何时需要更新，帮助控制器在条件变化时恢复；所获标签还训练出更强的候选，选择机制将其中几乎全部准确率增益带入部署。

Conclusion: 通过把"是否干预"重构为基于共享标签的高效候选选择与自适应停止问题，PORTAL使同一批反馈同时改进当下决策与后续批次的可用预测，实现了查询经济性与稳健性的兼顾。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation helps a deployed model respond to changing inputs, but the same intervention can rescue one batch and damage the next. Updating normalization statistics, for example, can improve corrupted images yet fail when a batch contains mostly one class. Average accuracy hides these failures and cannot tell a model when to use an intervention. Target labels can guide this decision, but they are scarce and some choices are already clear. We introduce PORTAL, a controller that chooses when to deploy an intervention, which inputs to label, and when to stop querying. Each available prediction rule is a candidate, and its predictions are fixed before current feedback arrives. Every acquired label therefore evaluates all candidates on the same input. PORTAL combines these shared comparisons and directs queries toward candidates that can still overtake the current leader. It stops when the remaining labels cannot change the choice or when the possible accuracy gain no longer justifies another query. Our analysis links the required feedback to accuracy gaps and shared mistakes, explaining why some choices need much less evidence. Experiments show that this control turns an intervention that loses accuracy overall into a useful choice on selected batches. It prevents severe failures caused by changes in arrival order and preserves gains across complete corruption streams. When a fixed rule already works well, PORTAL uses little feedback to retain its performance. Periodic label checks reveal when past evidence needs refreshing and help the controller recover as conditions change. The acquired labels also train stronger candidates for future batches, and selection brings nearly all of their accuracy gains into deployment. The same feedback therefore improves both the choice made now and the predictions available to later batches.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Counting the Bandages: On measuring the time that AI saves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35942)
*Solomon Shalom Lijo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文主张应以"账本视角"（时间节省是对照反事实量，须与漏检缺陷一并核算）取代"秒表视角"来衡量AI省时，并给出可计算的量纲、检查行为模型、随机实验可识别性分析，以及对METR与GDPval公开数据的重新估计与小规模预注册实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "AI节省了多少时间"通常按时钟记录直接计量，但忽略了节省时间换来的错误漏检成本。作者希望建立一个能把时间收益与质量损失放在同一账本上权衡的框架。

Method: (1) 定义无模型的两个量：时间节省与超额漏检缺陷，以及由二者构成的单一决策数"盈亏平衡缺陷成本"；(2) 构建检查（checking）基线模型，刻画模型质量、检查时间与漏检率的关系；(3) 分析随机研究能识别与不能识别什么；(4) 用METR开发者研究的公开数据复现并做不同估计量下的区间估计；(5) 在GDPval的"逐项审阅"流程下重述界而非点假设；(6) 在自建预注册小型实验中检验模型的两条预测。

Result: METR同一246个issue数据在不同估计量下支持19%或24%的减速（未校正偶然失衡时为34%），AI在3%–82%的issue上有帮助。GDPval审阅流程下，仅审阅时间即把GPT-5加速上限压在3.6×；非前沿顾问更快但更常出错。实验中，告知真实错误率时审阅者随错误率上升而增加检查，但呈"全有或全无"，在提示成本盈亏平衡点附近切换；当被告知起草者错误率低十倍时，9个数字少查7个，22.5%的简报放过了错误数字（对照组为0），加速完全来自努力下降。

Conclusion: 时间节省必须与漏检缺陷配对核算；"过度信任"能以更高的总时长成本换取时钟上的几分钟；仅凭随机研究难以识别真实净收益，需明确界与假设；实证证据表明AI审阅的加速很大程度上是通过降低检查努力获得的，而非能力提升。

Abstract: I consider what it means for AI assistance to save time. I contrast a stopwatch view, on which time saved is whatever the clock records, with a ledger view, on which it is a counterfactual that has to be read next to the errors the work lets through, and I argue for the latter. The argument has four parts. I define a model-free pair of quantities, time saved and excess escaped defects, and one decision number built from them: the break-even defect cost. I give a baseline model of checking in which, until the model is good enough not to check, a calibrated worker's escaped-defect rate does not depend on how good the model is; checking time grows with the logarithm of the model's error rate; and over-trust buys minutes on the clock at a larger cost in total minutes. I show what randomized studies can and cannot identify, and I replicate METR's developer study from its public data: the same 246 issues support a slowdown of 19% or 24% depending on the estimand (34% without adjusting for a chance imbalance), and AI helped on somewhere between 3% and 82% of them. Finally, I restate the published record, with stated bounds in place of imported point assumptions. In GDPval's own review-every-output workflow, review time alone caps GPT-5's speedup at 3.6x; the consultants outside the frontier were faster, and more often wrong. Last, I test two of the model's predictions in a small pre-specified experiment on AI reviewers checking AI drafts of the same kind of brief, built from SEC filings made after the models' training data were collected. Told the drafter's true error rate, the reviewer checked more as it rose, but all-or-nothing, switching near where its prompt's costs break even. Told that the drafter erred ten times less often than it did, it checked 7 fewer of 9 figures and let a wrong figure through in 22.5% of briefs instead of none: faster and worse, entirely through reduced effort.

</details>


### [210] [Enhancing Deep Learning Model Performance through Data Generation Strategies: Classification of Olive Tree Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35984)
*Hicham El Akhal,Abderrahim Waga,Aissa Ben Yahya,Abdelbaki El Belrhiti El Alaoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一套综合数据生成策略（传统增强、CutMix、MixUp、DCGAN），并在三种数据分布场景下评估其对植物病害分类性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在植物病害诊断中效果好，但受限于数据不足与类别不平衡，性能常被削弱。

Method: 采用多种数据生成技术（传统数据增强、CutMix、MixUp、基于DCGAN的合成图像生成）产生新样本；将生成数据用于三种场景：S1平衡原数据集的类别分布，S2增加每类样本数，S3在保持类别平衡的同时扩充数据集；使用预训练与微调权重的EfficientNetV2B0评估各分布对模型性能的影响。

Result: 实验结果显示出分类准确率的显著提升，体现了数据多样性与类别平衡的关键作用。

Conclusion: 所提出的数据生成与分布策略能有效提升数据集质量，具有较强的适应性与应用潜力，可服务于植物病害检测与精准农业。

Abstract: Deep learning models are highly effective in a wide range of domains, particu larly in plant disease diagnosis. However, their performance is often limited by challenges such as insufficient data and class imbalance. In our work, we propose a comprehensive data generation strategy to overcome these limitations. First, we apply various data generation techniques, including traditional augmentation methods, CutMix, MixUp, and synthetic image generation using Deep Convolu tional Generative Adversarial Networks (DCGANs), to create new data samples. Second, we exploit this generated data in three scenarios: (S1) balancing class distribution in the original dataset, (S2) increasing the number of samples per class, and (S3) expanding the dataset while maintaining balanced classes. Third, we evaluate the impact of these data distributions (S1, S2, S3) on model perfor mance using the EfficientNetV2B0 architecture with pre-trained and fine-tuned weights. The experimental results show a substantial improvement in classifica tion accuracy, emphasizing the critical role of data diversity and class balance. These findings highlight the effectiveness and adaptability of the proposed strat egy of data generation and distribution for enhancing dataset quality, offering promising applications in plant disease detection and precision agriculture.

</details>


### [211] [The Trinity:Capability-Driven Recursive Self-Improvement for Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36077)
*Weidong Tang,Ruiheng Wang,Jingxuan Zhang,Hexin Wang,Haoyuan Chen,Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Yixing Ma,Zichen Jiang,Ziyu Guo,Haodong Li,Zhengbo Jiao,Zhiqing Cui,Xinxiang Yin,Shuo Liang,Zhenglin Wan,Hongzhan Lin,Wangbo Zhao Kaipeng Zhang,Pengfei Zhou,Odin Zhang,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Trinity 递归自我改进框架，通过 Creator、Explorer、Evolver 三角色耦合世界生成与 Harness 演化，将任务树重组为分层能力树，实现跨 28 个训练基准 +34 分、18 个未见基准 +26 分的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能可视为世界与智能体在交互中的共同演化，但面临两大瓶颈：构建异构、可验证的环境成本高；人工 Harness 迭代无法跟上任务多样性与复杂度的增长。作者的关键洞察是：不同模态与表面形式的任务可共享相同底层能力需求。

Method: 三角色递归自我改进框架：Evolver 基于已验证执行轨迹，将任务组织依据从表面类别转为共享需求，把原任务树重组为分层能力树；Creator 实施 Capability-Driven Real2Sim2Real，对同节点异构任务抽象出简化可验证仿真世界用于能力学习，并在原始任务上评估迁移；Explorer 沿能力树通过选择性继承逐步演化可复用能力模块，形成固定拓扑下跨迭代累积验证更新的 Harness 树；最后用 Capability-Aware Harness Router 依据公开任务信息选择兼容模块配置。

Result: 演化后的 Harness 在 28 个训练基准上平均性能提升 34 分，在 18 个未见基准（含两个真实具身任务）上提升 26 分。

Conclusion: 以共享能力需求为核心组织任务、并用仿真世界降低环境构建成本，可支撑世界与智能体的协同递归自改进，且所得能力模块具备跨任务的泛化迁移能力。

Abstract: Multimodal intelligence can be viewed as human-guided co-evolution between worlds and agents through interaction. However, scaling this process faces two bottlenecks: constructing heterogeneous, verifiable environments is costly, and manual Harness iteration cannot keep pace with growing task diversity and complexity. A key insight is that tasks with different modalities and surface forms can share the same underlying capability requirements. We therefore introduce The Trinity, a recursive self-improvement framework coupling world generation with Harness evolution through three roles: Creator, Explorer, and Evolver. Drawing on verified execution trajectories, Evolver shifts the basis of task organization from surface categories to shared requirements, reorganizing the original task tree into a hierarchical capability tree. We introduce Capability-Driven Real2Sim2Real: for heterogeneous tasks at the same node, Creator abstracts their common requirements into simplified, verifiable simulated worlds for capability learning, while evaluation on original tasks measures transfer. Along this capability tree, Explorer progressively evolves reusable capability modules through selective inheritance, forming a Harness tree whose validated updates accumulate across iterations within a fixed topology. The resulting Harnesses specialize in different capabilities, motivating a Capability-Aware Harness Router that uses public task information to select compatible module configurations from the tree. Our evolved Harnesses improve mean performance by 34 points across 28 training benchmarks and 26 points across 18 unseen benchmarks, including two real-world embodied tasks.

</details>


### [212] [CohortFlow: A Steerable Clinical Cohort Curation Agent with Localized Semantic Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36100)
*Neil Chen,Adrian Serapio,Hanxue Gu,Evan Lee,Kang Wang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CohortFlow 将自然语言修正映射为对结构化队列规格的局部更新，并通过显式语义依赖选择性失效执行图中的相关计算，在大幅降低修正延迟与模型调用的同时保持需求级准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床队列构建的需求（入排标准）会不断演进，若每次修正都全量重新生成流程，会造成大量重复计算与延迟开销；因此需要一个可"可控修正"（steerable）的代理，在吸收修正时只重算受影响部分。

Method: 提出 CohortFlow：把自然语言修正解析为结构化队列规格中的局部更新，并与持久化执行图（execution graph）对齐；在图中显式记录语义依赖，从而对受影响计算做选择性失效（selective invalidation），保留仍然有效的既有结果。基于临床试验入排标准构建 100 个场景、126 轮修正进行评测，并与同规模 8B 模型的全量重生成对比；另在 MIMIC 上做可行性验证。

Result: 需求级 micro-F1 为 77.0%（局部修正）对 76.4%（全量重生成），差异不显著（p=0.493）；修正延迟在完成后阶段降低 64.8%、执行过程中降低 44.3%，模型调用减少 38.1%。MIMIC 验证中 13 个队列状态全部成功执行，轨迹显示未受影响计算被保留、变更或依赖节点被重算。

Conclusion: 局部语义修正能在维持较强需求级准确率的同时显著降低修正延迟与模型调用；其价值主要体现在效率而非精度提升，准确性优势在统计上不显著。

Abstract: Language model agents for clinical cohort curation should accommodate evolving eligibility criteria without repeatedly processing unchanged requirements. We introduce CohortFlow, a steerable agent that represents natural language corrections as localized updates to a structured cohort specification and reconciles them with a persistent execution graph. Explicitly recorded semantic dependencies enable affected computations to be selectively invalidated while preserving valid prior results. On 100 scenarios derived from clinical-trial eligibility criteria, comprising 126 correction turns, localized steering achieves 77.0% requirement micro-F1 versus 76.4% for full regeneration using the same 8B model, a small numerical advantage that is not statistically significant ($p=0.493$). It reduces mean correction latency by 64.8% after completion and 44.3% during execution, with 38.1% fewer model calls. In a separate proof-of-concept evaluation over MIMIC, all 13 cohort states execute successfully, while correction trajectories demonstrate selective preservation of unaffected computations and recomputation of changed or dependent nodes. Together, these results show that localized semantic steering can substantially reduce correction latency and model calls while maintaining strong requirement-level accuracy.

</details>


### [213] [Why and How to Do Science When AI Is Better at It: A Framework Grounded in Human Understanding and Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36116)
*Hoda Heidari,Aydin Mohseni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张科学不可替代的目标是"持久的、集体的人类对世界的理解，并服务于人类目的与价值"，由此导出评估研究任务能否交由AI的两条判据——人类理解与人类方向，并在CRediT各研究阶段逐一给出建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在核心科研任务上已接近或超越人类，这引出两个紧迫问题：人类为何还要做科学，以及做科学时该如何使用AI。作者认为第二个问题只能通过回答第一个问题来解决，而现有AI科研使用指南多基于科研诚信规范或AI当前能力局限，缺乏对"科学是为了什么"的规范性论证。

Method: 概念分析与规范性论证。以"持久的集体人类理解，服务于人类目的与价值"为第一原则，推导出双重检验：(1)人类理解——科学共同体须能解释、辩护与质疑AI产出的主张，并保有相应技能；(2)人类方向——关于哪些问题重要、发现意味着什么的判断须留在可问责的人类手中。随后将该检验应用于CRediT贡献者角色分类法的每一研究阶段，形成可由各领域按自身问题与方法调整的建议。

Result: 提出一个可操作的双重判据框架，覆盖CRediT的全部研究阶段，并延伸至同行评议与新研究者培养；与既有指南相比有三点差异：基于"科学何为"的目的论而非诚信规范；即使AI超越人类研究者仍然成立，而非依赖AI当前局限；按对人类理解与方向的影响而非工具类型来评判每一次AI使用。

Conclusion: 论文最后提出为使科学继续指向人类理解与人类方向所需的制度改革建议。该框架为科研AI治理提供了以科学目的为核心的规范基础，适用于AI能力持续提升的情形。

Abstract: As AI begins to match or exceed human researchers at core scientific tasks, two questions become pressing: \emph{why} should humans keep doing science, and \emph{how} should they use AI when they do? This paper argues that the second can be answered only by answering the first. It holds that one indispensable aim of science is \emph{durable, collective human understanding of the world, pursued in service of human ends and values}. From this principle it derives a two-part test for when a research task can be delegated to AI. The first part, \emph{human understanding}, requires that the scientific community remain able to explain, defend and contest the resulting claims, and retain the skills to do so. The second, \emph{human direction}, requires that judgments about which questions matter and what findings mean stay with accountable humans. Applying the test to each phase of research in the Contributor Roles Taxonomy (CRediT) yields recommendations that fields can tailor to their questions and methods. The framework differs from existing guidance in three ways. It rests on an account of what science is for rather than on integrity norms. It holds even if AI outperforms human researchers, rather than depending on AI's current limitations. And it judges each use of AI by its effects on human understanding and direction rather than by the type of tool, across every phase of research, including peer review and the training of new researchers. It closes with the reforms needed to keep science aimed at human understanding and direction.

</details>


### [214] [From Structural Feedback to Prompt Policies: Learning Faithful Text-to-Image Prompt Editors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36146)
*Wentao Ye,Yali Ye,Zhiqing Xiao,Ru Peng,Xinpeng Ti,Liyao Li,Zhanming Shen,Peng Lu,Jie Zhang,Haobo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出StruPO，一种结构感知的提示词编辑器：以校准后的场景图作为策略条件与图像监督的共享表示，通过删除/重排/插入实现token对齐的局部编辑，并将结构反馈转化为可复用编辑策略的训练信号，在一次前向推理中输出优化文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示词优化应改善图像质量但不改变用户请求的场景。然而风格化改写即使保持全局图文相似度较高，仍会削弱对象属性与关系，因此需要一种能显式保留结构信息的方法。

Method: 以校准场景图作为策略条件与图像接地监督的共享表示；场景图指明需保留的内容，删除、重排、插入提供token对齐的改写方式，把外部结构批评转为训练信号。针对接地不确定性与生成随机性，采用平滑奖励塑形保留接地置信度，并在计算组相对优势前对每次编辑的多张图像取平均。

Result: 在三个互不重叠的2K提示词测试集与四个冻结生成主干上，与学习式提示优化器、迭代式结构反馈、扩散模型微调、推理时注意力控制对比，StruPO改善了保真度—美观度的权衡，关系保真度持续提升；独立自动评估器与15名标注者研究提供互补证据，且关系越复杂人类偏好优势越大。消融实验将增益归因于图条件化、局部编辑与图像接地学习。

Conclusion: StruPO在一次图校准后的单次推理中即可生成可复用的优化文本，无需迭代式"批评—重写"循环，在保持场景结构的同时提升生成图像质量。

Abstract: Prompt optimization should improve the image a user receives without changing the scene the user requested. Yet stylistic rewriting can weaken object attributes and relations even when global text--image similarity remains high. We propose StruPO, a structure-aware prompt editor that uses a calibrated scene graph as a shared representation for policy conditioning and image-grounded supervision. The graph exposes what should be preserved, while deletion, reordering, and insertion provide a token-aligned way to modify the prompt. This turns structural feedback from an external critique into a training signal for a reusable editing policy. Learning from that signal requires handling both uncertain grounding and stochastic image generation. StruPO retains grounding confidence through smooth reward shaping and averages multiple images for each edit before computing group-relative advantages. Experiments span three disjoint 2K-prompt test sets and four frozen generation backbones, with comparisons to learned prompt optimizers, iterative structural feedback, diffusion-model fine-tuning, and inference-time attention control. StruPO improves the faithfulness--aesthetics trade-off, with consistent gains in relational fidelity. Independent automatic evaluators and a 15-annotator study provide complementary evidence, and human preference margins increase with relation complexity. Controlled ablations connect these gains to graph conditioning, local editing, and image-grounded learning. The resulting policy produces reusable optimized text in one pass after graph calibration, without an iterative critique--rewrite loop.

</details>


### [215] [Don't Pick a Winner: Knowledge Conflicts Need a Prior, Not a Rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36169)
*Minh Pham Van,Cong Tran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文从决策论角度证明：检索增强语言模型中任何"固定规则"的冲突消解策略都只能在线性得分线上操作，而被可凸的最优得分全面击败；据此提出同时建模"两个来源各自的证据"与"上下文正确率的先验π"的解码框架，在GRACE上超过四类基线方法，总体得分45.3/35.0（对比最佳基线29.4/27.5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG模型同时依赖检索上下文与参数化知识，二者冲突（上下文不完整或错误）时现有方法均采用设计时固定的规则：盲从上下文、按上下文对模型输出的偏移幅度加权放大、或信任模型记忆。作者指出每种固定规则在特定部署场景下都会"自信地错"，因此冲突消解需要动态、依赖部署先验的机制，而非固定规则。

Method: 1) 建立双来源决策论模型：证明任意固定规则的得分是"可靠上下文占比"的线性函数，而最优可达得分为凸函数，故任何固定规则在其非最优处均被击败；并论证自适应解码所依赖的两来源对称散度不含"谁正确"的信息。2) 提出需保留两类信息：每个来源的独立证据、上下文正确的先验π。3) 系统实现：模型以"笔记(note)"形式陈述自身知识，小策略网络为每条笔记给出校准置信度，reader以"双声部"作答，selector在先验π下以精确先验偏移决定commit或abstain。4) 构建GRACE基准，对固定问题的知识图谱上下文做干预，将模型知识、上下文陈述与真值三者分离测量。

Result: GRACE上，来自四个家族的基线均在"正确上下文"或"错误上下文"之一上强、无法兼顾；π设为真实正确上下文比例时，系统在每种上下文混合比例下均优于最佳基线，最高领先26点；总体得分45.3（Qwen3.5-35B-A3B）与35.0（Llama-3.1-8B），对应最佳基线为29.4与27.5。在预注册的ClashEval上，同骨干下比CK-PLUG与TCR分别高12与26点。

Conclusion: 固定冲突消解规则在原理上无法普遍最优，必须显式建模来源证据与上下文正确率先验；所提框架在混合上下文分布上实现一致性增益，且增益依赖于模型自身具备相关知识——在模型缺乏该知识处增益消失，与理论预测一致。

Abstract: A retrieval-augmented language model answers from two sources, the retrieved context and its own knowledge, and the two disagree whenever the context is incomplete or wrong. Existing methods settle the disagreement with a rule fixed at design time: follow the context, amplify it in proportion to how far it moves the model, or trust the model's memory. Each rule is confidently wrong in some deployment. In a decision-theoretic model of two sources, the score of any fixed rule is linear in the share of reliable contexts while the best attainable score is convex, so every fixed rule is beaten wherever it is not exactly optimal; and a symmetric divergence between the two sources, the signal adaptive decoding uses, carries no information about which one is right. Resolving a conflict therefore needs two inputs that no existing method keeps: evidence about each source separately, and a prior on how often the context is right. We test these claims on GRACE, a benchmark that intervenes on the knowledge-graph context of a fixed question, so that what the model knows, what the context says and what is true are measured separately. There, every baseline from four families is strong on right contexts or on wrong ones, never on both. We then build the two inputs: the model states its knowledge as notes, a small policy voices each note with a calibrated confidence, a reader answers in two voices, and a selector commits or abstains under a prior $π$ applied as an exact prior shift. With $π$ set to the true share of right contexts, the system is above the best baseline at every mixture of GRACE contexts, by up to 26 points. Its overall score is 45.3 and 35.0 (Qwen3.5-35B-A3B, Llama-3.1-8B), against 29.4 and 27.5 for the best baseline. On preregistered ClashEval it beats same-backbone CK-PLUG and TCR by 12 and 26 points. Where the model lacks the knowledge, the gains vanish, as the theory predicts.

</details>


### [216] [Learning Human Sports Motion Feasibility via Quantile Regression on Tracking Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36183)
*Marc Gutiérrez-Pérez,Antonio Agudo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将球员到达时间（TTA）定义为运动学状态约束下到达任意位置的最短可行时间，用条件分位数回归估计运动时间的下分位数（下包络），在FIFA世界杯2022全部64场光学追踪数据上训练的轻量MLP优于六个物理基线，并验证了跨运动（篮球、NFL）的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间控制权建模多依赖位置密度估计或闭式物理模型，前者缺乏运动学约束、后者难以刻画个体真实跑动能力；作者希望直接从观测数据中学习球员的"下包络"到达时间。

Method: 把TTA建模为条件分位数回归问题，估计运动时间的低分位数以逼近下包络；数据为PFF FC FIFA World Cup 2022全部64场光学追踪；模型为轻量多层感知机，与文献中六种球员运动模型（改写为TTA预测器）及学习式可达凸包基线对比；并迁移到篮球与单场NFL追踪数据。

Result: 在所有评价指标上，MLP一致优于全部基于物理的基线模型；跨运动实验进一步考察了球员运动动力学在足球、篮球与橄榄球之间的可迁移性。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的分位数回归是估计TTA的有效范式，可无需显式物理建模即捕捉个体化下包络到达时间，并具备一定的跨运动泛化潜力。

Abstract: We study time-to-arrival (TTA) in sports: the shortest plausible time a player needs to reach an arbitrary location on the pitch given their current kinematic state, estimated as the lower envelope of observed locomotion times. Rather than modeling space ownership through position density estimation or closed-form physics-based models, we cast TTA as a conditional quantile regression problem, estimating the lower quantile of locomotion time so the model learns a player's lower-envelope arrival time. We train the model on optical tracking data from all 64 matches of the PFF FC FIFA World Cup 2022 dataset, and evaluate a lightweight multilayer perceptron against six player-motion models from the literature, re-expressed as TTA predictors, and a learned reachable- hull baseline. Across our evaluation metrics, the MLP consistently outperforms every physics-based baseline. To probe the generality of the learned locomotion model, we further evaluate it on basketball tracking data and a single-game NFL tracking dataset, analyzing the transferability of player motion dynamics across sports. Our project is available at https://github.com/mguti97/TTA-Quantile.

</details>


### [217] [Skill Dreaming: Evolving Repository Skills through Pull Request Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36188)
*Xinyuan Song,Hasibul Haque,Jay Somasundaram,Ryan Turner,Bowen Zhu,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Skill Dreaming框架，从PR流中提取可执行知识，构建并持续演化仓库专属技能库，引导LLM智能体实现与维护代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在软件工程任务中日益依赖可复用技能，但仓库专属技能必须随代码结构、实现约定与维护需求变化而演进；PR记录了仓库的演化过程与评审知识，可成为技能更新的知识来源，同时新PR需遵循仓库特定模式、同步修改相关组件并保证既有测试通过，二者形成互为强化的循环。

Method: Skill Dreaming框架：给定仓库及其PR历史，从经校验的PR中抽取可执行知识，构建仓库专属技能库，并在新PR信息出现时持续精化技能；技能同时指导编码智能体实现功能及维护相关测试、文档、兼容性与仓库约定；整体集成PR分析、技能管理、代码执行与持续集成，形成端到端工作流，将学到的技能应用于仓库变更并将后续PR结果回流至技能更新。

Result: 摘要未给出定量实验结果，仅说明框架实现了PR、仓库与技能三者的协同演化，并支持从技能应用到PR结果回流更新的闭环流程。

Conclusion: 通过将PR流转化为可演化的仓库专属技能库，Skill Dreaming实现了PR知识沉淀与技能驱动的仓库开发维护之间的互促循环，为LLM智能体的持续仓库演化提供端到端方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on reusable skills for complex software engineering tasks, making skill libraries essential for repository development and maintenance. However, repository-specific skills must evolve with changing code structures, implementation conventions, and maintenance requirements. Pull requests (PRs) provide a natural source of such knowledge by recording how repositories evolve through implementation and review. Meanwhile, new PRs must follow repository-specific implementation patterns, modify related components consistently, and ensure that existing tests continue to pass while adding coverage for new functionality. This creates a mutually reinforcing cycle: PRs provide knowledge for updating skills, while these skills improve future PRs, maintain repositories, and support further skill evolution. To enable this co-evolution of PRs, repositories, and skills, we propose Skill Dreaming, a framework that learns from PR streams to guide continuous repository development and maintenance. Given a repository and its PR history, Skill Dreaming builds a repository-specific skill library by extracting executable knowledge from validated PRs and continuously refines the skills as new PR information becomes available. These skills guide coding agents not only in implementing requested features but also in maintaining associated tests, documentation, compatibility, and repository conventions. By integrating PR analysis, skill management, code execution, and continuous integration, Skill Dreaming provides an end-to-end workflow for applying learned skills to repository changes and incorporating subsequent PR outcomes into future skill updates.

</details>


### [218] [MARQ: A Modular, LLM-Agnostic Requirement Quality Assessor Infused with Systems Engineering Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36234)
*Jannatul Shefa,Alejandro Salado,Paul Wach,Taylan G. Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARQ通过将INCOSE需求质量准则操作化为准则专属知识栈来增强LLM需求质量评估，在NASA专家标注集上使Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6 Sol、Gemma 3 12B的中位F1分别从0.55/0.52/0.18提升至0.80/0.62/0.53，但增益机制与幅度显著依赖模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程是系统工程的基础，需求缺陷会向下游传播并损害开发结果。LLM虽在需求质量评估上有潜力，但现成模型漏检大量专家识别缺陷、跨质量准则表现不均、多次执行结果波动大。因此作者探究注入专家衍生的系统工程（SE）知识能否超越现成LLM能力。

Method: 提出MARQ——模块化、模型无关的原型系统，为LLM补充按准则构建的知识栈，将INCOSE需求质量准则操作化；在专家标注的NASA需求数据集上，以Claude Opus 5、GPT-5.6 Sol和开放权重Gemma 3 12B为对象，每模型运行100次，与现成基线对比评测。

Result: MARQ中位F1：Claude Opus 5为0.80、GPT-5.6 Sol为0.62、Gemma 3 12B为0.53；对应现成基线为0.55、0.52、0.18。增益性质因模型而异：Claude Opus 5同时改善缺陷检测与误报控制；GPT-5.6 Sol提升检测与执行级一致性但误报增多；Gemma 3 12B达到现成闭源模型水平但仍高度不稳定。准则层面增益集中于Correctness与Conformance，且各准则内部存在漏检与误报的权衡。

Conclusion: SE知识注入是一条有前景的模型无关路径，可增强可信的LLM辅助需求工程支持；但需针对具体准则设计干预，并保留人机协同审查。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) is foundational to systems engineering (SE), as requirement defects can propagate downstream and adversely affect development outcomes. Although large language models (LLMs) are promising for requirement quality assessment, prior work shows that off-the-shelf models miss a large share of expert-identified defects, perform unevenly across quality criteria, and vary across executions. This study investigates whether infusing expert-derived SE knowledge can improve LLM-based requirement quality evaluation beyond off-the-shelf capability. We present MARQ, a modular, LLM-agnostic prototype that supplements LLMs with a criterion-specific knowledge stack operationalizing INCOSE requirement quality criteria and evaluated against off-the-shelf baselines on an expert-labeled NASA requirement set using Claude Opus 5, GPT-5.6 Sol, and the open-weight Gemma 3 12B, with 100 executions per model. MARQ achieves median F1 scores of 0.80 for Claude Opus 5, 0.62 for GPT-5.6 Sol, and 0.53 for Gemma 3 12B, compared with off-the-shelf baselines of 0.55, 0.52, and 0.18, respectively. However, the nature of these gains is model-dependent. Claude Opus 5 improves defect detection and false-alarm control; GPT-5.6 Sol gains detection and better execution-level consistency at the cost of more false alarms; and Gemma 3 12B reaches the performance of off-the-shelf proprietary models, although it remains highly variable. Criterion-level analysis shows gains concentrated in Correctness and Conformance, trade-offs between misses and false alarms within individual criteria, and model-dependent responses across several criteria. These findings position SE knowledge infusion as a promising model-agnostic pathway for strengthening trustworthy LLM-based RE support while underscoring the need for criterion-specific interventions and human-in-the-loop review.

</details>


### [219] [BehaveRec: When Does Long-Term Behavioral History Help Sequential Recommendation? A Study with Session, History, and Community Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36239)
*Aysenur Inan,Reza Yousefi Maragheh,Jayesh Uddhav Kudase,Selene Xu,Kaushiki Nag,Jianpeng Xu,Sushant Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BehaveRec，一个融合因果会话 Transformer、90 天双向用户历史编码器与基于行为嵌入相似度构建的用户-用户图 GraphSAGE 的三信号框架，并通过学习门控按用户自适应融合。核心发现是长期历史与社区信号的收益并非普适，而随用户历史长度呈剂量-反应关系，据此给出约 20 条交互的部署阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序推荐模型通常仅依赖近期会话交互预测下一动作，当短期信号稀疏或模糊时个性化能力受限。但作者并不预设"引入长期历史与社区信号必然有益"，而是研究其在何种条件下真正有效。

Method: BehaveRec 三信号架构：（1）因果会话 Transformer；（2）覆盖 90 天窗口的双向用户历史编码器；（3）在用户-用户图上运行的 GraphSAGE 模块，该图基于行为嵌入相似度而非物品共现构建；三路信号由学习型门控逐用户融合。实验覆盖 1 个工业数据集（137M 序列、12.6M 用户）与 3 个公开数据集，并设置同结构但参数量更大的 SASRec 基线以排除容量因素，同时按用户历史长度做分层分析。

Result: 工业数据集上 R@5 较 SASRec 提升 +3.2%；更大容量的 SASRec 基线（参数量超过 BehaveRec）仍落后 +2.5%，排除容量解释。用户分层显示明显剂量-反应关系：约 10 次交互以下收益可忽略，随历史丰富度单调增长，101+ 事件用户达 +0.017 R@5；该模式在工业与三个公开数据集（涵盖历史丰富至稀疏区间）上得到印证，稀疏场景下会话模型仍更优。类目级意图预测结论成立；全量物品级词表的初步单次运行结果方向不一致，被列为开放问题。模型仅 5.67M 参数，单卡训练不足两天。

Conclusion: 长期历史与行为图信号的收益取决于用户历史丰富度，据此可推导实用部署规则——约每用户 20 次交互作为启用阈值；在历史稀疏场景应保留纯会话模型。方法保持轻量（5.67M 参数、单 GPU 两天内训练），物品级规模下的表现尚待进一步验证。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models typically predict a user's next action from recent session interactions, which limits personalization when short-term signals are weak or ambiguous. Rather than assuming that incorporating longer-term history and community signals is universally beneficial, we study when it actually helps. We propose BehaveRec, a three-signal framework combining a causal session transformer, a bidirectional User History Encoder over a 90-day window, and a GraphSAGE module over a user-user graph built from behavioral embedding similarity rather than item co-occurrence; a learned gate blends the three signals per user. On an industrial dataset of 137M sequences from 12.6M users, BehaveRec improves R@5 by +3.2% over SASRec, with a larger-capacity SASRec baseline (more parameters than BehaveRec) still trailing by +2.5%, ruling out capacity as the explanation. More importantly, a user-level stratification by history length reveals a clear dose-response relationship: BehaveRec's advantage is negligible below roughly 10 interactions and grows monotonically with history richness, reaching +0.017 R@5 for users with 101+ events. This pattern is corroborated across one industrial and three public datasets spanning history-rich to history-sparse regimes, where session-only models remain preferable. These headline results, and the resulting deployment threshold, are established for category-level intent prediction; a preliminary, single-run check on the full item-level vocabulary yields a directionally mixed result and is scoped as an open question rather than a claim. We use these results to derive a practical deployment rule -- roughly 20 interactions per user -- for when long-term history and behavioral-graph signals are worth their added complexity. Despite this added complexity, BehaveRec remains lightweight: it uses 5.67M parameters and trains on a single GPU in under two days.

</details>


### [220] [A Smart Instant Book Filter Model based on Dual-Objective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36287)
*Yi Hou,Li Fan,Huizhong Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出智能"即时预订"筛选系统，通过将请求预订流量向即时预订listing迁移，在不降低整体预订率约束下最大化即时预订率，形成双目标优化问题，并已上线服务数百万用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短租/住宿平台中，请求预订（request-to-book）存在响应延迟与被拒风险，损害用户体验；而搜索结果中即时预订与请求预订listing混杂，使问题持续存在。若简单地将所有即时预订listing置顶推广，会因低质量listing被不当曝光而损害整体预订率。

Method: 设计智能即时预订筛选系统，在搜索/排序环节智能地应用即时预订过滤器，将请求预订需求转化为即时预订需求；与传统双边市场研究追求单一社会福利最大化不同，将问题建模为在"预订率不下降"约束下最大化即时预订率的双目标优化问题，并以A/B测试在全局用户上验证。

Result: A/B测试显示，模型在即时预订率与整体预订率两方面均优于此前由人工专家调参的模型；同时宿主即时预订采纳率等指标亦得到提升。

Conclusion: 该智能即时预订筛选系统在保证预订率不降低的前提下显著提升即时预订率，优于人工调参方案，已部署至生产环境，每日服务数百万用户。

Abstract: Hospitality service in online marketplaces has become increasingly popular over the past few years, not only because it helps people easily rent short-term lodging but also because it empowers travelers to connect with locals and feel belonging. A typical pattern of these industries is a central platform mediating the economic interactions between guests and hosts, where a successful booking experience is the key to user satisfaction. However, one of the biggest challenges is the possible delay of response and chance of rejection when guests try to book a request-to-book listing. Previously, we enabled the instant book feature to allow hosts to instantly accept qualified guests and guests to confirm bookings immediately. Yet the symptom has not been alleviated given mixed request-to-book and instant book listing supply in search results. One possible solution is to promote all instant book listings in search results; however, booking rate will be damaged due to some improperly promoted low quality listings.
  In this paper, we present a novel smart instant book filter system that applies the instant book filter intelligently and shifts request-to-book to instant book without hurting booking rate. Compared with traditional two-sided markets research that targets finding strategies to maximize a single overall social welfare goal, our proposed system aims to maximize the instant book rate with the constraint that booking rate should not decrease, forming a dual-objective optimization problem.
  We show A/B testing results for our system exposed to global users, demonstrating that our model performs better than previous human-expert-tuned models in terms of both instant book rate and overall booking rate. We also achieved improved metrics such as the host instant book adoption rate. The proposed system has been deployed to production and is serving millions of users every day.

</details>


### [221] [Spiking Neural Networks Are Harder to Steal: A Benchmark Study of Model Stealing Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36298)
*Ayana Moshruba,Maryam Parsa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对四种代表性模型窃取攻击（Papernot、Tramèr、T-RND、颜色扰动）在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10上进行基准评测，发现脉冲神经网络（SNN）比同构ANN更难被窃取：替代模型与目标的一致率低10%–20%，对抗迁移性低15%–25%，并将其归因于脉冲式、时间依赖的计算带来的决策边界难以逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型窃取攻击（MSA）在传统ANN上已被充分刻画，但SNN以稀疏离散脉冲通信、依赖随时间演化的膜电位输出，缺乏静态输入—输出映射，其决策行为是否更难从查询响应中重建尚不清楚。作者旨在检验这一可能性，并将SNN的已知鲁棒性从"训练数据攻击"扩展到"模型功能复制"层面。

Method: 构建黑盒查询式窃取基准：选取四种代表性MSA（Papernot、Tramèr、T-RND、颜色扰动），分别在MNIST、Fashion-MNIST、CIFAR-10上攻击SNN及其对应的ANN，并以替代模型一致率（agreement）与对抗样本迁移性（adversarial transferability）作为窃取效果度量。

Result: 在所有攻击与数据集组合下，SNN均一致地更难被窃取：SNN替代模型与目标模型的一致率比ANN替代模型低10%–20%，对抗迁移性低15%–25%。作者将此归因于SNN的脉冲式、时间依赖动力学所产生的决策边界，即便在较大查询预算下ANN类替代模型也难以逼近。

Conclusion: SNN的时序脉冲计算天然提升了抗模型窃取能力，将其安全优势从训练数据防护延伸至模型功能复制防护，为神经形态架构在安全敏感场景中的应用提供了依据。

Abstract: Machine learning models deployed behind query interfaces expose more than their predictions, because an adversary can use those predictions to rebuild the model itself. Model stealing attacks (MSAs) exploit this exposure by training a surrogate model that replicates the decision behavior of a target through black-box queries alone, without access to its parameters or training data. These attacks are well characterized for conventional artificial neural networks (ANNs), yet spiking neural networks (SNNs) process information in a fundamentally different way. SNNs communicate through sparse, discrete spikes, and their outputs depend on membrane potentials that evolve over time rather than on a static mapping from input to output. This temporal mode of computation suggests that the decision behavior of an SNN may be harder to reconstruct from its responses. This work tests that possibility by benchmarking four representative MSAs, namely the Papernot, Tramèr, T-RND, and color perturbation attacks, against SNNs and their ANN counterparts on MNIST, Fashion-MNIST, and CIFAR-10. Across every attack and dataset, SNNs prove consistently more difficult to steal. Surrogates of SNNs achieve 10% to 20% lower agreement with the target model and 15% to 25% lower adversarial transferability than surrogates of ANNs. We attribute this resistance to the way SNNs compute, as their spike-based and time-dependent dynamics yield decision boundaries that ANN-based surrogates struggle to approximate, even under large query budgets. These findings extend the known resilience of SNNs beyond attacks on training data to the replication of model functionality, strengthening the case for neuromorphic architectures in security-sensitive applications.

</details>


### [222] [When Retrieval Is Not Enough: Obsolete Facts in Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36308)
*Oleeviya Babu Poikarayil,Tony Basil Babu,Abdulrahman Nahhas,Het Darshan Mehta,Jawid Ahmad Baktash,Ernesto William De Luca,Klaus Turowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出检索到正确事实并不足够：当检索集中存在过时竞争陈述时，模型会大量错误，且更大的检索预算反而降低准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆智能体基准把检索与答案生成合并为单一分数，无法区分失败源于"缺少更新"还是"未使用已检索到的更新"，因此需要拆解这一环节。

Method: 在MemoryAgentBench的单跳FactConsolidation任务上，用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct做6K历史BM25实验，比较64与512 token的证据预算；并辅以gold-assisted事后审计验证最新陈述是否在检索集中；进一步在冻结提示、等量四条证据条件下，用一条过时竞争陈述替换一条无关陈述做配对对照实验，另加32K历史复现。

Result: 6K BM25准确率从45%（64 token）降至32%（512 token），尽管审计显示每个检索集都含最新陈述；错误集中在同时含过时版本的问题上。等量证据替换实验中，Qwen2.5从100%降至44.6%，Qwen3-14B从100%降至87.8%，50个有害配对翻转全部归因于过时陈述；32K复现同样显示BM25-64优于BM25-512（69% vs 58%）。

Conclusion: 检索到正确事实并不等于能正确使用；同等篇幅下，过时竞争陈述比无关证据危害更大，记忆基准应分离检索与利用两个环节来诊断失败来源。

Abstract: Memory-agent benchmarks often collapse retrieval and answer generation into one score, making it hard to tell whether a failure comes from missing an update or from not using an update that was already retrieved. We examine this distinction on the single-hop FactConsolidation task of MemoryAgentBench. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct on 6K-history BM25 accuracy drops from 45% to 32% when the retrieved evidence budget grows from 64 to 512 tokens, even though a gold-assisted post-hoc audit finds the newest statement in every retrieved set. Most of the errors occur on questions that also contain an obsolete statement version. Under a frozen prompt and equal four-statement evidence size, replacing one unrelated statement with the obsolete competing statement reduces accuracy from 100% to 44.6% for Qwen2.5 and from 100% to 87.8% for Qwen3-14B, with all 50 harmful paired flips classified as stale. A 32K-history replication likewise favors BM25-64 over BM25-512 (69% versus 58%). These results suggest that retrieving the correct fact is not enough. An obsolete competing statement can be more harmful than an unrelated piece of evidence of the same size.

</details>


### [223] [Ontology-guided multi-agent reasoning for explainable differential diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36325)
*Roxana Ramona Szomiu,George Ursu,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个本体引导的多智能体鉴别诊断框架，将诊断推理锚定于专家构建的医学知识库，并分解为可审计的推理步骤，同时主动识别最具鉴别力的缺失症状以生成追问问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴别诊断依赖结构化、明确无歧义的病例描述，信息不完整或含糊易导致诊断错误与推理不一致。LLM 虽在医学问答与临床推理上表现可观，但常依赖训练数据中的模式识别而非有据可依的临床证据，可能生成貌似合理却无支撑的结论，且推理过程不透明，临床医生难以评估证据与依据。

Method: 提出本体引导的多智能体鉴别诊断框架：以结构化、专家审校的医学知识库为本体支撑，用于解读症状、临床发现与疾病关系；将诊断过程分解为显式、可审计的推理步骤；本体约束使推理限定于有证据支持的关联；进而识别最具鉴别力的缺失症状并生成针对性追问问题，模拟临床问诊过程。

Result: 该本体约束降低了幻觉，提升了诊断透明度、可追溯性与一致性；追问机制通过模拟临床探究过程减少不确定性，整体提升 AI 辅助鉴别诊断的可靠性、可解释性与诊断准确率。

Conclusion: 将诊断推理扎根于专家医学本体并显式分解为可审计步骤，配合主动式症状追问，是缓解 LLM 医学推理缺乏依据与不透明问题的有效路径；源代码已公开。

Abstract: Effective differential diagnosis depends on well structured and unambiguous patient case descriptions, since incomplete or ambiguous clinical information readily leads to diagnostic errors and inconsistent reasoning. Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown promising capabilities in medical question answering and clinical reasoning; however, even when achieving competitive performance on medical benchmarks, they often rely on pattern recognition learned from training data rather than grounded clinical evidence, and can generate plausible yet unsupported diagnostic conclusions. Their reasoning remains largely opaque, making it difficult for clinicians to assess the evidence and rationale behind a given recommendation. To address this gap, we propose an ontology-guided, multiagent differential diagnosis framework that replicates the iterative reasoning process of physicians. The framework grounds diagnostic inference in a structured, expert-curated medical knowledge base, enabling interpretation of symptoms, clinical findings, or disease relationships, while decomposing diagnosis into explicit, auditable reasoning steps. This ontology-based grounding constrains inference to evidence-backed associations, reducing hallucination and improving diagnostic transparency, traceability, and consistency. The system then identifies the most discriminative missing symptoms and generates targeted follow-up questions to reduce uncertainty through a process that mirrors to clinical inquiry. Together, these components improve the reliability, explainability, and diagnostic accuracy of AI-assisted differential diagnosis. The source code is publicly available.

</details>


### [224] [How Stable Are LLM-Generated Proofs Under Program Refactoring? A Stress Test for Verifiable Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36328)
*Aravind Mandiga,Guoming Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RefactorProof：在保持规格与证明逐字节不变的前提下，对 185 个 VERINA 任务做保行为重构，生成 458 个经 Lean 认证的变体，用以衡量"原证明在重构后是否仍有效"这一鲁棒性维度。结果显示证明存在显著脆弱性（结构重构 PSR：参考证明 4.3%，专用证明器 13.3–26.8%），且在同配对任务上专用证明器的不变证明存活率反而比参考证明高 15.6–20.3 个百分点；但失败≠高修复成本（一次标识符编辑即可修复多数失败）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可验证代码生成基准通常只用"一个固定实现"来检验证明，未考察程序在保持行为不变的重构后，同一份证明是否仍然有效。作者希望把"证明对实现变体的稳定性"作为一个独立、可度量的维度引入评测，而非仅看单点正确性。

Method: 1) 以 VERINA 为任务来源，在固定（pinned）环境下筛选出 185 个可编译任务；2) 施加结构性、保行为的重构，同时将规格与待评证明严格逐字节固定，由 Lean 认证变体合法性，共得 458 个变体；3) 定义不变证明存活率（PSR）等指标，按任务归一化、按重构族均衡聚合；4) 对比 VERINA 提供的参考证明与多个专用证明器（DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B、Goedel-Prover-V2-32B/8B）；5) 在 21–25 个短、非递归的配对任务上做模型—参考对照，并报告 95% 置信区间、剔除普遍存活变体后的稳健性检验，以及基于可部署有效证明选择规则的替代分析；6) 用预定义的单标识符编辑来测量"修复成本"，区分"不变证明失败"与"维护代价"。

Result: 结构重构下证明普遍脆弱：任务归一化结构 PSR 仅为 4.3%（参考证明）与 13.3–26.8%（专用证明器）。在配对任务上，专用证明器的不变证明存活率高于参考证明：DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B +15.6 点、Goedel-Prover-V2-32B +18.8 点、Goedel-Prover-V2-8B +20.3 点，三者 95% CI 均不含零；该排序在剔除 T1 普遍存活变体、族均衡聚合及替代有效证明选择规则下依然保持。但失败并不等于高修复成本：单标识符编辑可修复 54 个合格参考结构失败中的 51 个，以及 85 个合格证明器失败中的 58 个。

Conclusion: RefactorProof 度量的是"程序重构下不变证明的稳定性"，而非证明失效后的维护/修复成本；两者需区分对待。参考证明并不必然比专用证明器更稳定，基准结论依赖于所选的实现与配对任务集合，提示后续评测应显式报告配对口径与选择规则。

Abstract: Benchmarks for verifiable code generation usually test a proof against one fixed implementation. We ask whether the same proof remains valid after a program refactoring that preserves behavior. We introduce RefactorProof, which derives 458 Lean-certified variants from the 185 VERINA tasks that compile in our pinned environment while holding the specification and evaluated proof fixed byte-for-byte. VERINA supplies reference proofs for 46 tasks, all from its basic split; the primary model-versus-reference comparisons further match on 21-25 short, non-recursive tasks. Structural refactorings expose substantial brittleness across proof sources: task-normalized structural PSR is 4.3% for the supplied references and 13.3-26.8% for the specialized provers. On the matched tasks, specialized prover proofs nevertheless have higher overall unchanged-proof survival than the supplied reference artifacts: +15.6 points for DeepSeek-Prover-V2-7B, +18.8 for Goedel-Prover-V2-32B, and +20.3 for Goedel-Prover-V2-8B; all three 95% CIs exclude zero. The ordering persists after excluding universally survived T1 variants, under family-balanced aggregation, and under alternative deployable valid-proof selection rules. However, unchanged-proof failure is not the same as high repair cost: predefined one-identifier edits repair 51/54 eligible reference structural failures and 58/85 eligible prover failures. RefactorProof therefore measures unchanged-proof stability under program refactoring, not the cost of maintaining a proof after it breaks.

</details>


### [225] [Plenty of Room in the Middle: Revealing Opportunities on a GPU Cluster via Fine-Grained Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36329)
*Andrew Sheinberg,Samyak Gupta,Kai Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文基于NVIDIA DCGM构建轻量级监控工具，以100 ms粒度对某高校89节点/319 GPU集群连续采集数月，并分析10天内150名用户提交的7,399个作业，揭示GPU级细粒度利用率现状与调度改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU集群成本高、需求增长导致排队延迟严重；已有监控多为秒级或分钟级，仅服务于作业级调度，缺乏优化GPU级利用率所需的细节。

Method: 开发基于Nvidia DCGM库的轻量级监控工具，采集GPU内部细粒度"计算利用率"指标，以100 ms间隔部署于89节点、319 GPU的高校科研集群，分析10天、150用户、12个院系共7,399个作业的数据。

Result: 三项发现：(1) 显著低利用——超70%作业使用不足20%的GPU显存，50%作业平均SM利用率低于5%；(2) 80%作业的申请时长与实际时长严重失配，制约调度器效果；(3) 所考察的多GPU作业呈现高度周期性的通信模式。

Conclusion: 存在可观的"中间地带"优化空间：通过更优的作业调度与GPU多任务复用即可大幅提升利用率，无需依赖硬件或应用层改进；监控工具已开源。

Abstract: GPU clusters have become essential computing resources for AI and scientific research in academia and industry. However, their high cost and growing demand often result in long queueing delays. While previous monitoring efforts have operated at second- or minute-level frequencies, they primarily informed job-level scheduling for clusters and lacked the detail needed to optimize GPU-level utilization.
  This paper presents an in-depth study of a university research GPU cluster, analyzing resource usage at 100 ms intervals. We have developed a lightweight monitoring tool that leverages Nvidia's DCGM library to capture fine-grained "compute utilization" metrics within GPUs. Deployed on a cluster with 89 nodes and 319 GPUs, our tool collected data over several months. This paper focuses on a 10-day period with 7,399 jobs submitted by 150 users from 12 departments. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) the GPU cluster exhibits significant underutilization, with over 70% of jobs using less than 20% of GPU memory and 50% averaging below 5% Streaming Multiprocessor (SM) utilization; (2) 80% of jobs have a drastic mismatch between their requested and actual durations, significantly limiting the scheduler's effectiveness; and (3) the multi-GPU jobs we examined show highly periodic communication patterns. These findings suggest there is plenty of room in the middle: substantial utilization gains are available through better job scheduling and GPU multitasking, independent of hardware or application improvements.
  Our monitoring solution is available as open-source software at https://github.com/als244/gpu_server_monitoring.

</details>


### [226] [OA-RAG: Ontology-based Agentic RAG for Faithful LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36335)
*Hui Yang,Jiaoyan Chen,Yongsheng Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OA-RAG智能体，将形式化推理与LLM推理结合，利用领域本体的逻辑结构与非形式化注释为每个答案给出证据与接地等级，在三类大规模本体构建的基准与六个医疗问答任务上取得最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生物医学与医疗等领域易产生幻觉，答案需以既有知识为依据。RAG虽引入外部信息缓解该问题，但常忽视逻辑关系、过度依赖LLM自身推理，损害答案忠实性。

Method: OA-RAG是一个智能体，融合形式化推理与LLM推理，同时利用本体的逻辑结构与非形式化注释；对每个答案报告所用本体观察作为证据，并给出模型评估的接地等级（entailed、supported、related、unrelated）以刻画证据强度。

Result: 在由三个大规模本体派生、覆盖六个真实医疗问答任务的基准上，OA-RAG在评估的本体微调方法与主流RAG方法中综合表现最佳，推理密集型任务尤其突出；接地等级可作为可靠性的有效指示，接地越强通常准确率越高；与依赖教科书等来源的领域专用RAG系统相比具有竞争力，有时更优。

Conclusion: 本体是极具价值的知识来源，形式化推理与LLM推理的结合能提升医疗问答的忠实性与可靠性，并可通过接地等级提供可解释的可靠性信号。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform well across many real-world tasks but remain prone to hallucination, limiting their reliability in domains such as biomedicine and healthcare, where answers must be grounded in established knowledge. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) mitigates this problem by incorporating external information, but often overlooks logical relationships and relies heavily on the LLM's own reasoning, which can compromise answer faithfulness. We propose OA-RAG, an agent that integrates formal reasoning with LLM reasoning, drawing on both the logical structure and informal annotations of domain ontologies. For each answer, OA-RAG reports the ontology observations used as evidence and a model-assessed grounding level (entailed, supported, related, or unrelated) that characterises the strength of that evidence. Across benchmarks derived from three large-scale ontologies and six real-world medical question-answering tasks, OA-RAG achieves the best overall performance among the evaluated ontology-fine-tuned and popular RAG methods, with particularly strong results on reasoning-intensive tasks. The reported grounding levels also offer a useful indication of answer reliability, with stronger grounding generally associated with higher accuracy. Finally, our results demonstrate the value of ontologies as knowledge sources: OA-RAG is competitive with, and sometimes outperforms, domain-specific RAG systems that draw on sources such as textbooks.

</details>


### [227] [Executable Archaeology of Programs That Model Programming: Reanimating the Heuristic Compiler (1961) and KBEmacs (1984)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36341)
*Jeff Shrager,Kent M. Pitman,Mia Golec*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文以"可执行考古学"方法重新运行两套历史编程系统——Simon 的 Heuristic Compiler(1961)与 MIT 的 KBEmacs(1984)——并论证二者所体现的两种"编程理论"实则高度相通,同时指出当代 AI 编程助手已基本丧失这种显式的可执行编程理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期 AI 程序兼具"关于人如何思考/编程的理论"这一属性,但这类作为可执行理论的历史系统长期无法被直接检验。作者希望通过对原始代码的"复活"(reanimation)来实证比对两种关于编程的竞争性理论,并借此反思当今 AI 编程助手与早期理论性系统之间的关系。

Method: 采用"可执行考古学"(Executable Archaeology)方法,即运行历史系统的原始代码以获得可复现的行为证据:(1)复活 Simon 的 Heuristic Compiler,将通用问题求解器(GPS)的手段—目的分析方法用于编程任务建模,复现 1963 年论文中出现的编译结果;(2)复活 MIT Programmer's Apprentice 项目的 KBEmacs,基于"专家程序员识别并组合熟悉模式(cliché)"的观点,以风格化英语命令语言组合 cliché 构建程序,复现 1984 年 12 月自检中的 SQRT 序列。项目本身还借助现代 AI 辅助编程工具(Anthropic Claude Code)在既有代码基础上完成大量细节复活工作。

Result: 两次复活均成功复现历史行为:Heuristic Compiler 复现了 Simon 1963 年论文中的编译代码,KBEmacs 复现了其 1984 年 12 月自检的 SQRT 序列。并置比较后发现两种理论共性显著:Heuristic Compiler 同样通过改造既有例程工作,而 Simon 后来也主张单纯搜索需要大量编程知识作为支撑。

Conclusion: 两种看似不同的编程理论在实践中趋于一致,均强调对已有程序知识的复用;而两者所体现的显式、可执行的编程理论,在当今能力更强的大模型中已基本缺席。研究亦具反身性:使用现代 AI 工具完成对早期 AI 系统的复活,提示应将可解释的编程认知理论重新纳入 AI 编程助手的设计考量。

Abstract: Early AI programs were often also theories of how people think, and programs that wrote programs were, in part, theories of how people program. Here we report on the reanimation of two such programs that offer two seemingly different theories of programming and were developed for quite different purposes. The first, Herbert Simon's Heuristic Compiler (1961), was an experimental model of programming as problem-solving, applying the means-ends analysis methods of the General Problem Solver to investigate the nature of the programming task. The second, KBEmacs (1984), was a working prototype of an intelligent programming assistant developed by MIT's Programmer's Apprentice project, built on the view that expert programmers recognize and combine familiar patterns, or "cliches." It let a programmer build up programs by combining cliches through a stylized English command language. We approach both through the methodology of Executable Archaeology, the study of historical computational systems by running their original code, a process we call "reanimation." Our reanimation of the Heuristic Compiler reproduces the compiled code appearing in Simon's 1963 paper, and our reanimation of KBEmacs reproduces the SQRT sequence of its December 1984 self-test. Examined side by side, the two theories turn out to have much in common: the Heuristic Compiler also works by adapting existing routines, and Simon later argued that search alone requires a large body of programming knowledge. To highlight the reflexivity of this project, we deliberately used a modern AI-assisted programming tool, Anthropic's Claude Code (Opus 5.5), to carry out much of the detailed reanimation work, building on a significant base of existing software. The explicit, executable theories of programming that both systems embodied are largely absent from today's far more capable AI coding assistants.

</details>


### [228] [WAKMan: Workload Aware KVCache Manager](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36355)
*Suhas Kumar Morisetty,Vaibhavi Lokegaonkar,Sachin Mathew Varghese,Wesley Harris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WAKMan 是一种工作负载感知的 KV 缓存管理器，它根据实时流量动态选择淘汰策略，无需知道请求类型或读取 prompt，平均将命中率较 LRU 提升 5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存可复用 KV 状态以加速 prefill 并降低成本，但内存有限使淘汰策略成为决定收益的关键。生产流量混合了单轮、多轮、agentic 等请求类型，不同场景偏好不同策略（agentic 偏好 recency，热门 prompt 与长对话偏好 frequency），在 20 种混合下没有单一最优策略，LRU 与 S3FIFO 各有大幅优劣势，且单个工作负载参数即可改变胜者。

Method: 提出 WAKMan：在线从实时流量中推断并切换淘汰策略，不依赖请求类型标签，也不读取 prompt 内容。

Result: 相对 LRU 平均命中率提升 5 个百分点，在所有策略中取得最低的平均与最坏情况最优性差距（optimality gap）。

Conclusion: 工作负载感知的自适应淘汰策略优于任何静态单一策略，是多租户、混合流量 LLM 服务场景下提升前缀缓存收益的有效方案。

Abstract: Prefix caching enables a Large Language Model (LLM) servers to reuse the Key-Value (KV) states and accelerate prefill computation stage for requests that share a prefix with earlier prompts, thus reducing latency and serving cost. With limited memory resource, the KV cache eviction policy becomes a decisive factor in determining the overall benefits of prefix caching. Production traffic may have single-turn, multi-turn, agentic, or a mixture of any combination of these request types. Our findings indicate that agentic steps return within seconds and favor recency, while popular prompts and long conversations return after many other requests and favor frequency. Across 20 mixes, no single policy is always best: vLLM's default strategy LRU provides 12% higher cache hits as compared to the frequency-oriented S3FIFO on agentic-heavy mixes but trails it by up to 22% elsewhere, and even a single workload parameter can flip the winner even for any particular mix. We present WAKMan, a workload-aware KV cache manager that picks its eviction policy from live traffic without knowing request types or reading prompts. It improves hit rate over LRU by 5 percentage points on average, with the lowest mean and worst-case optimality gap of all policies.

</details>


### [229] [LoRA on a Budget: Function-Aware Post-Hoc Compression for Adapter Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36386)
*Shenghui Li,Thiemo voigt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出功能性秩失真前沿（Functional Rank Allocation, FRA）方法，用于在共享秩预算下压缩多个LoRA适配器群体，通过端到端测量构建前沿并做极小极大分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA适配器在服务时需在共享资源预算下压缩，本质是两层分配问题：层内秩分配与适配器间秩分配。谱冗余虽普遍存在，但仅靠谱重构无法确定适配器能容忍多少功能性压缩，因此需要基于功能性的压缩方法。

Method: 将群体压缩形式化为每个适配器的功能性秩失真前沿，再做群体级极小极大分配。FRA为无标签方法：端到端测量孤立模块截断，作为可加搜索代理并用动态规划求解，对每个完整候选重新测量，结合互补的谱提议取低风险包络，最后在前沿上做精确极小极大分配。

Result: 在来自三个公开群体的78个适配器上，FRA在受限预算情形下相较均匀谱压缩提升了最坏情况和尾部效用。固定预算探针表明孤立成本能较好地排序候选，但校准随提议形状漂移，端到端验证与谱提议限制了功能搜索误差。

Conclusion: 基于功能性秩失真前沿的两级分解在给定精确前沿时等价于全局联合优化；FRA作为实用近似，通过端到端验证与谱提议的包络在受限预算下取得更优的最坏情况与尾部效用。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has become a key mechanism for efficiently specializing foundation models into many task- and user-specific variants. Serving many LoRA adapters under a shared resource budget turns post-hoc compression into a two-level allocation problem: rank must be distributed across layers within each adapter and across adapters within the fleet. Although trained LoRAs contain substantial spectral redundancy, spectral reconstruction alone does not determine how much compression an adapter can functionally tolerate. We formulate fleet compression through a functional rank-distortion frontier for each adapter, followed by fleet-level minimax allocation under a shared rank budget. Given exact frontiers, this decomposition is equivalent to joint optimization over all layer ranks. We introduce Functional Rank Allocation (FRA), an unlabeled method for constructing practical frontiers. FRA measures isolated module truncations end to end, uses these measurements as an additive search surrogate solved by dynamic programming, remeasures every complete candidate, and takes the lower-risk envelope with a complementary spectral proposal before exact minimax allocation over the constructed frontiers. Across 78 adapters from three public fleets, FRA improves worst-case and tail utility over uniform spectral compression in constrained-budget regimes. Fixed-budget probes show that isolated costs rank candidates well, but their calibration shifts across proposal shapes; end-to-end verification and the spectral proposal therefore limit functional-search error.

</details>


### [230] [MAREval: A Multi-Agent Framework for Evaluating Natural Language Recommendation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36389)
*Reza Yousefi Maragheh,Jayesh Uddhav Kudase,Aysenur Inan,Ramin Giahi,Kai Zhao,Parth Parikh,Jianpeng Xu,Jason Cho,Evren Korpeoglu,Sushant Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAREval多智能体框架，用LLM自动评估推荐解释质量，兼顾可扩展性与人类对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐解释的评估对信任与透明至关重要，但人工评估成本高、难以规模化；单智能体LLM自动评估存在提示敏感、输出不一致的问题。

Method: MAREval编排四类组件：(i) 规划智能体，采用新型Chain of Debate (CoD)提示协调各智能体角色并保证评估计划逻辑一致；(ii) 主持智能体，通过缓解提示漂移规范讨论；(iii) 仲裁智能体，聚合多轮评估输出；(iv) 蒙特卡洛采样，提升鲁棒性与人类判断对齐度。

Result: 在公开数据集TopicalChat与专有电商数据上，MAREval优于强单智能体与多智能体基线，人类判断对齐度更高；重复试验方差显著更低。大规模人工标注关卡中达到生产质量阈值（既往评估器未达标），在线A/B测试显示互动与收入指标有统计显著提升。

Conclusion: MAREval可作为真实系统中可扩展、可靠且与人类对齐的推荐解释评估方案。

Abstract: Evaluating natural language explanations in recommender systems is essential for fostering user trust, transparency, and engagement. However, existing evaluation approaches like human evaluations, while accurate, are resource-intensive and impractical at the scale required by modern recommendation platforms. Also, automated methods using single-agent LLMs suffer from prompt sensitivity and inconsistent outputs. To address these challenges, we propose MAREval, a structured multi-agent framework for evaluating recommendation explanations using large language models. MAREval orchestrates (i) a planner agent that uses a novel Chain of Debate (CoD) prompting strategy to coordinate agent roles and enforce logically consistent evaluation plans; (ii) a moderator agent that regulates discussions by mitigating prompt drift; and (iii) an arbitrator agent that aggregates outputs from multiple evaluation rounds and (iv) a Monte Carlo sampling method, improving robustness and alignment with human judgment. We conduct comprehensive evaluations on both public (TopicalChat) and proprietary recommendation datasets, demonstrating that MAREval outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Comprehensive experiments on a public benchmark and a proprietary e-commerce dataset show that MAREval improves alignment with human judgments over strong singleand multi-agent baselines. Stability analyses indicate substantially lower variability across repeated trials. In a large human-annotation gate, MAREval meets production quality thresholds where prior evaluators fall short, and online A/B testing demonstrates statistically significant improvements in engagement and revenue metrics. These results establish MAREval as a scalable and reliable solution for human-aligned evaluation of recommendation explanations in real-world systems

</details>


### [231] [kw-bench: Breaking Frontier Harnesses on Real-World Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36405)
*George Juraj Salapa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 kw-bench：首个面向"调查型知识工作"的确定性评分基准，以跨三代 SAP 的五年 VAT 重建为任务家族；实验表明模型能力不足时，harness（外壳/运行框架）而非模型决定上限，前沿模型上任务则迅速饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 coding-agent 基准多为"生成式"（按简要产出财务模型、备忘、表格，由 rubric 或 LLM 评判），缺少对知识工作中真正昂贵的一环——调查取证（审计、税务、尽调、监管报送、理赔）——的检验。此类任务答案唯一存在，但隐藏于跨系统的不完整、不一致证据中，需精确还原并逐分自证。

Method: 从专家叙述的真实项目构建 kw-bench，首个任务族为跨三代 SAP（legacy ECC、部分迁移的 S/4HANA、顾问重构）的五年 VAT 重建；输入为原始系统导出（页眉、本地数字格式、部分关联表、无连接文档）。评分完全确定性，不使用 LLM judge。实验：同一模型 DeepSeek v4.1 Flash 上跑四个 harness（Claude Code、Codex、OpenCode、micro-cc），限时两小时；再以 Claude Opus 5.5 测试 micro-cc 与 Claude Code。

Result: DeepSeek v4.1 Flash：两个模型无关的独立 harness 有效（OpenCode 0.542、micro-cc 0.528），两个实验室自研 harness 脱离自家模型后得 0.00。失败原因归为"惯性"——需在长会话中维持大量弱信号。换成前沿模型 Claude Opus 5.5 后任务坍缩：micro-cc 0.9986（22 分钟）、Claude Code 1.000（25 分钟）。

Conclusion: 原本需数周的咨询项目可在数分钟内、以数美元成本完成。结论：低于前沿水平时，harness 决定模型能走多远；达到前沿后，模型能力使 harness 差异消失。基准填补了调查型知识工作评测的空白，并强调确定性评分的可复现性。

Abstract: Each new generation of frontier model pushes the edge of what is possible. Inside a harness -- a loop, a shell, a filesystem, the ability to write and run its own code -- a model can take on increasingly difficult work.
  A growing body of work treats the coding-agent harness as infrastructure for knowledge work rather than a coding tool, and a new wave of benchmarks tests it there. Most of them are generative: build a financial model, a memo, a spreadsheet from a brief, judged by a rubric or by another model. Few test the expensive part of knowledge work, which is investigative. In audit, tax, due diligence, regulatory reporting, claims or any dispute over numbers, the answer already exists -- hidden in incomplete and inconsistent evidence spread across systems -- and has to be recovered and defended to the cent.
  We introduce kw-bench, a benchmark built from real engagements specialists described to us. Its first task family is a five-year VAT reconstruction across three generations of SAP -- legacy ECC, a partial S/4HANA migration and a consultant's redesign -- delivered as raw system extracts: page headers, local number formats, partial link tables, no documentation of how anything connects. Grading is deterministic, with no LLM judge.
  We ran four harnesses -- Claude Code, Codex, OpenCode and micro-cc -- on the same model, DeepSeek v4.1 Flash, for two hours. The two independent, model-agnostic harnesses delivered (OpenCode 0.542, micro-cc 0.528); the two lab harnesses, away from their home model, delivered nothing (0.00 each). What defeated them was inertia: keeping many weak signals alive across a long session. On a frontier model the task collapses. micro-cc and Claude Code on Claude Opus 5.5 scored 0.9986 and 1.000, in 22 and 25 minutes. A weeks-long engagement now costs minutes and a few dollars; below the frontier, the harness decides how far a model gets.

</details>


### [232] [Verifiable Efficiency Limits of Innate LLM Planning in Sliding Puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36415)
*Yezhou Cheng,Charles Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可验证的SPE框架（SPE=R×Q×E），在无工具、无执行反馈条件下隔离模型的"内生规划能力"，用4×4滑块拼图评测并量化长时序规划局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具辅助的规划成功可能源于外部搜索与验证，掩盖了模型自身的规划能力，因此需要在不使用工具、不获取执行反馈的条件下单独衡量模型的内生（unaided）规划能力。

Method: 让模型在单次回复中生成完整规划（无工具、无执行反馈），将成功加权的规划效率分解为 SPE=R×Q×E：执行可靠性R、合法规划间的规划协调性Q、已完成规划的动作经济性E；通过轨迹重放与经认证的最优解验证各因子，联合得分计入未成功尝试。评估7种模型条件在4×4滑块拼图上的8,400份完整规划输出，主要队列严格控制步长与障碍数量。

Result: 即使完成全部试验的模型，在更长时序下经济性仍下降；完成规划中的高效率可与低目标达成率并存。SPE可区分整体能力层级，其因子可区分"协调失败"与"低效完成"。

Conclusion: 该框架使无辅助规划中的长时序局限可被定量、可验证地刻画。

Abstract: Tool-assisted planning success can reflect external search and verification, leaving a model's unaided planning ability unresolved. We isolate innate planning through complete plans generated in one response without tools or execution feedback. We propose a verifiable framework that decomposes success-weighted plan efficiency as $\mathrm{SPE}=R\times Q\times E$: execution reliability, planning coordination among legal plans, and action economy among completed plans. Trajectory replay and certified optima verify these factors; the joint score includes unsuccessful attempts. We evaluate 8,400 full-plan outputs from seven model conditions on $4\times4$ sliding puzzles, controlling exact horizon and obstacle count in the primary cohort. Models that complete every trial still become less economical at longer horizons, while higher efficiency among completed plans can coexist with poor goal attainment. SPE separates the overall capability hierarchy, while its factors distinguish coordination failure from inefficient completion. The framework makes long-horizon limitations in unaided planning quantitatively verifiable.

</details>


### [233] [LM-DSB: A Lightweight Multi-agent System for Automated Daily Safety Bulletin Generation in Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36433)
*Xianxiang Zhao,Advik Mehta,Falak Sethi,Brian Gue,Qipei Mei,Lingzi Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LM-DSB轻量级多智能体系统，结合RAG与本地可部署LLM（Llama 3-8B），在消费级PC上自动生成施工每日安全简报（DSB），两阶段验证显示其生成质量与专家可接受度均优于单智能体方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 施工安全管理中DSB（每日安全简报）依赖人工编写，每日耗时2-3小时且质量不稳定，约25%班组从不开展作业前规划，安全人员精力被文书工作占用而无法投入现场监督。

Method: 构建分层多智能体架构（planner、researcher、writer三类专业智能体），结合施工安全文档的领域微调与37份HSE文档的RAG检索增强；模型采用本地可部署的Llama 3-8B以适配消费级硬件。验证分两阶段：阶段1为多配置对比评测（TF-IDF、BLEU、ROUGE、长度比），阶段2为N=40的专家评分。

Result: 阶段1中多智能体RAG配置全面优于其他配置：TF-IDF 0.70、BLEU 0.39、ROUGE-1 0.66，长度比1.01（单智能体RAG仅0.39），文档生成近完整；阶段2专家评分均值3.50-4.20（5分制），法规合规性4.20、事实准确性4.10最为突出。

Conclusion: 合理的架构设计可使轻量级模型在消费级硬件上胜任安全关键型文档生成，推动施工安全管理的技术民主化。局限包括知识库覆盖范围、语言限制及缺乏现场部署验证，后续应开展多模态集成与工作流落地研究。
