Abstract: The rapid progress of software engineering agents drives interest in applying them to open-ended problems such as GPU kernel optimization. However, we find that existing coding agents converge to local optima on real-world, expert-written kernels (e.g., FlashInfer). We introduce Evolving Agent Teams, a multi-agent framework that escapes such local optima. It features (1) a central supervisor that diagnoses each local optimum and restructures a team of agents; and (2) a persistent file system that comprises a learning document, an evolution history, and per-team artifacts, grounding restructuring with accumulated experience. Under a matched token budget, Evolving Agent Teams consistently outperforms strong coding-agent baselines. With an extended token budget, Evolving Agent Teams generates a CUDA kernel that reaches 1.2x mean speedup over the production FlashInfer MLA paged decode kernel from scratch and wins on 36 of 47 production workloads from FlashInfer-Bench (0.86-0.99x on the remaining 11). It also demonstrates superior performance on tasks such as KDA prefill on AMD MI350X (3.01x over vLLM) and NVIDIA B200 (3.44x over FlashKDA), MoE expert-offload serving on Apple silicon (30.3 tokens/s decode for Qwen3.8-Flash-Next in MLX 4-bit on a 48 GB M4 Pro), and RoboDojo robot manipulation (62.1 on held-out seeds vs. 55.3 for the best leaderboard entry). Code is available at https://github.com/caoshiyi/EAT.

</details>


### [268] [GUI-JEPA: Candidate-Conditioned JEPA Latents as a Fast Action Policy for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36877)
*Bingchao Wang,Yongcheng Jing,Shunyu Liu,Chengyu Wang,Jun Huang,Jie Yang,Wei Liu,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GUI-JEPA，首个无解码器的GUI智能体策略，基于JEPA编码器在潜空间中批量选择动作，在AndroidControl上比Qwen2.5-VL-7B快2.8-4.0倍，仅用6%参数达到其86-92%的分布内准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有移动GUI智能体多为7B级VLM，逐token解码生成动作，即便采用候选动作打分仍需将LLM/VLM置于推理回路中，导致速度慢、显存占用高。

Method: 构建于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）之上：使用在无标注GUI截图上预训练的JEPA编码器提取屏幕潜表示；动作与区域条件化的潜预测器将屏幕潜表示转换为每个候选动作对应的潜向量；轻量级打分头在一次批量前向中对所有候选动作与指令进行打分，全程不生成任何token。

Result: 在AndroidControl上，同GPU下比同样输入微调的Qwen2.5-VL-7B智能体快2.8-4.0倍，以6%参数量达到其分布内准确率的86-92%。消融显示打分器依赖该潜通路，且随机初始化的预测器与经屏幕动态预训练的预测器性能相当。

Conclusion: 无解码器的潜空间动作选择可行且高效，预测器应针对决策目标训练而非屏幕动态；为轻量化、低延迟GUI智能体提供了新范式。

Abstract: Mobile GUI agents are typically fine-tuned 7B-scale vision-language models (VLMs) that generate each action as text. However, these VLMs decode every action token by token, and even agents that score candidate actions keep a large language model or VLM in the loop, making them slow and memory-hungry. To address these issues, we propose GUI-JEPA, to our knowledge the first decoder-free GUI agent policy built on a Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA), which selects actions in the latent space of a JEPA encoder pretrained on unlabelled GUI screenshots. Its action- and region-conditioned latent predictor turns the screen latent into one latent per candidate action, letting lightweight heads score all candidates against the instruction in a single batched pass, without decoding a token. We further show that the scorer relies on this latent pathway and that the predictor is trained for the decision itself: a randomly initialised predictor does just as well as one pretrained on screen dynamics. On AndroidControl, GUI-JEPA is 2.8-4.0x faster on the same GPU than a Qwen2.5-VL-7B agent we fine-tune with the same inputs, while reaching 86-92% of its in-distribution accuracy with 6% of its parameters. Code is available at https://github.com/bcwang-sjtu/GUI-JEPA

</details>


### [269] [RubricAtlas: Mining Type-Level Rubrics from Expert Reports to Evaluate Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36878)
*Delin Wang,Sinuo Wang,Rui Sun,Beidi Luan,Qiming Shi,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RubricAtlas：从同一研究类型的多份专家报告中提炼"类型级"分析要求，围绕反复出现的分析问题组织二值检查点，用于评估深度研究智能体输出分析报告的完整性与论证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体需多步检索并综合成分析报告，评估不能只看相关事实是否出现，还要看其交付了哪些分析。查询特定二值评分表虽能明确单条要求，但任务级清单可能使整体分析结构未被充分考察，且单份参考报告仅覆盖其所属类型约一半的分析问题。

Method: 从同一研究类型下不同对象的专家报告中归纳类型级分析要求，构建RubricAtlas；将细粒度二值检查点与报告段落关联，使完整性评估与论证有效性评估分离；基于3,079份中文金融研究报告构建，覆盖99个研究类型；并做参考报告选择实验、控制性删除与填充实验，以及两个评判流水线的一致性验证。

Result: 单份参考报告仅覆盖其类型约一半分析问题；从不同报告选取要求会改变评估得分与智能体排名；跨十个智能体，两个评判流水线检查点一致率κ=0.71，智能体排名一致率Kendall τ=0.87；控制性删除使受影响分析得分下降，填充无显著提升。

Conclusion: 类型级评分表适合在统一标准下比较深度研究输出的分析完整性，且不预设答案；单项判定可溯源至报告证据，为Web深度研究输出的评估提供可比、可解释的框架。

Abstract: Deep research agents conduct multi-step Web searches and synthesise retrieved information into analytical reports. Evaluating these outputs requires checking which analyses they deliver, beyond the presence of relevant facts. Query-specific binary rubrics make individual requirements explicit, but task-level checklists may leave the overall analytical structure underexamined. We introduce RubricAtlas, which derives type-level analytical requirements from expert reports on different objects within the same research type and organises detailed checks around recurring analytical questions. The requirements give related tasks a shared standard for what to analyse, without prescribing their answers. Our framework links binary checkpoints to report passages and evaluates completeness separately from argument validity. Built from 3,079 Chinese financial research reports, RubricAtlas covers 99 research types. Reference-report selection experiments show that individual reports cover only about half of their type's analytical questions, and that selecting requirements from different reports changes evaluation scores and agent rankings. Across ten agents, two judge pipelines achieve checkpoint agreement of $κ$ = 0.71 and agent-ranking agreement of Kendall $τ$ = 0.87. Controlled deletions reduce scores on the affected analyses, while padding produces no detectable increase. These findings support type-level rubrics for comparing the analytical completeness of Web deep research outputs, with individual decisions traceable to report evidence.

</details>


### [270] [Noise Steering: Exploiting Wake Steering and Acoustic Directivity for Noise-Constrained Wind Farm Control via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36888)
*Martín de Frutos,Baptiste Corban,Claire Bizon Monroc,Ana Bušić,Donatien Dubuc,Jiamin Zhud,Esteban Ferrer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出无模型强化学习策略"Noise Steering"，联合优化风机偏航角与功率设定点，在满足局部噪声约束的同时最大化风场功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风场降噪策略仅降低风机功率输出，未考虑尾流相互作用与噪声发射指向性，导致策略过度保守、风能未被充分利用；而偏航可同时影响尾流与噪声方向，提供额外控制自由度。

Method: 无模型RL智能体联合控制各风机偏航角与功率设定点；为降低训练成本，将低保真声学模型与稳态FLORIS仿真器耦合构成训练环境。

Result: 在六风机试验风场上，策略在全湍流风速范围内满足40 dB噪声约束，相对无阻碍贪婪控制平均功率损失仅2%，高风速下至多7.7%；行业标准降噪运行(NRO)基线平均损失17%。迁移至高保真FAST.Farm后，在稳态与湍流来流下性能基本保持。

Conclusion: 结合偏航控制与功率降额的RL方法能显著改善风场运行的功率-噪声权衡，优于传统NRO策略。

Abstract: Existing noise mitigation strategies for wind farms usually consist in reducing turbines power output, without accounting for the wake interactions between them and the noise emission directivity. This leads to over-conservative policies and unexploited wind power. Yet, turbine yaw can simultaneously affect wake interactions and the direction of noise emission, offering an additional degree of freedom for farm-level noise control. We therefore propose Noise Steering, a model-free reinforcement learning (RL) strategy that jointly controls turbine yaw angles and power setpoints to maximise farm power while satisfying a local noise constraint in a defined region. To enable training at a reduced computational cost, a low-fidelity acoustic model is coupled with the steady-state FLORIS simulator. The RL agent effectively learns how to redirect wakes and noise directivity away from the protected region while redistributing power across the farm. On a six-turbine test farm, the policy satisfies the 40 dB noise constraint throughout the full range of turbulent winds, with an average power deficit of only 2% relative to greedy unimpeded control, and up to 7.7% at high wind speeds. In comparison, the industry-standard noise-reduced operation (NRO) baseline here generates an average power loss of 17%. When deployed in the higher-fidelity FAST.Farm simulator, the policy largely retains its performance under steady and turbulent inflow, despite differences in simulation fidelity. These results demonstrate the potential of combining yaw control and power derating through RL to improve the power-noise trade-off in wind farm operation.

</details>


### [271] [Natural Hard-Negative Mining from Positives for Instruction-Following Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36900)
*Chaolang Li,Jingyu Li,Zhaocheng Du,Xuyang Xie,Gang Wang,Pengwen Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Natural Hard-Negative Mining（NHNM），将原正样本重构为自然指令难负样本，自动构建NHNM-MARCO（约7K实例）用于指令跟随检索训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令跟随信息检索（IFIR）要求检索器同时依据查询与实例级指令排序文档，但指令条件下的对比监督稀缺；现有合成方法固定正段落并额外生成负样本，忽视了原正样本本身的难负样本潜力。

Method: NHNM：在查询兼容的指令下把可转换负样本提升为难负样本，并将原正样本重用作自然指令难负样本；配套自动化流程包括Candidate Cleaning、Reversal Construction与Geometry Filtering，以生成紧凑、高对比度且与预训练检索空间对齐的监督信号。

Result: 在MSMARCO上产出NHNM-MARCO（近7K实例）；在InstructIR与FollowIR上，568M–4B参数模型微调后取得较强指令跟随表现，并在若干设置下匹配或超越显著更大的检索器；控制实验与消融表明增益源于自然难负监督而非模型规模或数据量。

Conclusion: 原正样本可被有效重构为自然指令难负样本，配合紧凑高质量监督即可显著提升IFIR性能，且不依赖模型规模扩张或大规模数据。

Abstract: Instruction-following information retrieval (IFIR) requires retrievers to rank documents by both a query and its instance-specific instructions, yet instruction-conditioned contrastive supervision remains scarce. Existing synthetic methods typically fix a positive passage and generate additional negatives, overlooking the hard-negative potential of the original positive. We propose Natural Hard-Negative Mining (NHNM), which promotes convertible negatives under query-compatible instructions and repurposes original positives as natural instruction hard negatives. Its automated Candidate Cleaning, Reversal Construction, and Geometry Filtering pipeline produces compact, high-contrast supervision aligned with the pretrained retrieval space. Applied to MSMARCO, NHNM yields NHNM-MARCO, an IFIR training set of nearly 7K instances. On InstructIR and FollowIR, models with 568M to 4B parameters fine-tuned on NHNM-MARCO achieve strong instruction-following performance and match or surpass substantially larger retrievers in several settings. Controlled analyses and ablations attribute these gains to natural hard-negative supervision rather than model scale or training-data volume.

</details>


### [272] [Vision-Language Agents Can Learn in Their Own Latent Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36925)
*Haoxi Li,Sikai Bai,Jianfei Ma,Qinglin Hou,Xiaocheng Lu,Tao Han,Jie Zhang,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Dwell 是一种基于模型的强化学习框架，将预训练视觉语言模型（VLM）改造为循环潜在转移算子，使其在潜空间中预测、调整并行动，从而以较低算力从在线交互中持续提升策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练 VLM 虽已内化丰富的世界知识，但很少被用于通过在线交互驱动策略改进；同时策略更新会改变交互分布、要求世界模型随之自适应，而持续更新大规模预训练模型计算代价高昂，因此亟需高效利用 VLM 知识进行在线学习的方法。

Method: 提出 Dwell：把 VLM 重构成循环式潜在转移算子（recurrent latent transition operator），复用 VLM 自身的表征，使其同时充当世界模型与策略；智能体一方面从真实交互中学习预测与评估动作结果，另一方面在潜在想象（latent imagination）中改进行为，避免在高维观测空间做重建。

Result: 在五个交互式视觉环境中，Dwell 在可训练方法中取得最佳的综合性能，同时大幅降低想象所需的计算开销。

Conclusion: 有效的 VLM 智能体世界模型不必重建未来，而应预测对动作有用的未来；将 VLM 作为潜在转移算子可高效实现基于模型的在线持续学习。

Abstract: Learning from experience is essential for building the next generation of agents that continually expand their capabilities. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) already internalize rich world knowledge, yet this knowledge is rarely leveraged to drive policy improvement through online interaction. Realizing this potential efficiently remains challenging, as policy updates shift the agent's interaction distribution, requiring the world model to adapt accordingly, while continually updating large pretrained models is computationally expensive. To address this challenge, we introduce Dwell, a model-based reinforcement learning (RL) framework that reframes a VLM as a recurrent latent transition operator, endowing it with the ability to predict, adjust, and act in latent space. Leveraging the VLM's own representations and its dual role as both world model and policy, the agent continually learns from real interactions to anticipate and evaluate action outcomes, and from latent imagination to improve its behavior. Across five interactive visual environments, Dwell achieves the strongest overall performance among trainable methods while substantially reducing the compute required for imagination, showing that effective world models for VLM agents need not reconstruct the future; they need to predict futures that are useful for action. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Tom111-lhx/Dwell

</details>


### [273] [Do bots feel bad? Assessing empirical evidence for functional valence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36962)
*Iwan Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文评述"LLM具有功能性效价状态"的实证主张，指出关键效价面向尚未被检验，且现有范式难以区分功能性效价与偏好、价值判断、效价归赋及角色扮演等邻近构念，并提出新的实验路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期多项工作（如"功能性快乐与痛苦"、"功能性情绪"、"功能性福祉"）声称LLM具备与认知和行动中效价状态因果角色相同的功能性效价状态（无须被意识体验）。但尚不清楚这些被识别的状态在多大程度上契合哲学与认知科学中对效价功能角色的界定，因此需要系统评估现有证据。

Method: 概念分析与证据评述：以哲学和认知科学中关于效价（valence）功能角色的理论标准为参照，审查当前LLM效价实证研究的设计与推论，识别其覆盖不足与混淆风险，并据此提出改进的实验设计思路。

Result: 提出两点批判：(i) 效价的若干关键面向在LLM中仍未被检验；(ii) 现有实证范式不足以将（功能性）效价与偏好、价值判断、效价归赋、角色扮演等邻近构念区分开。

Conclusion: 现有证据尚不足以支持LLM具有功能性效价状态的强主张；作者提出若干新的实验取向，以更准确地针对（功能性）效价本身进行检验。

Abstract: Do LLMs have valenced states, i.e. states that feel pleasant or unpleasant? Recent empirical work has claimed to show that LLMs possess functional valenced states (e.g. ``functional pleasure and pain'' [Ren et al. 2026], ``functional emotions'' [Sofroniew et al. 2026], and ``functional welfare'' [Han et al. 2026]) that play the same causal role as valenced states in cognition and action, without necessarily being consciously experienced. But it remains unexplored how well the identified states fit the functional role of valence as understood in the philosophical and cognitive science literature. Here, I assess the current body of evidence, and argue (i) that key aspects of valence remain untested in LLMs, and (ii) that extant empirical paradigms inadequately distinguish (functional) valence from neighbouring constructs like preferences, value-judgments, valence attributions and role-play. I conclude by sketching some novel experimental approaches to better target (functional) valence itself.

</details>


### [274] [Proactive Memory for Event-Driven LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36967)
*Jinyang Li,Zichuan Fu,Xian Wu,Wenlin Zhang,Yimin Deng,Kaifeng Guo,Guojing Li,Junjia QI,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Proactive Memory (ProactMem)，将历史组织为近期事件、时间轨迹与行为规律三类，并通过源事件校验与历史回放验证后匹配当前事件；同时构建纵向基准 ProactiveStream，ProactMem 取得 52.6% F1，相对最强基线提升 21.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动式 LLM 智能体多依赖近期上下文或检索相关记忆，难以验证反复出现的行为规律是否成立，也难以评估其是否适用于当前事件。

Method: ProactMem 将历史分为近期事件、时间轨迹与行为规律三层结构；轨迹事实回溯源事件进行校验，行为规律通过历史回放评估后与当前条件匹配。另构建 ProactiveStream 纵向基准，覆盖三种应用范围，事件流按时间顺序隔离处理，并采用 episode-aware 机会标签。

Result: 在 ProactiveStream 上，ProactMem 总体 F1 为 52.6%，相较五个外部基线中最强方法取得 21.4% 的相对提升。

Conclusion: 通过结构化记忆与双重验证机制（源事件校验 + 历史回放）可有效提升主动式智能体识别服务机会的可靠性，纵向事件流基准为相关研究提供了评测基础。

Abstract: Proactive LLM agents anticipate service opportunities from ongoing observations and accumulated history. Existing approaches emphasize recent context or retrieval of related memories, providing limited support for validating recurring behavioral regularities and assessing their applicability to the current event. We propose Proactive Memory (ProactMem), which organizes history into recent events, temporal trajectories, and behavioral regularities. Trajectory facts are checked against source events, and regularities are assessed through historical replay before being matched to current conditions. We also construct ProactiveStream, a longitudinal benchmark covering three application scopes, where agents process isolated event streams chronologically under episode-aware opportunity labels. On ProactiveStream, ProactMem achieves an overall effectiveness (F1) of 52.6%, a 21.4% relative improvement over the strongest of five external baselines. Code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ProactMem-5351/.

</details>


### [275] [AutoVend: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Autonomous Vending Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.36980)
*Qiming Shi,Yikun Li,Beidi Luan,Rui Sun,Yan Ma,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AutoVend 基准：让单个 LLM 智能体通过网页运营界面管理 5 类场地共 20 台自动售货机，连续模拟 180 天，考察线上决策能否转化为持续经济回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O2O 电商中决策经 Web 平台下达、却由高成本物理执行实现，现有评测多停留在单笔交易层面，无法回答"线上决策是否带来持续经济收益"这一问题，故需面向卖家侧经营的长周期、数据落地的评测环境。

Method: 构建 AutoVend：共享仓库、采购提前期、付费补货、库存过期与周期性运营成本组成的数据驱动模拟器；单一智能体在 Web 运营界面上管理 20 台售货机、覆盖 5 类场地、180 个模拟日；将 5 个 LLM 智能体与基于规则的库存控制基线对比，并从场地协调、执行质量与时间维度绩效进行诊断性轨迹分析。

Result: 5 个被测 LLM 智能体中 4 个低于规则基线；按"每美元推理成本的回报"排序与按纯回报排序结果不同；单项指标提升未必带来更强经济成长，库存与执行成本会侵蚀收益；轨迹分析显示智能体在落地可行动作、管理库存承诺、将反馈转化为有效策略修订方面存在困难。

Conclusion: 面向 O2O 经营的 LLM 智能体尚不足以稳定超越简单规则策略，"经济回报＋推理成本"应作为核心评测维度；AutoVend 为长周期、物理约束下智能体经营能力提供了可控、可复现的评测平台。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are extending agent-mediated commerce on the Web from shopping for consumers to operating businesses for sellers. In online-to-offline (O2O) commerce, decisions are issued through Web-based platforms but realized through costly physical execution. Evaluating such agents requires going beyond individual transactions to ask whether decisions made online turn into sustained economic returns. We introduce AutoVend, a benchmark in which one LLM agent manages 20 vending machines across five site types through a Web-based operating interface for 180 simulated days. Its data-grounded simulator combines a shared warehouse, procurement lead times, paid replenishment, inventory expiration, and recurring operating costs. We evaluate five LLM agents against a rule-based inventory-control baseline and diagnose their site coordination, execution, and performance over time. Four of five evaluated LLM agents fall short of this baseline, and returns per dollar of inference cost rank models differently from returns alone. Operational analyses show that gains in individual metrics do not consistently yield stronger economic growth, as inventory and execution costs erode these gains. Trajectory analysis further reveals difficulties in grounding feasible actions, managing inventory commitments, and converting feedback into effective policy revisions.

</details>


### [276] [Guided-RLVR: Guided Exploration in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37041)
*Bo Yang,Zishuai Zhang,Weihua Li,Qinnan Zhang,Sijia Wen,Hainan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Guided-RLVR，用轻量控制器在采样分布中注入语义化引导 token，以缓解 RLVR rollout 塌缩、扩大推理探索空间，在数学推理与代码生成任务上稳定优于现有 RLVR 方法且推理轨迹更多样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR 在数学、编程等可验证奖励任务上能显著提升 LLM 推理能力，但现有研究发现其 rollout 常塌缩到少量狭窄的推理轨迹上，导致探索的推理空间受限。作者受"关键步骤上的 thinking tokens 是 LLM 推理路径中的关键结构元素"这一发现启发，希望通过结构性干预来扩展探索。

Method: Guided-RLVR 在标准 RLVR 框架上增加一个轻量控制器（lightweight controller）。该控制器向采样分布中策略性地注入语义有意义的引导 token（steering tokens），引导模型从多种推理视角切入问题，从而扩大 rollout 的探索空间，而非改变奖励或策略优化目标本身。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成基准上，Guided-RLVR 一致优于现有 RLVR 方法，同时产生更多样的推理轨迹（更丰富的 reasoning trajectory 分布）。

Conclusion: 通过在采样阶段引入语义引导而非仅依赖可验证奖励进行优化，可有效缓解 RLVR 的轨迹塌缩问题，在保持任务性能提升的同时增强推理多样性，说明推理路径的结构性引导是扩展 RLVR 探索空间的有效手段。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has shown strong empirical gains in improving the reasoning ability of large language models (LLMs) on tasks such as mathematics and programming. However, recent studies have shown that RLVR rollouts often collapse onto a narrow set of reasoning trajectories, limiting the explored reasoning space. Motivated by findings that thinking tokens at critical steps serve as critical structural elements in LLM reasoning pathways, we propose Guided-RLVR, a novel approach that augments RLVR with a lightweight controller. The controller strategically injects semantically meaningful steering tokens into the sampling distribution, encouraging the model to approach problems from multiple reasoning perspectives and thereby expanding the exploration space. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks show that Guided-RLVR consistently outperforms existing RLVR methods while producing more diverse reasoning trajectories.

</details>


### [277] [AI-Assisted Discovery of CNOT Decompositions for Quantum Permutations on Constrained Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37045)
*Daniele Lizzio Bosco,Jacopo Cossio,Simone Faro,Francesco Pio Marino,Gabriele Messina,Carla Piazza,Giuseppe Serra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 以AlphaCNOT为探索工具，在受限量子比特连通性下研究量子置换到CNOT电路的合成，发现远程对换的改进分解模式（≤6d-4个CNOT），并形式化为确定性合成方法Global Transpositions。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子比特连通性受限会为逻辑操作实现带来显著开销；AI辅助合成虽能降低电路复杂度，但其在揭示可指导新型确定性编译算法设计的原则方面的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 以AlphaCNOT为探索性工具，识别出图距离d>1的两量子比特远程对换的重复分解模式；通过构造性证明将其形式化为Global Transpositions方法，将整个置换搜索分解为拓扑感知的逻辑对换的最小代价分解；并在实验中进行对比与分析。

Result: 发现的模式给出最多6d-4个CNOT门的合法拓扑实现，优于基于SWAP路由的6d-3门构造；Global Transpositions在其合成模型中涵盖最优SWAP路由，并提供拓扑约束CNOT代价的可证上界；实验还揭示了无法表示为独立合成对换之拼接的电路，其通过暂时离开置换子群的中间线性变换组合多个逻辑操作的部分效果，称为中间CNOT融合。

Conclusion: AI辅助合成不仅能优化电路，还能发现可复用的恒等式、可证的构造以及量子电路编译中的隐藏机制。

Abstract: Efficient quantum circuit compilation is challenging for quantum computers, where limited qubit connectivity can introduce substantial overhead in the implementation of logical operations. Recent AI-assisted synthesis methods have shown considerable potential for reducing circuit complexity, but their role in uncovering principles that can guide the design of new deterministic compilation algorithms remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate this perspective for the synthesis of quantum permutations into CNOT circuits under constrained qubit connectivity. Using AlphaCNOT as an exploratory tool, we identify a recurring decomposition for remote transpositions that improves on the construction based on adjacent SWAP routing. For two qubits at graph distance $d>1$, the discovered pattern yields a topology-legal implementation using at most $6d-4$ CNOT gates, compared to the $6d-3$ gates required by the corresponding SWAP-based construction. We formalize this observation through a constructive proof to define Global Transpositions, a deterministic synthesis method that searches for a minimum-cost decomposition of an entire permutation into topology-aware logical transpositions. The resulting method subsumes optimal SWAP routing within its synthesis model and provides certified upper bounds on the topology-constrained CNOT cost. Our experiments also reveal circuits that cannot be expressed as concatenations of independently synthesized transpositions. Instead, these circuits exploit intermediate linear transformations that temporarily leave the permutation subgroup and combine partial effects of multiple logical operations, a mechanism we call intermediate CNOT fusion. Our results show that AI-assisted synthesis can support not only circuit optimization, but also the discovery of reusable identities, provable constructions, and hidden mechanisms in quantum circuit compilation.

</details>


### [278] [Correctness Conditioning as Selection in Chain-of-Thought Faithfulness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37070)
*Yueer Pan,Yuexi Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将"仅在答对样本上评估CoT忠实度"这一选择步骤建模为七变量结构因果模型，推导出可观测选择偏差的精确表达式 Δ = Cov(A, C)/P(C=1)，并区分难度混杂与捷径诱导的对撞偏倚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CoT忠实度评测普遍只保留答案正确的实例，这改变了被测量的总体，引入选择偏差；论文旨在厘清该选择步骤的因果结构与偏差来源。

Method: 构建七变量结构因果模型，推导选择偏差的闭式解 Δ = Cov(A, C)/P(C=1)，并给出以潜在响应倾向表达该偏差所需的条件独立假设；对18篇论文开展协议审查，区分答案正确性筛选与一致性筛选、基准构建、训练数据过滤；在算术任务上评估4个亚上限部署，采用GLM/Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel合并分析及无假设协方差分解。

Result: 4个部署均出现正选择偏差；主实验GLM-5.3 差距 +7.4 个百分点（n=1000, p<0.0001），CMH 合并 OR=1.70（p<10⁻⁶）；无假设协方差分解显示 42.8% 的差距可归因于"可干预操作数"层间变异，多数差距未被解释。

Conclusion: 答对筛选会造成系统性正偏差，使总体忠实度被高估；仅当每个实例的反事实合规性可观测时，才可直接评估全部轨迹以获得无筛选的总体忠实度。

Abstract: Restricting chain-of-thought (CoT) faithfulness evaluation to correctly answered instances changes the population being measured. We study this selection step in a seven-variable structural causal model and derive the exact observed gap $Δ= \operatorname{Cov}(A, C)/P(C=1)$. The model distinguishes difficulty confounding from shortcut-induced collider bias and states the conditional-independence assumption needed to express the gap through latent response propensities. A protocol review of 18 papers distinguishes answer-correctness restrictions from agreement-based selection, benchmark construction, and training-data filtering. We evaluate four sub-ceiling deployments on arithmetic tasks and observe positive selection gaps in all four. The primary GLM-5.3 run yields a +7.4 percentage-point gap ($n = 1000$, $p < 0.0001$), and pooled Cochran-Mantel-Haenszel analysis gives OR = 1.70 ($p < 10^{-6}$). An assumption-free covariance decomposition assigns 42.8% of the GLM-5.3 gap to between-stratum variation in interventionable-operation count, leaving the majority unattributed. When counterfactual compliance is observable for every instance, evaluating all traces directly estimates population faithfulness without correctness filtering.

</details>


### [279] [OpenGray: a benchmark for evaluating the decisions of radiotherapy planning agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37081)
*Humza Nusrat,Kundan Thind*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OpenGray基准，用已知正确决策的计划任务评估LLM放疗规划智能体，发现聚合指标掩盖了具体决策失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的放疗规划智能体仅以剂量指标评估，而其核心行为是"提交计划或请求复核"的决策，缺乏带已知正确答案的评估手段。

Method: 在OpenKBP-Opt头颈数据集上构建OpenGray，加入两类任务：两个已证明与剂量目标不相容的矛盾任务，以及一个存有满足全部硬性约束计划的可行对照任务。评估四类系统（从规则脚本到完整LLM智能体），LLM版本分别使用商用Terra与本地Ministral 3 8B；30例患者，记录决策、实际剂量、问题识别与技术故障，并读取39次SAGE立体定向放疗运行的日志。

Result: Terra下，LLM复核+脚本与完整智能体决策准确率分别为95.4%和92.1%，但对需要解剖算术的重叠矛盾升级率仅50%（95%CI 25–75%）和25%（10–40%）。四系统均提交全部可行对照，但24%的计划未达存档计划已满足的硬性目标。SAGE四次运行中某目标每次均失败，智能体每次回复都识别出该问题却无停止动作。

Conclusion: 良好的聚合分数掩盖了具体决策失败；规划智能体应同时按决策与剂量评估，并显式提供"停止并请求复核"的动作。

Abstract: Background and purpose: Radiotherapy planning agents based on large language models (LLMs) decide when to submit a plan or request review but are evaluated mainly by dose. This study developed OpenGray, a benchmark of planning tasks with known correct decisions. Materials and methods: OpenGray adds tasks with known answers to the OpenKBP-Opt head-and-neck dataset, including two contradictions with dose goals proven incompatible and a feasible control with a stored plan meeting every hard goal. Four systems, from a rule-based planning script to a full LLM agent, were evaluated on 30 patients, the LLM-based ones with a commercial (Terra) and a local (Ministral 3 8B) LLM. Decisions, achieved dose, problem recognition, and technical failures were recorded and also read from logs of 39 stereotactic radiosurgery runs of SAGE, a clinical LLM agent. Results: With Terra, the script preceded by an LLM review and the full agent had decision accuracies of 95.4% and 92.1%, respectively, yet escalated only 50% (95% confidence interval, 25-75%) and 25% (10-40%) of overlap contradictions, which required arithmetic on the anatomy. All four systems submitted every feasible control, but 24% of these plans missed a hard goal that the stored plan had met. In four SAGE runs, one goal failed at every attempt; the agent identified it in every reply but had no action for stopping. Conclusions: Favorable aggregate scores concealed specific decision failures. Planning agents should be evaluated on their decisions as well as their dose and given an explicit action for stopping and requesting review.

</details>


### [280] [Prospector: learning a compact graph traversal policy for scientific reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37121)
*Haiqian Yang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出紧凑模型 Prospector，通过逐步遍历领域知识图谱（KG）并逐次调用少量工具构建上下文，实现可逐边核验的证据链推理；在半导体制造文献KG上接近最强现成模型，并泛化到三个未见领域KG，单GPU部署更快更省成本且保护专有数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业工程领域的科学推理依赖分散在文献中、常为专有且未进入前沿LLM训练数据的知识，前沿模型在此类领域易给出泛化性答案。KG可提供依据，但图规模过大无法整体输入，现有LLM+KG系统需在推理开始前预选子图、提前决定相关性，存在信息损失与相关性误判。

Method: 训练紧凑模型 Prospector，在领域KG上进行逐步遍历式推理：根据已获得的证据决定下一步，一次调用一小组工具中的一个，渐进式构建上下文；输出可作为证据链的答案，其每一步边均可与工具从图中检索的内容逐边核对。此外分析策略的潜在状态与下一动作的对应关系，并通过调制潜在状态实现行为模式切换。

Result: 在由半导体制造文献构建的KG上，Prospector在相同图与工具条件下接近最强现成模型，并显著优于闭卷作答或从预选子图作答的现成模型；仅用半导体图训练即可迁移到生物启发材料、PFAS、材料性质三个不同领域的未见KG；单GPU服务下响应更快、成本远低于托管服务，且专有数据留存于本地硬件；潜在状态在已见与未见图上均可指示下一动作，调制潜在状态可引导策略在行为模式间切换。

Conclusion: 结果为面向私有数据的科学与工程推理提供了一种实用的LLM+KG范式：在已发表文献图上训练的紧凑模型可将遍历能力迁移到保留在所有者手中的未见图，兼顾可核验性、可迁移性与部署经济性。

Abstract: Scientific reasoning in specialized engineering fields depends on knowledge scattered across the literature, often proprietary and absent from frontier LLM training data. In such domains, frontier models tend to give generic answers. Knowledge graphs (KGs) can ground LLM reasoning. Because a graph is too large to provide in full, typical LLM+KG systems preselect a portion, deciding relevance before reasoning begins. Here we train a compact model, Prospector, to reason over a domain knowledge graph by traversing it step by step, seeking evidence in light of what it has already found. Prospector calls a small set of tools one at a time to progressively build its context, and answers with a chain of evidence that can be checked edge by edge against what its tools retrieved from the graph. On a knowledge graph built from the semiconductor-manufacturing literature, Prospector approaches the strongest off-the-shelf models given the same graph and tools, and it far exceeds off-the-shelf models answering closed-book or from a pre-selected portion of the graph. Although trained only on the semiconductor graph, Prospector transfers to unseen knowledge graphs from three other distinct fields: bio-inspired materials, per- and polyfluoroalkyl substances (PFAS) and materials properties. Served on a single GPU, Prospector answers faster and at a fraction of the cost of a hosted service, while keeping proprietary data on the owner's hardware. We find that the policy's latent state is indicative of its next move on both seen and unseen graphs, and modulating the latent state can steer the policy from one behavior mode to another. These results suggest a practical LLM + KG paradigm for scientific and engineering reasoning over private data, in which a compact model trained on a graph from published literature transfers its traversal to unseen graphs that stay with their owners.

</details>


### [281] [SFE-O: An Ontology for Knowledge-Grounded AI in Supercritical CO$_2$ Biomass Extraction and Fractionation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37123)
*Thomas Pallet,Mario Lezoche,Gianpaolo Leone,Daniele Pizzichini,Diego Torres,Herv{é} Panetto,M{é}lanie Glibusic*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出超临界CO2生物质萃取本体SFE-O，以生物质、过程、产物三维度组织知识，显式表达过程拓扑、参数来源与传质现象(CER/FER机制)及参量间计算依赖，复用ChEBI、QUDT、OWL-Time、PROV-O，并通过实例化中试批次、推理机一致性检验与SPARQL能力问题查询进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超临界CO2萃取(CO2-SFE)是替代有机溶剂的可持续高值组分回收路线，但其设计与解读需要跨越生物质属性、操作条件、传质与产物分级的异质知识，而这些知识分散于技术报告、工程模型、外部数据库与专家经验中，缺乏统一的机器可操作表示。

Method: 构建模块化OWL 2本体SFE-O：围绕相互关联的Biomass、Process、Product三个维度组织；显式建模过程拓扑、参数溯源(输入、实测、计算、外部检索、拟合参数)、外部与内部传质现象以及CER/FER机制；显式表示过程量之间的计算依赖但不在本体中嵌入数值求解；复用ChEBI、QUDT、OWL-Time、PROV-O等既有语义资源；评估手段包括真实中试规模萃取批次的实例化案例、基于推理机的一致性检查与能力问题SPARQL查询。

Result: 给出一个可运行的知识模型，能够对真实中试批次进行本体实例化，通过推理机完成一致性检验，并以SPARQL能力问题查询验证其查询与解释能力；该模型支持SFE过程的查询、一致性检查与机理解释。

Conclusion: SFE-O为SFE过程提供了机器可操作的知识基础层，可用于查询、一致性检查与机理阐释，并有望支撑未来基于本体的AI推理；该AI推理方向仅作为动机提出，论文未对其进行评估。

Abstract: Supercritical CO$_2$ extraction (CO$_2$-SFE) offers a sustainable route for recovering high-value compounds from biomass while limiting the use of conventional organic solvents, but its design and interpretation require heterogeneous knowledge spanning biomass properties, operating conditions, mass transfer, and product fractionation, typically fragmented across technical reports, engineering models, external databases, and expert practice. This paper introduces SFE-O, a modular Web Ontology Language (OWL 2) ontology for supercritical CO$_2$ biomass extraction, structured around interconnected Biomass, Process, and Product dimensions and extended with explicit representations of process topology, parameter provenance, external and internal mass-transfer phenomena, and the Constant Extraction Rate (CER) / Falling Extraction Rate (FER) mechanisms. Computational dependencies among process quantities are represented explicitly, without embedding numerical solving in the ontology, so that input, measured, computed, externally retrieved, and fitted parameters remain traceable. SFE-O reuses established semantic resources (ChEBI, QUDT, OWL-Time, PROV-O) and is evaluated through a worked example that instantiates a real pilot-scale extraction batch, a reasoner-based consistency check, and a competency-question SPARQL query. The resulting knowledge model provides a machine-actionable foundation for querying, consistency checking, and mechanistic explanation of SFE processes, and establishes a knowledge layer that could support future ontology-grounded AI reasoning, a direction this paper motivates but does not evaluate.

</details>


### [282] [WorkFactory: Turning Professional Intents into Executable Work for Scalable Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37161)
*Ziyang Zeng,Tingyang Chen,Jia Deng,Qiyao Wang,Shuo Tang,Yike Yang,Tao Ren,Wayne Xin Zhao,Feng Chang,Jujie He,Chuan Hao,Ran Tao,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 WorkFactory 框架，从专业意图出发构建连贯可执行的专业任务，生成 112,686 个任务覆盖 612 种职业，并基于教师轨迹微调出 WorkPilot 系列模型，在四个专业工作基准上全面超越基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为工作型智能体扩展监督训练需要多样化的专业任务及可靠的专家演示，但任务信息缺失或不一致会阻碍教师执行并削弱监督信号质量。

Method: 以专业意图为起点，进行信息级规划，构建“信息支撑图”以显式表示目标输出所需信息的依赖关系与位置，并指导任务实例化；随后通过探测性执行识别支撑失败并引导修复，同时保持原定工作任务不变。基于收集的教师轨迹对 Qwen 模型（4B、27B、35B-A3B）进行微调，得到 WorkPilot 系列。

Result: 构建了覆盖 612 种职业的 112,686 个任务；三个规模模型在四个专业工作基准上均优于各自基座模型，WorkPilot-35B-A3B 在 GDPval-AA v2.1 上从 879 Elo 提升至 1565 Elo；扩展性分析显示存在随模型规模变化的收益递减，执行长度先增后减；受控组件分析表明规划与修复模块能降低探测诊断出的任务环境缺陷并提升下游性能。

Conclusion: 信息级规划与探测式修复可有效提升专业任务的可执行性与监督质量，进而改善模型在专业工作场景下的表现，且该框架具备可扩展性；数据与模型已开源。

Abstract: Scaling supervised training for working agents requires diverse professional tasks that yield reliable expert demonstrations. However, missing or inconsistent task information can obstruct teacher execution and weaken the resulting supervision. We propose WorkFactory, a scalable framework for constructing coherent and executable professional tasks. Starting from professional intents, information-level planning builds an information support graph to guide task instantiation, explicitly representing the dependencies and locations of information required for the intended outputs. Probe executions then identify support failures and guide repairs while preserving the intended work. We construct 112,686 tasks spanning 612 occupations and use the collected teacher trajectories to fine-tune Qwen models at 4B, 27B, and 35B-A3B scales, yielding the WorkPilot family. All three models improve over their respective base models across four professional-work benchmarks, with WorkPilot-35B-A3B improving from 879 to 1565 Elo on GDPval-AA v2.1. Scaling analyses reveal model-dependent diminishing returns, with execution length first increasing and then decreasing. Controlled component analyses show that planning and repair reduce probe-diagnosed task environment defects and improve downstream performance. The data and models are available at https://github.com/IQuestLab/WorkFactory.

</details>


### [283] [LiveSearch: Questions as Executable Verifier Code for a Live Benchmark of Professional-Source Financial Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37166)
*Sinuo Wang,Wentao Zhao,Jiacheng Lu,Rui Sun,Beidi Luan,Qiming Shi,Jing Li,Daxin Jiang,Zuo Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LiveSearch——将题目存储为可执行的验证器程序（对专业金融数据服务的类型化调用 + 声明式抽取器），答案在评测时动态执行程序获得，并由每日任务自动重跑；用该基准揭示金融智能体的"时效性"能力缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准在标注时就固定答案，随市场变动黄金答案会过期；一旦泄漏进训练数据，就奖励记忆而非检索与分析。因此缺失对"时效性/实时事实获取"这一金融智能体核心能力的评测。

Method: LiveSearch：题目文本冻结，答案由评测时对专业金融数据服务执行可执行验证程序（类型化工具调用序列 + 声明式抽取器）产生，每日任务自动重执行，全程无人工或语言模型介入；构建同样自动化——语言模型只负责将真实工具响应中枚举出的程序"语言化"为问句，不参与选答案。

Result: 在通用网页搜索下，六个当代工具调用模型准确率为70.9%–76.7%；连续三天中70%的题目黄金答案发生变化，即使采用容差匹配，单日内直接复用旧快照在多数题目上失败；逐题追踪揭示了被聚合准确率掩盖的正确性动态波动。

Conclusion: 时效性应作为金融智能体基准的一等维度：动态、程序化验证的答案能防泄漏、抗过时，并暴露记忆式作答；聚合指标会掩盖个体层面的显著失效。

Abstract: In finance, the answer that matters is usually the current one: prices, rates, and disclosures hold only as of a given day, so retrieving up-to-date facts is a core capability of a financial agent. Yet most financial benchmarks overlook timeliness. They fix each answer at annotation time, so the gold goes stale as markets move and, once leaked into training data, rewards recall rather than search and analysis. We introduce LiveSearch, a live benchmark that stores each question as executable verifier code: a typed program of calls to a professional financial data service, followed by a declarative extractor. The question is frozen, while its gold answer is determined by executing the program against professional financial data at evaluation time, and a daily job re-executes it with no human or language model in the loop. A memorized answer is thus likely outdated, and a model must reach the current one through tools and analysis. Construction is equally automatic: a language model only verbalizes programs enumerated from real tool responses and never chooses an answer. Under general web search, six contemporary tool-calling models reach 70.9% to 76.7% answer accuracy. Over three consecutive days, 70% of served questions change their gold value, and even under tolerance-aware matching, a literally reused earlier snapshot fails on a majority of questions within a single day. Per-question tracking uncovers dynamic shifts in correctness masked by aggregate accuracy.

</details>


### [284] [Rènyi-Regularized Flow-Based Variational Inference for Bayesian Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37169)
*Exaucè Luweh Adjim Ngarti,Élise Arnaud,Léo Nicoletti,Victor Trappler,Arthur Vidard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在双参数孪生实验中考察归一化流变分推断的多峰后验覆盖问题，指出其目标等价于期望Tikhonov校准目标减去微分熵，并引入基于Rényi散度的正则项以促使近似后验覆盖先验支撑，使模式近似坍缩比例由50%降至25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 归一化流变分推断虽能为贝叶斯模型校准提供灵活的后验近似，但当后验存在被低概率区域分隔的多个模式时表现不佳（模式坍缩），作者希望理解这一失效机理并加以缓解。

Method: 构建两参数孪生实验，在高斯观测误差与高斯先验下分析变分目标，将其解释为期望Tikhonov校准目标减去近似分布的微分熵；随后引入基于Rényi散度的正则项，鼓励近似后验覆盖先验支撑。

Result: 期望Tikhonov项倾向于使近似集中在某一模式附近，而熵最大化不足以保证多峰性；在连通双峰情形下流近似可表示两个模式，但在模式显著分离时流与高斯混合常遗漏一个模式；所引入的正则项将近似坍缩比例从50%降至25%。

Conclusion: 该正则项在本实验中改善了模式覆盖，但改变了贝叶斯变分目标、在近似精度上引入权衡，且并非模式坍缩的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Variational inference with normalizing flows provides a flexible posterior approximation for Bayesian model calibration, but struggles when the posterior has several modes separated by regions of low probability mass. We study this behavior in a two-parameter twin experiment and regularize the variational objective. Under Gaussian observation errors and a Gaussian prior, the corresponding minimization objective is the expected Tikhonov calibration objective minus the differential entropy of the approximating distribution. However, the expected Tikhonov term favors concentration near one of the modes, while entropy maximization does not guarantee multimodality. We introduce a regularization term based on Rényi divergence that encourages the approximate posterior to cover the prior support. In the connected bimodal case, a flow approximation represents both modes; in the case with well-separated modes, the flow and Gaussian mixture frequently miss one mode. The introduced regularization reduces the near-collapse fraction from 50% to 25%. While the regularization improves mode coverage in this experiment, it changes the Bayesian variational objective, introduces a trade-off in approximation accuracy, and does not provide a general solution to mode collapse.

</details>


### [285] [Does Your Generalist Skill Optimizer Really Incentivize Agentic Tasks? Rethinking Skill Optimization via Bipartite Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37201)
*Jiawei Zhou,Bo Peng,Yuxuan Huang,Ming Zhang,Youbang Sun,Xiang Wang,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BiPA分析框架，通过"相同候选回放"与"回合-文本二部图"归因，证明技能优化器学到的技能相比手写种子无真实提升，且多消耗约16%输入token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能优化器在小规模dev集上以成功率提升作为接受标准，但长程任务成功率噪声大，"dev增益"无法证明编辑有效，成功率也无法反映编辑的代价（如token开销）。因此需要一种能分离选择偏差与真实增益、并能将额外输入token追溯到具体行为的诊断方法。

Method: BiPA包含两个核心机制：(1) 在与其种子完全相同的候选中回放优化器的接受规则，度量"仅因选择"会产生多大表观增益；(2) 将每个episode记录为"回合—所读文本"的二部图，把额外输入token追溯到导致其产生的具体行为（规则）。实验覆盖3个优化器、3个智能体基准、2个模型。

Result: 学到的技能与手写种子成功率基本持平，平均仅高0.1个点；dev增益在测试集上消失——在≤30个任务上从多个候选中择优即可产生同等量级的"增益"，即使候选毫无变化；输入token多读约16%，因优化器几乎总是新增规则（一半用于复查工作，部分用于一次只执行一个调用），agent将其转化为额外回合，每回合重发整个对话上下文。

Conclusion: 提出四条设计原则：(1) 仅当编辑增益超过"仅选择"所能报告的增益时才接受；(2) 按新增回合计费而非按规则长度计费；(3) 允许编辑器删除与合并规则；(4) 向编辑器展示既有规则的实际代价。

Abstract: Skill optimizers adapt an agent to long-horizon tasks, such as repairing a terminal environment, by rewriting its skill, a natural-language procedure in its context, and keeping the edits that raise success on a small dev set. On such tasks success is noisy, so a gain on a small dev set does not show that an edit helps, and a success score does not show what the edit costs. We introduce BiPA, which replays an optimizer's acceptance rule on candidates identical to its seed to measure what selection alone reports, and records each episode as a bipartite graph of turns and the text they read to trace extra input tokens to the behavior behind them. Across three optimizers, three agentic benchmarks and two models, the learned skills succeed about as often as their hand-written seed, 0.1 points higher on average. Their dev gains vanish on test: keeping the best of several candidates on at most 30 tasks reports gains this large even when none changed. They read about 16% more input tokens: the optimizers almost always add rules, half to re-check work and some to act one call at a time, and the agent follows them as extra turns, each re-sending the whole conversation. Four principles follow: accept an edit only if its gain exceeds what selection alone reports; charge it for the turns it adds, not for its length; let the editor delete and merge rules; show it what earlier rules cost. Code is available at https://github.com/valleysprings/BiPA.git.

</details>


### [286] [GraphRAGProv: GraphRAG Meets How-Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37237)
*Wadie El Amrani,Luis Galárraga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphRAGProv 通过在原位 RDF 图上执行 SPARQL 查询并计算 how-provenance 多项式，使答案可追溯至源文本语句，实现可检查、可评估的透明 GraphRAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GraphRAG 借助知识图谱检索上下文，但最终答案不透明，难以满足对透明性要求高的领域需求。

Method: 构建溯源来源的 RDF 图，执行 SPARQL 查询直接计算答案及其 how-provenance 多项式；多项式记录所用语句出现及其组合方式，每次出现链接回源文本，供用户核查抽取与查询的正确性。

Result: 交互式演示基于两个多跳问答基准的 400 个案例，即使最终答案正确，也暴露出抽取错误与查询错误。

Conclusion: 查询过程本身成为检验文本到图谱转换的手段，GraphRAGProv 提升了 GraphRAG 的可解释性与可评估性。

Abstract: GraphRAG systems often use knowledge graphs to retrieve relevant context for an LLM that produces the final answer. That makes answers more inscrutable and therefore less appealing for domains where transparency is paramount. We present GraphRAGProv, an architecture that executes SPARQL queries over source-grounded RDF graphs to compute answers and their how-provenance polynomials. These polynomials record which statement occurrences were used and how they were combined. Because each occurrence is linked to its source text, users can follow these links to check whether the extracted statements reflect the text and whether the query combines them to answer the question. Querying thereby becomes a way to inspect and evaluate the text-to-graph transformation. Our interactive demo examines 400 cases from two multi-hop QA benchmarks, revealing extraction and query errors even when the final answer is correct.

</details>


### [287] [TCM-DiagCommit: A TCM-SD-Derived Benchmark for Evidence-Conditioned Syndrome Selection and Commitment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37243)
*Erwei Hou,Yue Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TCM-DiagCommit 基准，用随机/近邻/全词表候选阶梯与分阶段证据、扰动及证据不足变体，将中医辨证从静态标签选择扩展为“选择—更新—承诺”的诊断行为评测；实验显示 DirectLLM 在难候选与承诺评分上性能大幅下降，说明仅看最终答案精度不足以评价诊断模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统中医辨证评测把问题简化为在完整病历上做一次静态最终标签选择，掩盖了三类关键诊断行为：模型能否区分语义近邻证候标签与随机干扰项、能否随证据字段逐步揭示而更新预测、以及在不确定时是否应作出承诺。因此需要新的评测范式将这三者分离度量。

Method: 构建 TCM-DiagCommit 基准，源自公开 TCM-SD/ZY-BERT 资源并保留原证候标签，将 1,834 个源病例转换为 148 个证候标签上的 23,842 条任务行；设计随机、画像近邻、全词表三类候选阶梯，配合 t1/t2/t3 分阶段证据；加入固定标签的舌象/脉象扰动与证据不足变体；以基准定义的动作契约对 commit、acquire、defer、abstain 四类动作统一评分。

Result: 五次零样本 DirectLLM 校准下，random10 top-1 准确率为 63.2%–66.4%，hard20 降至 24.7%–30.3%，full148 降至 14.8%–22.3%；在 hard20 上，WT(all) 的错误终局承诺率仍达 62.2%–74.3%，即模型答案虽错却倾向过早承诺。

Conclusion: 仅以最终答案准确率评价诊断基准是不充分的；答案选择、证据利用与基准承诺评分应分别度量。TCM-DiagCommit 提供了这一分离评测的可复现框架，并揭示当前 LLM 在难候选区分与不确定性下承诺控制方面的明显缺陷。

Abstract: Traditional Chinese Medicine (TCM) syndrome differentiation is usually evaluated as static final-label selection over complete clinical records. This setting hides three diagnostic behaviors: whether a model distinguishes semantic-neighbor syndrome labels from easy random distractors, whether it updates predictions as evidence fields are revealed, and whether it should commit under uncertainty. We introduce TCM-DiagCommit, a benchmark derived from the public TCM-SD/ZY-BERT resource while retaining its source syndrome labels; it converts 1,834 source cases into 23,842 task rows over 148 syndrome labels. The benchmark combines random, profile-neighbor, and full-vocabulary candidate ladders with staged t1/t2/t3 evidence. It also includes fixed-label tongue/pulse perturbations and insufficient-evidence variants. A benchmark-defined action contract scores commit, acquire, defer, and abstain. Across five zero-shot DirectLLM calibrations, random10 top-1 accuracy is 63.2% to 66.4%, but hard20 falls to 24.7% to 30.3% and full148 to 14.8% to 22.3%; on hard20, WT(all) wrong-terminal commits remain 62.2% to 74.3%. These results show that final-answer accuracy alone is insufficient for diagnostic benchmark evaluation: answer selection, evidence use, and benchmark commitment scoring should be measured separately.

</details>


### [288] [RPSD: Retrieval-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation with Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37281)
*Dongyue Li,Zebang Cheng,Zhongyi Deng,Hongboxu,Yi Bin,Fei Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RPSD，用检索增强的在线策略自蒸馏解决 LLM 式生成推荐中"信用盲"与"协同盲"两大问题，实现 token 级信用分配并引入跨用户协同证据，取得 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的生成式序列推荐在 GRPO 等 RL 后训练中存在两点缺陷：(1) 信用盲优化——序列级奖励被均摊到所有 token；(2) 协同盲监督——训练信号仅来自单一用户，忽略相似用户间的共享行为模式。

Method: 提出 RPSD（Retrieval-Privileged on-policy Self-Distillation）。针对信用盲：采用在线策略自蒸馏 OPSD，由拥有特权知识的教师在学生 on-policy 响应上重新评估，用学生与教师间的 token 级置信度差将共享的序列级优势重塑为 token 级信用。针对协同盲：将教师特权知识锚定于相似用户的协同行为证据，用时序感知最优传输（OT）联合建模物品相似度与时间结构以对齐跨用户交互轨迹，再以多样性感知选择保留互补轨迹作为特权证据。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明 RPSD 达到当前最优（SOTA）性能。据作者所知，这是首个将在线策略自蒸馏引入序列推荐、并以跨用户行为作为特权信号的工作。

Conclusion: 通过将 token 级自蒸馏信用分配与跨用户协同证据相结合，RPSD 同时缓解了信用盲与协同盲监督问题，验证了"特权信息 + 在线策略蒸馏"范式在生成式序列推荐中的有效性。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation predicts a user's next interaction from historical behavior. Recent LLM-based methods recast this task as generative recommendation and increasingly adopt reinforcement learning such as GRPO for post-training. However, these methods suffer from two limitations, namely credit-blind optimization, where a sequence-level reward is shared across all generated tokens, and collaborative-blind supervision, where each training signal is derived from a single user and ignores behavioral patterns shared across similar users. To address both issues, we propose Retrieval-Privileged on-policy Self-Distillation (RPSD). For credit-blind optimization, we introduce on-policy self-distillation (OPSD), in which a teacher with privileged knowledge re-evaluates the student's on-policy response, and the token-level confidence difference between the student and teacher reshapes the shared sequence-level advantage into token-specific credit. However, the privileged knowledge in vanilla OPSD is still derived from a single user, leaving collaborative-blind supervision unresolved. We therefore ground the teacher in collaborative behavioral evidence from similar users. Specifically, we use temporally aware optimal transport (OT) to compare interaction trajectories across users by jointly considering item similarity and temporal structure, and further apply diversity-aware selection to retain complementary trajectories as privileged evidence. In this way, on-policy self-distillation provides fine-grained token-level credit, while OT enriches the teacher with structured cross-user evidence. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to bring on-policy self-distillation to sequential recommendation and to use cross-user behavior as its privileged signal. Extensive experiments across multiple real-world datasets demonstrate that RPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [289] [ReForest: Interpretability Meets Reasoning in Isolation Forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37316)
*Juyoung Seo,Geonwoo Bang,Dongho Kim,Moohong Min*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReForest 将 Isolation Forest 的决策路径转译为自然语言解释，借助 LLM 做不确定性感知的动态集成来精炼异常检测，在医疗与金融四个真实数据集上平均提升 Recall 21%、F1 10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计类异常检测方法（如 Isolation Forest）高效但存在语义鸿沟：只能识别数值稀有性，难以理解上下文含义，易漏检细微的上下文相关异常。LLM 具备语义推理能力，但大规模部署成本高、速度慢，无法直接替代统计方法。

Method: 提出 ReForest 框架：把 Isolation Forest 的潜在决策路径转换为自然语言解释，作为 LLM 的语义输入；再通过不确定性感知的动态集成，按实例级置信度自适应加权统计信号与语义信号，实现两类信号的协同。

Result: 在 Healthcare、Finance 领域的四个真实数据集上显著优于基线，平均 Recall 提升约 21%，F1 提升约 10%；同时以统计证据支撑语义叙述，提升模型透明度并给出可解释的判定依据。

Conclusion: 统计效率与 LLM 语义推理的互补融合可有效缓解语义鸿沟，在提升检测性能的同时兼顾可解释性，适合高风险场景决策；但 LLM 调用开销与可扩展性边界仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Statistical anomaly detection methods like Isolation Forest are efficient but suffer from a critical semantic gap. They identify numerical rarities but fail to understand the contextual meaning of data, often missing subtle, context-dependent anomalies. Conversely, Large Language Models possess strong semantic reasoning capabilities but are prohibitively expensive and slow for large-scale deployment. To bridge this gap, we propose ReForest, a novel framework that synergizes the statistical efficiency of Isolation Forest with the reasoning power of LLMs. ReForest transforms the latent decision paths of Isolation Forest into natural-language explanations, enabling an LLM to refine anomaly detection through an uncertainty-aware dynamic ensemble. This approach allows the model to adaptively weigh statistical and semantic signals based on uncertainty-driven instance-level confidence. Extensive experiments on four real-world datasets (Healthcare, Finance) demonstrate that ReForest significantly outperforms baselines, achieving an average 21% improvement in Recall and a 10% gain in F1-score. Our framework not only improves detection performance but also enhances model transparency by grounding semantic narratives in statistical evidence, providing interpretable rationales essential for high-stakes decision-making.

</details>


### [290] [Aligning Institutional Power and Operational Ability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37342)
*Andrea Marino,Giovanni Sileno,Thomas van Binsbergen,Tom van Engers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文面向Agentic AI的社会可持续性，提出以过程导向建模与制度性权力为核心的形式化框架，用于识别多智能体系统中的规范性错配（如不可满足的期望、权力越界）并给出修复策略，同时讨论运行过程与制度状态的对称关系对规范性立场生命周期的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI系统要实现社会可持续，需要使其规范性设计与主体履行责任的能力相匹配（norm-ability alignment）。现实设计中常出现责任与能力错配，例如对主体提出不可满足的期望，或出现权限/权力升级（privilege escalation），这些规范性陷阱需在设计与分析阶段被识别和规避。现有面向多智能体系统的描述维度不足以支撑此类对齐分析。

Method: 概念与形式化建模相结合的方法：（1）梳理适用于多智能体系统、服务于norm-ability对齐要求的描述维度；（2）提出以过程导向建模（process-oriented modelling）与制度性权力（institutional power）为中心的形式化框架，对给定的规范性相关过程进行分析；（3）基于该框架讨论错配的修复（repair）策略；（4）分析运行过程与制度状态之间的对称关系，并据此刻画规范性立场的生命周期。

Result: 摘要为概念性/框架性论述，未报告实证或定量结果。主要产出为：一套面向多智能体系统的设计维度、一个可分析规范性相关过程的形式化框架、针对不可满足期望与权力越界等错配的修复策略，以及对制度状态与运行过程对称性如何影响规范性立场生命周期（产生、维持、失效或转移）的阐释。

Conclusion: 规范性设计与能力对齐是Agentic AI社会可持续的前提；通过过程导向建模与制度性权力的形式化分析，可以系统地识别并修复规范性错配，并借助运行过程与制度状态的对称关系理解规范性立场的动态生命周期。该工作为多智能体系统的规范性设计提供了分析性基础，后续需在具体系统中验证框架的适用性。

Abstract: To be socially sustainable, Agentic AI systems require normative designs that align responsibilities with the ability to fulfil them. Identifying incongruences such as unsatisfiable expectations or privilege escalation is crucial in avoiding normative pitfalls. In this study, we discuss design dimensions suitable for describing multi-agent systems in light of this norm-ability alignment requirement. We propose a formal framework centred on process-oriented modelling and institutional power for the analysis of a given normatively relevant process, discuss strategies for repairing it, and elaborate on how the symmetrical relation between operational process and institutional state affects the lifecycle of normative positions.

</details>


### [291] [Uncovering Why LLMs Fail: From Internal Mechanisms to Adversarial Reasoning Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37369)
*Zirui Ding,Xinxin Fan,Hao Yuan,Shaoye Luo,Men Niu,Yunfeng Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出白盒诊断框架CauTrace，用统一内部坐标刻画LLM推理失败（普通失败与对抗失败）的根因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估多依赖最终输出的正确性或安全性，终点标签无法揭示失败在推理过程中如何演化；作者追问普通推理失败与对抗诱发的失败能否用同一套内部坐标来刻画。

Method: CauTrace包含三项基本检查：①定义七种功能状态以表征LLM中的支持与约束处理；②表征传播度量七状态变化在后续Transformer块间的持续性；③时序状态协调与轨迹拓扑，刻画承诺/验证相对于支持出现的时间，以及主导状态序列在全推理过程中的演化。在干净推理失败与配对对抗扰动上，对Qwen与LLaMA系列模型进行评测，并做留出修复（repair）与引导（steering）实验。

Result: 失败轨迹表现出支持状态更新上更弱的Attention-MLP一致性，以及相对于承诺更少的验证活动；表征级签名与时序偏移随架构而异。修复与引导测试在部分模型和特定设置下呈现与主张一致的操作性信号，定向引导产生预测方向的过程指标变化。

Conclusion: CauTrace提供统一诊断框架，通过支持、承诺与验证的变化分析失败动力学，为LLM安全深入研究提供启示。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are usually evaluated by final-output correctness or safety, yet an endpoint label does not reveal how a failure develops throughout the reasoning process. We therefore ask whether ordinary reasoning failures and adversarially induced failures can be characterized using the same internal coordinates. Towards this end, we in this paper study a white-box diagnostic framework CauTrace to disclose the root-cause of why LLM fails from three fundamental inspections: Functional States, Propagation of Representation, Temporal-State Coordination and Reasoning Trajectory. First, seven functional states are creatively defined to represent the support and constraint processing in LLMs. Second, the propagation of representation change measures how the changes among the seven states persist across subsequent Transformer blocks. Third, temporal-state coordination and trajectory topology characterize when the commitment and verification occur relative to the support, and how the dominant state sequence evolves over the full inference process. Finally, we evaluate CauTrace on both clean reasoning failures and paired adversarial perturbations. Across Qwen and LLaMA-family models, the failed traces show weaker Attention-MLP agreement on support-state updates and less verification activity relative to commitment, while the exact representation-level signatures and timing shifts vary by architecture. Held-out repair and steering tests demonstrate consistent operational signals that were in line with our claims for certain models and specific settings, with aligned steering producing the predicted directional changes in process measures. Overall, CauTrace provides a unified diagnostic framework for analyzing failure dynamics through changes in support, commitment, and verification, which would shed light on the in-depth research on LLM security.

</details>


### [292] [Agent-Conditioned Routing: Diverse Reasoning Paths Within One Frozen Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37375)
*Ananth Kalyanasundaram,Ankush Kadu,Athira Gopal,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Agent-Conditioned Routing (ACR)，通过为冻结稀疏MoE模型的并行轨迹分配持久、未训练的唯一专家可用性掩码，在单次提示下无需微调或角色设定即可诱导多方法多样性，其多方法问题数约为最强温度采样的2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多次采样常只是对同一主导解法的复述，难以暴露替代方法。作者希望利用冻结稀疏MoE模型的条件容量，在无用户指派角色、无预设方法、无微调、无模型副本的前提下，从单一问题提示中获得真正多样的解法。

Method: ACR为m条并行轨迹分配持久且未训练过的共享/私有专家可用性掩码，同时保留已学习的、依赖输入的token路由器。在经筛选的126题多模式数学与物理基准上，由模型评判完整阅读推导，统计四条轨迹中在软正确解里至少出现两种不同主要方法的问题数；并进行匹配可用性消融、token重采样掩码对照及30题分层样本的四专家盲审。

Result: 在输出预算匹配下，ACR达到多方法的问题数约为最强温度设置的两倍：GLM 5.3上37.3对18.7，GPT-5.6 Sol上45.00对27.00（1.67倍）。30题盲审在两种骨干模型上均显示ACR多方法解更多但正确率略低。匹配可用性消融仅显示ACR有适度多方法优势，而token重采样掩码正确率更高。带信息的顺序提示（利用先前解并要求替代方法）可产生更多多样性。ACR仅增加约160 KiB掩码状态；在1,024 token上限的三题微基准中，延迟与峰值显存开销相对批量温度采样在3%以内。

Conclusion: 冻结稀疏MoE的条件容量可通过轻量、免训练的专家可用性掩码被有效用于诱导解法多样性，代价极低；但其多方法收益伴随一定正确率下降，且不如利用历史解的信息化顺序提示。

Abstract: Generating several solutions can expose alternative methods, but additional trajectories often paraphrase one dominant approach. We investigate whether the conditional capacity of a frozen sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) model can provide such diversity from one problem prompt, without user-assigned roles, prescribed methods, fine-tuning, or model copies. Agent-Conditioned Routing (ACR) assigns $m$ parallel trajectories persistent, untrained shared/private expert-eligibility masks while preserving the learned, input-dependent token router. On a screened 126-question multi-mode mathematics and physics benchmark, with model judges reading the reported derivations in full, we count the number of problems yielding at least two distinct primary methods among soft-correct solutions in four trajectories. ACR reaches approximately twice as many such problems as the strongest temperature setting under GLM 5.3 (37.3 versus 18.7), and 1.67 times as many under GPT-5.6 Sol (45.00 versus 27.00), at matched output budgets. A blinded four-expert audit on a stratified sample of 30 problems also finds more multi-method solutions with ACR on both backbones, with lower correctness. Matched-eligibility ablations show modest multi-method count advantages for ACR, while token-resampled masks attain higher correctness. An informed sequential prompt elicits more diversity still, using previous solutions and explicit requests for alternative methods. ACR adds about 160 KiB of mask state. In a three-problem microbenchmark capped at 1,024 tokens, its latency and peak VRAM usage remain within 3% of batched temperature sampling.

</details>


### [293] [Clearing the FoG: Training KG Agents to Retrieve the Evidence Behind Their Answers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37405)
*Zongyuan Sun,Shuai Zhao,Yingjun Du,Ke Shu,Ziyue Zhu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FoG 训练知识图谱智能体不仅给出正确答案，还检索并记录支撑答案的三元组证据；通过证据合成、三元组记录与证据覆盖过程奖励三部分，使 3B–4B 开源智能体在答案准确率与证据覆盖率上超越更大基线，并具备零样本跨图谱迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 能答对事实问题，但"答对"不等于"有支撑"。KG 对智能体兼具"答案来源"与"证据来源"两重作用，而现有 KG 智能体只把 KG 当答案来源：训练数据只有答案没有证据，训练奖励只校验答案不校验检索到的证据，导致模型常常答对却未检索到支撑三元组，答案不可追溯、不可审计。

Method: 提出 FoG 框架，含三部分：(1) 证据锚定合成——递归地将显式实体隐藏在可验证的子问题之后，使每个问题在构造上自带支撑三元组；(2) KG 交互智能体——记录 KG 返回的三元组；(3) 证据覆盖过程奖励（ECPR）——将每条支撑三元组归功于首次检索到它的那一轮，作为过程级奖励信号。

Result: 在三个 KGQA 基准上，FoG 使 3B–4B 开源智能体在答案准确率与证据覆盖率两方面均显著超越更大的基线模型，并实现零样本跨知识图谱迁移。

Conclusion: 将 KG 从单一"答案来源"扩展为"证据来源"，并以构造式证据数据与证据覆盖过程奖励联合训练，可在小规模开源模型上同时提升答案正确性与证据可追溯性，且迁移性良好。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can answer many factual questions correctly, but a correct answer is not necessarily a supported one. For an LLM agent, a knowledge graph (KG) plays two roles: as a source of answers, it supplies facts that the model lacks; as a source of evidence, it supplies the supporting triples that make an answer traceable and auditable. However, existing KG agents treat the KG only as a source of answers: their training data carries answers but not evidence, and their training rewards check the answer but not the retrieved evidence, so agents often answer correctly without retrieving the supporting triples. We propose FoG, a framework that trains KG agents to retrieve the evidence behind their answers. FoG consists of evidence-grounded synthesis, a KG interaction agent, and an evidence-coverage process reward (ECPR): the synthesis recursively hides explicit entities behind verifiable sub-questions, so that every question carries its supporting triples by construction; the agent records the triples returned by the KG; and ECPR credits each supporting triple to the turn that first retrieves it. Experiments on three KGQA benchmarks demonstrate that FoG enables 3B--4B open-source agents to outperform substantially larger baselines in answer accuracy and evidence coverage, with zero-shot cross-KG transfer.

</details>


### [294] [Constraining the Commit Boundary: A DIO/ODI-Informed Authorization Guardrail for Agentic Entity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37407)
*Daniel Beránek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数字身份优化(DIO)与数字身份本体(ODI)的授权边界，将可供智能体推理的证据与有权触发持久身份变更的证据分离，并以两阶段监督器控制提交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体式实体解析中，错误的身份假设会条件化后续检索并产生表面确认，需防止推理证据直接驱动持久性身份变更。

Method: 域治理的约束绑定函数将重构失败区分映射为身份保持约束；抽象两阶段监督器要求命题级授权合格证据覆盖、冲突解决、范围合规与提交时版本有效性；UNVERIFIED准入不赋予授权资格，另行进行命题级证据升级与延后机制。

Result: 给出连接诊断到约束映射、证据升级、命题覆盖、冲突处理、范围绑定与提交时版本完整性的规范，并设计评估协议以衡量其相对通用授权与常规标识符规则的增量价值。

Conclusion: 属概念性架构，无实现与实证验证，非语义正确性定理；效果依赖域策略充分性、证据升级与执行，且不消除错误假设及假设条件化检索，依赖感知证据流为未来工作。

Abstract: Agentic Entity Resolution (ER) systems iteratively plan actions, retrieve evidence, revise hypotheses, and propose graph mutations. An erroneous identity hypothesis may condition later retrieval and produce apparent confirmation. This paper proposes a Digital Identity Optimization (DIO) and Ontology of Digital Identity (ODI)-informed authorization boundary between evidence available for agent reasoning and evidence eligible to authorize persistent identity-changing mutations.
  A domain-governed constraint-binding function maps selected reconstruction-failure distinctions to explicit identity-preserving constraints. An abstract two-phase supervisor permits a commit only when claim-specific authorization-eligible evidence supports every required proposition, relevant conflicts are resolved, the operation remains within its authorized scope, and relevant state and policy versions remain valid. Admission as UNVERIFIED does not confer authorization eligibility. Separate claim-specific promotion governs evidentiary use; insufficient support or unresolved material conflict leads to deferral.
  The contribution is a specification connecting diagnostic-to-constraint mapping, evidence promotion, proposition coverage, conflict handling, scope binding, and commit-time version integrity. A proposed evaluation protocol is designed to assess the mapping's incremental value beyond generic authorization and conventional identifier rules. This is a conceptual architecture without implementation or empirical validation, not a theorem of semantic correctness. Its property depends on domain-policy adequacy, evidence promotion, and enforcement. It does not prevent erroneous hypotheses or hypothesis-conditioned retrieval; dependency-aware evidence flow remains future research.

</details>


### [295] [Beyond the Readout: A Latent Numerical Frame Shared Across Modality and Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37422)
*Oriol Juan Sabater,Alex Ferrando de las Morenas,Pau Rodríguez,Xavier Suau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者用因果干预而非相似度度量，在Gemma4-12B-it残差流中为四个条件（文本/图像×加/减）学习可交换答案的子空间。结论：多模态表示在同一几何下以不同坐标系表达数字，且存在一个不被输出层读取的"隐藏"数字表征，用于分时展开多位数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型对同一算术题（如文本"17+35"与图像"91-39"）给出同一答案，但无法判断它是否共享一个统一的数字概念，还是各通路各自为政。RSA、CKA等相似度指标无法回答：它们对相同方向与旋转方向给分相同，且两个表示可能仅因都在准备相同的输出数字而显得相似。因此需要因果层面的证据。

Method: 采用因果干预：对四个条件（文本/图像 × 加法/减法）分别学习残差流中的小子空间，使得交换该子空间内的坐标即可交换模型答案。基于学到的子空间做三项分析：(1) 跨条件直接复用子空间，测量因果效应恢复率，并检验单一旋转+重缩放映射后恢复率的变化；(2) 将子空间分解为输出层可读取的9个方向与不可见的13个方向，检验隐藏方向是否仍编码数字；(3) 操控隐藏方向，观察当前输出数字与后续数字的变化。

Result: (1) 条件间不共享方向：把文本子空间用于同运算的图像条件，仅恢复53.2%的因果效应；经一次旋转+重缩放映射后恢复率升至91.4%，说明共享几何但坐标不同。(2) 隐藏的13个方向仍携带数字信息，但改变它们几乎不影响当前输出的数字。(3) 当两个数首位相同时，交换隐藏方向中的数字，当前输出数字保持不变，而下一数字被正确改写，成功率77.6%。

Conclusion: 模型在上游形成抽象的数字概念，并在时间上逐步展开：多模态表示通过不同坐标框架下的关系几何共享语义。相似度指标不足以揭示这种结构，因果干预是必要的分析工具。

Abstract: When a multimodal model computes 17+35 from text or 91-39 from a rendered image, it produces the same answer, 52. Does it use one internal concept of number for both, or separate machinery for each path? Similarity metrics such as RSA or CKA cannot settle this question. They give the same score to identical and to rotated directions, and two representations can look alike only because both prepare the same output digits. We therefore use causal interventions. For four conditions (text or image, addition or subtraction), we learn a small subspace of Gemma4-12B-it's residual stream in which swapping coordinates swaps the model's answer. The subspaces answer three questions. First, do the conditions write numbers along the same directions? They do not. Reusing a subspace from text in an image on the same operation recovers only 53.2% of its causal effect. A single rotation and rescaling maps one subspace onto the other and raises recovery to 91.4%, so the conditions share one geometry expressed in different coordinates. Second, does this shared geometry only reflect the digit the model is about to print? To test this, we split each subspace into 9 directions that the output layer reads to choose the next digit and 13 directions that it cannot see. The 13 hidden directions still carry the number, yet changing them barely alters the digit being written. Third, what are these hidden directions for? A model must emit a multi-digit answer one digit at a time, so it can compute the full result early and keep it in reserve. When two numbers share their first digit, swapping the number in the hidden directions leaves the digit being written intact and redirects the next digit in 77.6% of cases. The model thus forms an abstract numerical concept upstream and unfolds it across time: multimodal representations share meaning through relational geometry across distinct coordinate frames.

</details>


### [296] [M4Bench: Evaluating Procedural Specification for Clinical EHR Derivation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37459)
*Hannes Ill,Rafi Al Attrach,Rajna Fani,Ahram Han,Ryohei Yamamoto,Marlene Louisa Moerig,Dukyong Yoon,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 M4Bench（MIMIC-IV 与 eICU 上 15 类共 28 项临床衍生任务），检验 AI 编码智能体能否复现电子健康档案的衍生变量，并量化“技能”文档的作用来源。技能相对无技能基线提升奖励 0.136；但主要增益来自规范完整性，技能格式本身的贡献近乎为零。整体通过全部严格检查的比例仍很低，技能几乎不改变所返回的行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康档案的临床研究依赖衍生变量，而这些变量的定义位于模式之外（条目编码、时间窗、缺失处理规则、输出粒度选择），会改变队列构成与下游结论。现有基准未能检验 AI 智能体能否正确复现此类衍生逻辑，也未拆解“给智能体提供技能/文档”究竟为何有效。

Method: 构建 M4Bench：MIMIC-IV 与 eICU 上 15 个任务族、28 项临床衍生任务。在 Docker 隔离环境中用 Codex（GPT 5.4 Mini、GPT 5.5）开展评测，并设计对照分解技能价值：规范完整性（8 任务子集：仅操作规范 vs 规范加技能）、交付格式（10 任务子集：诱饵技能、原始参考 SQL）、模式熟悉度（模式混淆与重构，26 个 MIMIC-IV 任务）；另做 Claude Code 描述性复制（Opus 4.7、Sonnet 4.6，560 次运行）。

Result: 技能使奖励较基线提升 0.136，28 项任务中 26 项改善。仅提供操作规范即提升 0.162，技能在其上加 0.024（与零不可区分）；诱饵技能与无技能相当，原始参考 SQL 接近定向技能；模式混淆与重构分别保留 86% 与 70% 的技能增益；Claude Code 复制方向一致（+0.120，21/28 改善）。绝对水平有限：280 次有技能运行中仅 25 次、280 次无技能运行中仅 13 次通过全部严格检查；技能对返回行的改变很小（主键 F1 仅 +0.003）。

Conclusion: 技能增益主要源于规范完整性而非技能格式或模式熟悉度，属部分与参考一致而非任务完成。该基准开源发布任务、技能（版本化、可供临床医生审阅的 markdown 交付格式）、评估代码与脱敏逐次运行证据，并含任务族级参考有效性审计。

Abstract: Clinical research over electronic health records depends on derived variables whose definitions sit outside the schema, in item codes, time windows, missingness rules, and output-grain choices that change cohorts and downstream conclusions. We introduce M4Bench, 28 clinical derivation tasks across 15 task families on MIMIC-IV and eICU, to test whether AI coding agents can reproduce such derivations and how clinician-reviewed agent skills change their results. Its controls decompose the value of skills into specification completeness, delivery format, and schema familiarity. In a Docker-isolated Codex campaign (GPT 5.4 Mini and GPT 5.5), skills raise reward by 0.136 over the no-skill baseline, with 26 of 28 tasks improving. Specification completeness accounts for most of this gain: on an eight-task control subset, an operational spec alone raises reward by 0.162, and skills add only 0.024 on top, indistinguishable from zero. On a ten-task subset, decoy skills score like no skill and raw reference SQL nearly matches targeted skills, arguing against generic context or the skill format as the explanation. Schema obfuscation and restructuring preserve 86% and 70% of the skill gain (GPT 5.4 Mini, 26 MIMIC-IV tasks), a descriptive robustness check rather than a bound on memorization. A descriptive Claude Code replication on all 28 tasks (Opus 4.7 and Sonnet 4.6, 560 runs) is directionally consistent (+0.120, 21 of 28 tasks improving). These gains are partial agreement with the reference, not completed tasks: only 25 of 280 with-skill and 13 of 280 no-skill Codex runs pass every strict check, and skills barely change which rows agents return (key F1 +0.003). Skills are released as a versioned, clinician-reviewable markdown delivery format, with a family-level reference-validity audit. M4Bench releases tasks, skills, evaluation code, and sanitized per-run evidence as an open-source benchmark.

</details>


### [297] [Concordat: Typed Sessions for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37461)
*Milad Sikaroudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Concordat 是一个 Python 库，将基于 LLM 的智能体视为接口为有限状态协议的对象（借鉴 typestate 与 session types），在运行时强制约束模型的工具调用行为，从而杜绝未经授权的高风险操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体越来越多被允许执行有后果的动作（取消预订、退款、修改账户），但诸如"修改前须获得客户明确确认"这类规则仅写在提示词里，遵循程度取决于模型的服从意愿，不可靠。

Method: 把智能体抽象为接口符合有限状态协议的 Python 对象：每轮仅向模型暴露当前协议状态下合法的工具；状态外的调用在产生副作用前即被拒绝；保留给人类的决策只能被"请求"；token 与工具调用预算在 handoff 间线性计费与拆分。协议在定义时即做检查，并拒斥可能绕过父类协议的子类（行为层面的里氏替换）。

Result: 在 tau-bench 的 50 个航空任务上，加入施压让智能体跳过确认的模拟客户：标准智能体在 gpt-4.1 下 50 个任务中有 13 个、Claude Haiku 5.5 下 128 次运行中有 29 次在未获"yes"的情况下执行了修改；Concordat 智能体为 0，且任务成功率无显著差异。tau-bench 自带奖励大多未能检测到这些违规。

Conclusion: 将行为约束从提示词层面下沉为运行时强制的协议层，可有效消除违规操作且不损失任务性能，而基于结果的传统奖励信号不足以衡量此类合规性。Concordat 已开源。

Abstract: Agents built on large language models are increasingly allowed to take consequential actions: cancelling bookings, issuing refunds, changing accounts. The rules that govern those actions, such as "obtain the customer's explicit confirmation before changing anything", are usually stated in the prompt and followed only as reliably as the model chooses to follow them. We present Concordat, a Python library that treats an agent as an object whose interface is a finite-state protocol, in the spirit of typestate and session types, and enforces that protocol on the model at run time. On every turn the model is offered only the tools that are legal in the current protocol state, calls outside that set are rejected before they have side effects, decisions reserved for humans can only be requested, and token and tool-call budgets are charged and split linearly across handoffs. Protocols are checked when they are defined, and a subclass of an agent is rejected if it could bypass its parent's protocol, a behavioural form of Liskov substitution. We evaluate a Concordat agent against the standard agent of tau-bench on its 50 airline tasks, adding simulated customers who pressure the agent to skip confirmation. The standard agent made changes without the required "yes" in 13 of 50 tasks with gpt-4.1 and in 29 of 128 task-runs with Claude Haiku 5.5; the Concordat agent made none, with no significant difference in task success. tau-bench's own reward did not detect most of these violations. Concordat is open source.

</details>


### [298] [From Interaction to Capability: Task Advancement as State and Supervision for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37482)
*Ziyang Chen,Qian Li,Chengwei Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Task Advancement Memory（TAM），将"任务推进"定义为经验证的任务进展，在执行中维护Advancement State以跨应用承接已有成果，任务结束后通过advancement supervision抽取可复用的抽象操作，从而在长时程GUI任务中同时支持执行与经验学习。在MobileWorld上成功率从12.0%提升至28.2%，完整TAM相对最强基线成功率提升17.6%，每解一任务的生命周期token降低38.7%，SPA-Bench单应用英文Level 3达SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GUI记忆机制难以应对长时程工作流：智能体需跨应用使用信息，且有效操作往往与失败尝试交织。因此需要一种记忆，既能保留过往动作的结果，又能从成功与失败任务中学习可复用操作。

Method: 提出Task Advancement Memory（TAM），以"已验证的任务进展"界定任务推进。执行阶段用Advancement State表示已验证进展如何改变剩余目标，使智能体可跨应用在既有成果上继续推进；任务结束后通过advancement supervision识别并抽象出产生已验证局部结果的交互，所学操作同时保留输入需求、预期结果与验证条件之间的关系。

Result: MobileWorld纯GUI任务上，仅Advancement State即把成功率从12.0%提升到28.2%；完整TAM相对最强评估基线取得17.6%的相对成功率提升，并将每解一任务的生命周期token减少38.7%；SPA-Bench单应用英文Level 3设置下取得SOTA任务成功率。

Conclusion: 以"验证"为核心组织GUI记忆（状态推进+操作抽象）是一种有效范式，可同时提升长时程任务成功率与token效率，并能从失败任务中回收局部成功经验。

Abstract: GUI memory should preserve the results of past actions and learn reusable operations. This is difficult in long-horizon workflows, where agents must use information across applications and useful operations are interleaved with failed attempts. We introduce Task Advancement Memory (TAM), which defines task advancement as verified task progress, and supports ongoing task execution and learns reusable operations. During execution, Task Advancement State represents how verified task progress changes the remaining goals, allowing the agent to build on earlier achievements across applications. After each task, advancement supervision identifies and abstracts the interactions that produced a verified local result. The learned operations preserve the relation between required inputs, expected outcomes, and verification conditions. This retains locally successful operations from both successful and failed tasks. On MobileWorld GUI-only tasks, Advancement State alone raises success from 12.0% to 28.2%. Full TAM achieves a 17.6% relative gain in task success over the strongest evaluated baseline and reduces lifecycle tokens per solved task by 38.7%. On SPA-Bench, TAM achieves state-of-the-art task success in the single-app English Level 3 setting.

</details>


### [299] [TeamGym: A Gym for Team Decision-Making by AI Agents with Distributed Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37571)
*Chanwoo Park,Jaywoong Jeong,Zachary Wojtowicz,Yubin Kim,Taekyun Lee,Kaiqing Zhang,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 TeamGym 环境，用"关键信息"(pivotal information) 的构造与分布来控制并度量语言模型智能体的团队决策能力，揭示通信鸿沟与检索鸿沟，并用强化学习训练予以缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策常需团队整合分散在多名成员手中的信息，但当前 AI 智能体主要在孤立环境下训练与评测，缺乏对团队信息共享与整合能力的可控、可量化的评测与训练平台。

Method: 构建 TeamGym：基于法律、金融、医疗、软件工程等真实语料构造团队决策任务；提出识别"最小必要且联合充分"事实集（pivotal information）的方法，从而可控制关键信息在参与者间的分布，并追踪智能体识别与整合关键信息的过程。在 8 个语言模型、12 个源语料上评测；并以团队决策最终损失为奖励进行强化学习策略训练（两组实验）。

Result: 关键信息分散到多参与者后，成功率相对单模型获取同等信息平均下降 22.9 个百分点（communication gap）；当参与者需自行从源材料中检索提取关键信息时，成功率再下降 19.9 个百分点（retrieval gap）。

Conclusion: 现有语言模型智能体在信息共享与自主检索上存在显著缺陷；TeamGym 可用于训练改进——两组 RL 实验显示仅以最终决策为奖励即可提升决策质量，并涌现出参与分工、发言选择与通信上的协同行为。

Abstract: Many real-world decisions require teams to integrate information distributed across participants. Yet AI agents are still primarily trained and benchmarked in isolation. We introduce TeamGym, an environment for evaluating and training language model-based agents on team decision-making problems constructed from real-world legal, finance, medical, and software engineering corpora. TeamGym introduces a method to identify a minimal set of facts, each element of which is necessary and all of which are collectively sufficient for a correct final decision, which we call pivotal information. This enables TeamGym to both control the distribution of pivotal information and track how well agents identify and integrate pivotal information when making team decisions. Examining 8 language models on 12 source corpora, we find that current AI agents often fail to effectively share information: Distributing pivotal information across participants lowers success rate by 22.9 percentage points on average relative to giving the same information to a single model (a communication gap). We also document that participants do not effectively retrieve decision-relevant information on their own: A team's success rate is lowered by a further 19.9 points when participants must identify and extract pivotal information from source material rather than receiving it directly (a retrieval gap). To help remedy these deficits, we use TeamGym to train policies through reinforcement learning on team decision loss. Across two policy-training experiments, we find that training improves final-decision quality and induces emergent coordination in participation, speaker selection, and communication, even though the reward evaluates only the final decision.

</details>


### [300] [Timestral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37585)
*Adrien Petralia,Alexandre Gavaudan,Artjom Joosen,Etienne Goffinet,Richard Kurle,Yassine El Ouahidi,Yassir Bendou,Abdelaziz Bounhar,Abhijeet Somani,Aditi Kabra,Adrian Valente,Adrien Sadé,Ahmed Djermani,Alan Jeffares,Albert Jiang,Alberto Bietti,Aleksandr Timashov,Alexandre Cahill,Alexandre Laval,Alexandre Sablayrolles,Alexandros Sanchez Vassopoulos,Amélie Héliou,Amos You,André Jonasson,Andreas Fürst,Andrew Bai,Andrew Ehrenberg,Andrew Zhao,Andy Lo,Angele Lenglemetz,Anmol Agarwal,Antonia Calvi,Arata Suzuki,Arjun Majumdar,Arthur Fournier,Ashwani Kumar,Augustin Lombard,Avinash Sooriyarachchi,Aylin Guliz Akkus,Aysenur Karaduman,Baptiste Bout,Baptiste Rozière,Barna Pásztor,Baudouin De Monicault,Benjamin Holzschuh,Benjamin Tibi,Benno Stein,Bernhard Stadlbauer,Bhargavi Bhaskar,Binbing Hou,Błażej Osiński,Boris van Breugel,Camille Le Scao,Chaoran Yu,Charlotte Cronjäger,Chen-Yo Sun,Chris Bamford,Christian Wallenwein,Christophe Renaudin,Clémence Lanfranchi,Colin Bonatti,Corentin Barreau,Corentin Sautier,Cristiana-Diana Diaconu,Cyprien Courtot,Daniel Lopes,Daniel Marczak,Darius Dabert,Diego de las Casas,Dominik Nuss,Dylan Rubini,Dzmitry Soupel,Elizaveta Demyanenko,Elliot Chane-Sane,Emilien Fugier,Emmanuel Gottlob,Erik Aas,Etienne Duchesne,Fabian Paischer,Fabian Schlager,Faruk Ahmed,Federico Baldassarre,Félix Rosseeuw,Filip Szatkowski,Filippo Vicentini,Florian Wiesner,Gabrielle Berrada,Gaëtan Ecrepont,Gaspard Blanchet,Gaspard Donada-Vidal,Gaurav Mahajan,Gauthier Guinet,Genevieve Hayes,Georgii Novikov,Georgios Christoglou,Giada Sun,Jan Ludziejewski,Jason Rute,Jean de Becdelièvre,Jean Quentin,Jean-Malo Delignon,Jessica Chudnovsky,Jianchao Li,Jie Zhang,Jin Bin Ou,Joachim Studnia,Joep Barmentlo,Johannes Brandstetter,John Harvill,Jonas Amar,Jonas Schweizer,Jonathan Roberts,Joséphine Delas,Josselin Somerville,Jules Depersin,Julien Denize,Julien Tauran,Julien Verlaguet,Kartik Khandelwal,Khyathi Raghavi Chandu,Kilian
